```
     4→ * Returns true when MediaLightbox is open in non-modal mode.
```

tool_result Read
```
1→import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
     2→
     3→type Listener = () => void;
     4→
     5→let openCount = 0;
     6→const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
     7→
     8→function notifyListeners(): void {
     9→  listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
    10→}
    11→
    12→function getSnapshot(): boolean {
    13→  return openCount > 0;
    14→}
    15→
    16→function subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
    17→  listeners.add(listener);
    18→  return () => {
    19→    listeners.delete(listener);
    20→  };
    21→}
    22→
    23→export function acquireLightboxOpenState(): () => void {
    24→  openCount += 1;
    25→  notifyListeners();
    26→
    27→  let released = false;
    28→  return () => {
    29→    if (released) {
    30→      return;
    31→    }
    32→    released = true;
    33→    openCount = Math.max(0, openCount - 1);
    34→    notifyListeners();
    35→  };
    36→}
    37→
    38→export function useLightboxOpenState(): boolean {
    39→  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getSnapshot, getSnapshot);
    40→}
    41→
    42→export function __resetLightboxOpenStateForTests(): void {
    43→  openCount = 0;
    44→  notifyListeners();
    45→}
    46→
```

```
1→import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
     2→import { renderHook, act } from '@testing-library/react';
     3→import { useLightboxOpen } from '../useLightboxOpen';
     4→import {
     5→  __resetLightboxOpenStateForTests,
     6→  acquireLightboxOpenState,
     7→} from '../../state/lightboxOpenState';
     8→
     9→describe('useLightboxOpen', () => {
    10→  afterEach(() => {
    11→    __resetLightboxOpenStateForTests();
    12→  });
    13→
    14→  it('returns false by default', () => {
    15→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    16→    expect(result.current).toBe(false);
    17→  });
    18→
    19→  it('returns true when state is already acquired before mount', () => {
    20→    const release = acquireLightboxOpenState();
    21→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    22→    expect(result.current).toBe(true);
    23→    act(() => {
    24→      release();
    25→    });
    26→  });
    27→
    28→  it('reacts when a lightbox opens', () => {
    29→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    30→    expect(result.current).toBe(false);
    31→
    32→    let release: () => void = () => {};
    33→    act(() => {
    34→      release = acquireLightboxOpenState();
    35→    });
    36→
    37→    expect(result.current).toBe(true);
    38→    act(() => {
    39→      release();
    40→    });
    41→  });
    42→
    43→  it('supports nested open handles and closes only when all are released', () => {
    44→    const { result } = renderHook(() => useLightboxOpen());
    45→    expect(result.current).toBe(false);
    46→
    47→    let releaseFirst: () => void = () => {};
    48→    let releaseSecond: () => void = () => {};
    49→
    50→    act(() => {
    51→      releaseFirst = acquireLightboxOpenState();
    52→      releaseSecond = acquireLightboxOpenState();
    53→    });
    54→    expect(result.current).toBe(true);
    55→
    56→    act(() => {
    57→      releaseFirst();
    58→    });
    59→    expect(result.current).toBe(true);
    60→
    61→    act(() => {
    62→      releaseSecond();
    63→    });
    64→    expect(result.current).toBe(false);
    65→  });
    66→});
    67→
```

```
1→import { usePanes } from '@/shared/contexts/PanesContext';
     2→import { useLightboxOpen } from '@/features/lightbox/hooks/useLightboxOpen';
     3→
     4→/**
     5→ * Calculates side-pane handle offset from the generations pane state.
     6→ */
     7→export const useBottomOffset = (): number => {
     8→  const {
     9→    isGenerationsPaneLocked,
    10→    isGenerationsPaneOpen,
    11→    generationsPaneHeight,
    12→  } = usePanes();
    13→  const isLightboxOpen = useLightboxOpen();
    14→
    15→  if (isLightboxOpen) return 0;
    16→
    17→  return (isGenerationsPaneLocked || isGenerationsPaneOpen)
    18→    ? generationsPaneHeight
    19→    : 0;
    20→};
    21→
```

## Summary: useLightboxOpen and useLightboxOpenState

### 1. **useLightboxOpen Definition**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/features/lightbox/hooks/useLightboxOpen.ts`

A simple wrapper hook that returns a boolean:
```typescript
export const useLightboxOpen = (): boolean => {
  return useLightboxOpenState();
};
```
- It's a thin passthrough with just a docstring explaining "Returns true when MediaLightbox is open in non-modal mode"
- No transformation, memoization, or additional logic

### 2. **useLightboxOpenState Definition**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/features/lightbox/state/lightboxOpenState.ts`

The actual state management (46 lines):
```typescript
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';

type Listener = () => void;

let openCount = 0;
const listeners = new Set<Listener>();

function notifyListeners(): void {
  listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
}

function getSnapshot(): boolean {
  return openCount > 0;
}

function subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function acquireLightboxOpenState(): () => void {
  openCount += 1;
  notifyListeners();
  let released = false;
  return () => {
    if (released) return;
    released = true;
    openCount = Math.max(0, openCount - 1);
    notifyListeners();
  };
}

export function useLightboxOpenState(): boolean {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getSnapshot, getSnapshot);
}

export function __resetLightboxOpenStateForTests(): void {
  openCount = 0;
  notifyListeners();
}
```

Uses `useSyncExternalStore` to track a ref-counted `openCount` with an explicit `acquireLightboxOpenState()` / `releaseLightboxOpen()` pattern.

### 3. **All Consumers of useLightboxOpen**

Only **2 actual consumers**:

1. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/features/layout/hooks/useBottomOffset.ts`**
   ```typescript
   import { useLightboxOpen } from '@/features/lightbox/hooks/useLightboxOpen';
   
   export const useBottomOffset = (): number => {
     const isLightboxOpen = useLightboxOpen();
     if (isLightboxOpen) return 0;
     return (isGenerationsPaneLocked || isGenerationsPaneOpen)
       ? generationsPaneHeight
       : 0;
   };
   ```

2. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/features/lightbox/hooks/__tests__/useLightboxOpen.test.ts`** (test file)
   - Imports `useLightboxOpen` and tests it with `acquireLightboxOpenState`

### 4. **Direct Consumers of acquireLightboxOpenState**

**2 files import directly from lightboxOpenState:**

1. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useLightboxViewportLock.ts`**
   ```typescript
   import { acquireLightboxOpenState } from '@/features/lightbox/state/lightboxOpenState';
   
   export function useLightboxViewportLock({ isActuallyModal }: UseLightboxViewportLockArgs): void {
     useEffect(() => {
       if (isActuallyModal) return;
       const releaseLightboxOpen = acquireLightboxOpenState();
       const unlockViewport = getViewportLockRuntime().lockLightboxViewport();
       return () => {
         unlockViewport();
         releaseLightboxOpen();
       };
     }, [isActuallyModal]);
   }
   ```
   - This is the primary producer of the lightbox open state

2. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useLightboxViewportLock.test.ts`** (test file)

### Key Architecture Points
- **State model:** Ref-counted singleton with acquire/release semantics (supports nested opens)
- **Consumer of state:** `useBottomOffset` (layout feature) observes whether lightbox is open
- **Producer of state:** `useLightboxViewportLock` acquires it when lightbox mounts in non-modal mode
- **Test reset:** `__resetLightboxOpenStateForTests()` for test isolation

```
1→import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import {
     3→  isMobileUA as isMobileUaSignal,
     4→  useIsMobile as useIsMobileSignal,
     5→  useIsTablet as useIsTabletSignal,
     6→  useIsTouchDevice as useIsTouchDeviceSignal,
     7→} from './deviceSignals';
     8→import { useDeviceInfo as useDeviceInfoSignal } from './responsiveViewModel';
     9→
    10→export const isMobileUA = isMobileUaSignal;
    11→
    12→/** App-facing mobile signal with a defensive fallback for non-browser runtimes. */
    13→export function useIsMobile() {
    14→  const detected = useIsMobileSignal();
    15→  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    16→    return false;
    17→  }
    18→  return detected ?? window.innerWidth < 768;
    19→}
    20→
    21→/** App-facing tablet signal with a defensive fallback for non-browser runtimes. */
    22→export function useIsTablet() {
    23→  const detected = useIsTabletSignal();
    24→  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    25→    return false;
    26→  }
    27→  return detected ?? (window.innerWidth >= 768 && window.innerWidth < 1024);
    28→}
    29→
    30→/** App-facing touch capability signal with runtime fallback for browser environments. */
    31→export function useIsTouchDevice() {
    32→  const detected = useIsTouchDeviceSignal();
    33→  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    34→    return false;
    35→  }
    36→  if (detected !== undefined) {
    37→    return detected;
    38→  }
    39→  return 'ontouchstart' in window || navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0;
    40→}
    41→
    42→/** Composite device view model with normalized base signals from this public module. */
    43→export function useDeviceInfo() {
    44→  const viewModel = useDeviceInfoSignal();
    45→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
    46→  const isTablet = useIsTablet();
    47→  const isTouchDevice = useIsTouchDevice();
    48→
    49→  return useMemo(() => ({
    50→    ...viewModel,
    51→    isTablet,
    52→    isPhone: isMobile && !isTablet,
    53→    isTouchDevice,
    54→  }), [viewModel, isMobile, isTablet, isTouchDevice]);
    55→}
    56→
```

```
    67→  variantId: string;
    68→  generationId: string | null;
    69→  projectId: string | null;
    70→  status: VariantProjectScopeStatus;
    71→  queryError?: string;
    72→}
    73→
    74→interface GenerationTaskRepositoryOptions {
    75→  projectId?: string;
    76→  supabaseRegistry?: SupabaseClientRegistry;
    77→}
    78→
    79→export function normalizeScopedGenerationInput(
    80→  input: ScopedGenerationInput,
    81→): { id: string; projectId?: string } {
    82→  return typeof input === 'string' ? { id: input } : input;
    83→}
    84→
    85→export async function resolveGenerationProjectScope(
    86→  generationId: string,
    87→  expectedProjectId?: string,
    88→  supabaseRegistry: SupabaseClientRegistry = supabaseClientRegistry,
    89→): Promise<GenerationProjectScopeResolution> {
    90→  const supabaseResult = supabaseRegistry.getClientResult();
    91→  if (!supabaseResult.ok) {
    92→    return {
    93→      generationId,
    94→      projectId: null,
    95→      status: 'query_failed',
    96→      queryError: supabaseResult.error.message,
    97→    };
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  const supabase = supabaseResult.client;
   101→  const { data, error } = await supabase
   102→    .from('generations')
   103→    .select('id, project_id')
   104→    .eq('id', generationId)
   105→    .maybeSingle();
   106→
   107→  if (error) {
   108→    if (isNoRowsPostgrestError(error)) {
   109→      return {
   110→        generationId,
   111→        projectId: null,
   112→        status: 'missing_generation',
   113→      };
   114→    }
   115→    return {
   116→      generationId,
   117→      projectId: null,
   118→      status: 'query_failed',
   119→      queryError: error.message,
   120→    };
   121→  }
   122→
   123→  if (!data) {
   124→    return {
   125→      generationId,
   126→      projectId: null,
   127→      status: 'missing_generation',
   128→    };
   129→  }
   130→
   131→  if (expectedProjectId && data.project_id !== expectedProjectId) {
   132→    return {
   133→      generationId,
   134→      projectId: data.project_id ?? null,
   135→      status: 'scope_mismatch',
   136→    };
   137→  }
   138→
   139→  if (!data.project_id) {
   140→    return {
   141→      generationId,
   142→      projectId: null,
   143→      status: 'missing_project_scope',
   144→    };
   145→  }
   146→
   147→  return {
   148→    generationId,
   149→    projectId: data.project_id,
   150→    status: 'ok',
   151→  };
   152→}
   153→
   154→export async function resolveVariantProjectScope(
   155→  variantId: string,
   156→  expectedProjectId?: string,
   157→  supabaseRegistry: SupabaseClientRegistry = supabaseClientRegistry,
   158→): Promise<VariantProjectScopeResolution> {
   159→  const supabaseResult = supabaseRegistry.getClientResult();
   160→  if (!supabaseResult.ok) {
   161→    return {
   162→      variantId,
   163→      generationId: null,
   164→      projectId: null,
   165→      status: 'query_failed',
   166→      queryError: supabaseResult.error.message,
   167→    };
   168→  }
   169→
   170→  const supabase = supabaseResult.client;
   171→  const { data, error } = await supabase
   172→    .from('generation_variants')
   173→    .select('id, generation_id, project_id')
   174→    .eq('id', variantId)
   175→    .maybeSingle();
   176→
   177→  if (error) {
   178→    if (isNoRowsPostgrestError(error)) {
   179→      return {
   180→        variantId,
   181→        generationId: null,
   182→        projectId: null,
   183→        status: 'missing_variant',
   184→      };
   185→    }
   186→    return {
   187→      variantId,
   188→      generationId: null,
   189→      projectId: null,
   190→      status: 'query_failed',
   191→      queryError: error.message,
   192→    };
   193→  }
   194→
   195→  if (!data) {
   196→    return {
   197→      variantId,
   198→      generationId: null,
   199→      projectId: null,
   200→      status: 'missing_variant',
   201→    };
   202→  }
   203→
   204→  if (!data.generation_id) {
   205→    return {
   206→      variantId,
   207→      generationId: null,
   208→      projectId: data.project_id ?? null,
   209→      status: 'missing_generation',
   210→    };
   211→  }
   212→
   213→  if (expectedProjectId && data.project_id && data.project_id !== expectedProjectId) {
   214→    return {
   215→      variantId,
   216→      generationId: data.generation_id,
   217→      projectId: data.project_id,
   218→      status: 'scope_mismatch',
   219→    };
   220→  }
   221→
   222→  const generationScope = await resolveGenerationProjectScope(
   223→    data.generation_id,
   224→    expectedProjectId,
   225→    supabaseRegistry,
   226→  );
   227→
   228→  if (generationScope.status !== 'ok') {
   229→    return {
   230→      variantId,
   231→      generationId: data.generation_id,
   232→      projectId: generationScope.projectId,
   233→      status: generationScope.status,
   234→      queryError: generationScope.queryError,
   235→    };
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  return {
   239→    variantId,
   240→    generationId: data.generation_id,
   241→    projectId: generationScope.projectId,
   242→    status: 'ok',
   243→  };
   244→}
   245→
   246→export function applyParentTaskFilter<T extends { is: (column: string, value: unknown) => T }>(query: T): T {
   247→  return applyRootTaskFilter(query);
   248→}
   249→
   250→export async function getPrimaryTaskIdForGeneration(
   251→  generationId: string,
   252→  options?: GenerationTaskRepositoryOptions,
   253→): Promise<GenerationTaskMapping> {
   254→  const mappings = await getPrimaryTaskMappingsForGenerations([generationId], options);
   255→  return mappings.get(generationId) ?? {
   256→    generationId,
   257→    taskId: null,
   258→    status: 'missing_generation',
   259→  };
   260→}
   261→
   262→export async function getPrimaryTaskMappingsForGenerations(
   263→  generationIds: string[],
   264→  options?: GenerationTaskRepositoryOptions,
   265→): Promise<Map<string, GenerationTaskMapping>> {
   266→  if (generationIds.length === 0) {
   267→    return new Map();
   268→  }
   269→
   270→  const requestedIds = Array.from(new Set(generationIds));
   271→  const supabaseResult = (options?.supabaseRegistry ?? supabaseClientRegistry).getClientResult();
   272→  if (!supabaseResult.ok) {
   273→    const failures = new Map<string, GenerationTaskMapping>();
   274→    requestedIds.forEach((generationId) => {
   275→      failures.set(generationId, {
   276→        generationId,
   277→        taskId: null,
   278→        status: 'query_failed',
   279→        queryError: supabaseResult.error.message,
   280→      });
   281→    });
   282→    return failures;
   283→  }
   284→
   285→  const supabase = supabaseResult.client;
   286→  const query = supabase
   287→    .from('generations')
   288→    .select('id, tasks, project_id')
   289→    .in('id', requestedIds);
   290→
   291→  const { data, error } = await query;
   292→
   293→  if (error) {
   294→    const failures = new Map<string, GenerationTaskMapping>();
   295→    requestedIds.forEach((generationId) => {
   296→      failures.set(generationId, {
   297→        generationId,
   298→        taskId: null,
   299→        status: 'query_failed',
   300→        queryError: error.message,
   301→      });
   302→    });
   303→    return failures;
   304→  }
   305→
   306→  const rowsById = new Map((data || []).map((row) => [row.id, row]));
   307→
   308→  const mappings = new Map<string, GenerationTaskMapping>();
   309→  for (const generationId of requestedIds) {
   310→    const row = rowsById.get(generationId);
   311→
   312→    if (!row) {
   313→      mappings.set(generationId, {
   314→        generationId,
   315→        taskId: null,
   316→        status: 'missing_generation',
   317→      });
   318→      continue;
   319→    }
   320→
   321→    if (options?.projectId && row.project_id !== options.projectId) {
   322→      mappings.set(generationId, {
   323→        generationId,
   324→        taskId: null,
   325→        status: 'scope_mismatch',
   326→      });
   327→      continue;
   328→    }
   329→
   330→    const parsed = parseGenerationTaskId(row.tasks);
   331→    mappings.set(generationId, {
   332→      generationId,
   333→      taskId: parsed.taskId,
   334→      status: parsed.status,
   335→    });
   336→  }
   337→
   338→  return mappings;
   339→}
   340→
   341→export function toGenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry(
   342→  mapping: GenerationTaskMapping | undefined,
   343→): GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry {
   344→  return {
   345→    taskId: mapping?.taskId ?? null,
   346→    status: mapping?.status ?? 'not_loaded',
   347→    ...(mapping?.queryError ? { queryError: mapping.queryError } : {}),
   348→  };
   349→}
   350→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * Tool Settings Service
     3→ *
     4→ * Pure async functions for fetching tool settings from Supabase.
     5→ * Extracted from useToolSettings.ts to reduce hook file complexity.
     6→ *
     7→ * Contains:
     8→ * - Auth caching logic (getCachedUserId)
     9→ * - Settings fetch with single-flight deduplication (fetchToolSettingsSupabase)
    10→ * - Module-level state for caching and deduplication
    11→ */
    12→
    13→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
    14→import { deepMerge } from '@/shared/lib/utils/deepEqual';
    15→import { isCancellationError, getErrorMessage } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
    16→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    17→import { readUserIdFromStorage } from '@/shared/lib/supabaseSession';
    18→import { isKnownSettingsId } from '@/shared/lib/settingsIds';
    19→import {
    20→  operationFailure,
    21→  operationSuccess,
    22→  type OperationFailure,
    23→  type OperationResult,
    24→} from '@/shared/lib/operationResult';
    25→import { toolDefaultsRegistry } from '@/tooling/toolDefaultsRegistry';
    26→
    27→// ============================================================================
    28→// Module-level state
    29→// ============================================================================
    30→
    31→// Single-flight dedupe for settings fetches across components
    32→const inflightSettingsFetches = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>();
    33→
    34→// Lightweight user cache to avoid repeated localStorage reads within a short window
    35→let cachedUser: { id: string } | null = null;
    36→let cachedUserAt: number = 0;
    37→const USER_CACHE_MS = 10_000; // 10 seconds
    38→
    39→const unknownSettingsIdsReported = new Set<string>();
    40→
    41→function reportUnknownSettingsId(toolId: string): void {
    42→  if (isKnownSettingsId(toolId) || unknownSettingsIdsReported.has(toolId)) {
    43→    return;
    44→  }
    45→  if (import.meta.env.MODE === 'test') {
    46→    return;
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  unknownSettingsIdsReported.add(toolId);
    50→  if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    51→    console.warn(
    52→      `[toolSettingsService] Unknown settings key "${toolId}". ` +
    53→      'Add this key to SETTINGS_IDS if it should be persisted via useToolSettings.',
    54→    );
    55→  }
    56→}
    57→
    58→/**
    59→ * Seed the user cache from an external source (e.g. AuthContext).
    60→ *
    61→ * Call this as early as possible — ideally in AuthContext right after getSession()
    62→ * resolves, before AuthGate opens. This lets getCachedUserId() return
    63→ * immediately from cache without acquiring any navigator.locks, avoiding stalls
    64→ * during token refresh.
    65→ */
    66→export function setCachedUserId(userId: string) {
    67→  cachedUser = { id: userId };
    68→  cachedUserAt = Date.now();
    69→}
    70→
    71→/** @internal Only for test isolation — do not call in production code. */
    72→export function _resetCachedUserForTesting() {
    73→  cachedUser = null;
    74→  cachedUserAt = 0;
    75→}
    76→
    77→// ============================================================================
    78→// Types
    79→// ============================================================================
    80→
    81→interface ToolSettingsContext {
    82→  projectId?: string;
    83→  shotId?: string;
    84→}
    85→
    86→export type ToolSettingsErrorCode =
    87→  | 'auth_required'
    88→  | 'cancelled'
    89→  | 'network'
    90→  | 'scope_fetch_failed'
    91→  | 'invalid_scope_identifier'
    92→  | 'unknown';
    93→
    94→interface ToolSettingsErrorOptions {
    95→  recoverable?: boolean;
    96→  cause?: unknown;
    97→  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
    98→}
    99→
   100→export class ToolSettingsError extends Error {
   101→  readonly code: ToolSettingsErrorCode;
   102→  readonly recoverable: boolean;
   103→  readonly metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
   104→  readonly cause?: unknown;
   105→
   106→  constructor(
   107→    code: ToolSettingsErrorCode,
   108→    message: string,
   109→    options: ToolSettingsErrorOptions = {},
   110→  ) {
   111→    super(message);
   112→    this.name = 'ToolSettingsError';
   113→    this.code = code;
   114→    this.recoverable = options.recoverable ?? false;
   115→    this.metadata = options.metadata;
   116→    this.cause = options.cause;
   117→  }
   118→}
   119→
   120→export function isToolSettingsError(error: unknown): error is ToolSettingsError {
   121→  return error instanceof ToolSettingsError;
   122→}
   123→
   124→export function classifyToolSettingsError(error: unknown): ToolSettingsError {
   125→  if (isToolSettingsError(error)) {
   126→    return error;
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  if (isCancellationError(error)) {
   130→    return new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   131→      recoverable: true,
   132→      cause: error,
   133→    });
   134→  }
   135→
   136→  const message = getErrorMessage(error);
   137→  if (message.includes('Authentication required')) {
   138→    return new ToolSettingsError('auth_required', message, {
   139→      recoverable: false,
   140→      cause: error,
   141→    });
   142→  }
   143→
   144→  if (
   145→    message.includes('Failed to fetch')
   146→    || message.includes('ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES')
   147→    || message.includes('Network connection issue')
   148→    || message.includes('Network exhaustion')
   149→  ) {
   150→    return new ToolSettingsError('network', message, {
   151→      recoverable: true,
   152→      cause: error,
   153→    });
   154→  }
   155→
   156→  return new ToolSettingsError('unknown', message, {
   157→    recoverable: false,
   158→    cause: error,
   159→  });
   160→}
   161→
   162→function toOperationFailure(error: ToolSettingsError): OperationFailure {
   163→  return operationFailure(error, {
   164→    policy: error.recoverable ? 'best_effort' : 'fail_closed',
   165→    recoverable: error.recoverable,
   166→    errorCode: error.code,
   167→    message: error.message,
   168→    cause: error.cause,
   169→  });
   170→}
   171→
   172→export function toToolSettingsErrorFromOperationFailure(failure: OperationFailure): ToolSettingsError {
   173→  const code = failure.errorCode as ToolSettingsErrorCode;
   174→  const normalizedCode: ToolSettingsErrorCode = (
   175→    code === 'auth_required'
   176→    || code === 'cancelled'
   177→    || code === 'network'
   178→    || code === 'scope_fetch_failed'
   179→    || code === 'invalid_scope_identifier'
   180→    || code === 'unknown'
   181→  ) ? code : 'unknown';
   182→
   183→  return new ToolSettingsError(normalizedCode, failure.message, {
   184→    recoverable: failure.recoverable,
   185→    cause: failure.cause ?? failure.error,
   186→  });
   187→}
   188→
   189→/** The wrapper format returned by fetchToolSettingsSupabase */
   190→export interface SettingsFetchResult<T = unknown> {
   191→  settings: T;
   192→  hasShotSettings: boolean;
   193→}
   194→
   195→// ============================================================================
   196→// Auth helpers
   197→// ============================================================================
   198→
   199→/**
   200→ * Get authenticated user ID without acquiring navigator.locks.
   201→ *
   202→ * Resolution order (all synchronous / lock-free):
   203→ *   1. In-memory cache (set by AuthContext via setCachedUserId)
   204→ *   2. localStorage session (same key Supabase uses; contains full user object)
   205→ *   3. null — user is genuinely signed out
   206→ *
   207→ * Previously this called getSession() / getUser() which both acquire a shared
   208→ * navigator.lock. During token refresh Supabase holds an EXCLUSIVE lock, so
   209→ * ALL shared-lock requests queue behind it — blocking for 600ms-16s. By reading
   210→ * the user ID from localStorage instead we avoid locks entirely. Token validity
   211→ * for actual data requests is handled by createSupabaseClient's cached token.
   212→ */
   213→export function getCachedUserId(): Promise<{ data: { user: { id: string } | null }; error: null }> {
   214→  // Check in-memory cache first
   215→  if (cachedUser && (Date.now() - cachedUserAt) < USER_CACHE_MS) {
   216→    return Promise.resolve({ data: { user: { id: cachedUser.id } }, error: null });
   217→  }
   218→
   219→  // Read from localStorage — synchronous, no navigator.locks, always fresh
   220→  const localUserId = readUserIdFromStorage();
   221→  if (localUserId) {
   222→    cachedUser = { id: localUserId };
   223→    cachedUserAt = Date.now();
   224→    return Promise.resolve({ data: { user: { id: localUserId } }, error: null });
   225→  }
   226→
   227→  // No session in storage — user is signed out
   228→  return Promise.resolve({ data: { user: null }, error: null });
   229→}
   230→
   231→// ============================================================================
   232→// Settings fetch helpers
   233→// ============================================================================
   234→
   235→type SettingsRow = { data: { settings: unknown } | null; error: unknown };
   236→
   237→function describeScopeError(error: unknown): string {
   238→  if (error instanceof Error && error.message) {
   239→    return error.message;
   240→  }
   241→  if (error && typeof error === 'object') {
   242→    const message = (error as { message?: unknown }).message;
   243→    if (typeof message === 'string' && message.length > 0) {
   244→      return message;
   245→    }
   246→  }
   247→  return 'unknown error';
   248→}
   249→
   250→function throwIfAborted(signal?: AbortSignal): void {
   251→  if (!signal?.aborted) {
   252→    return;
   253→  }
   254→
   255→  throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   256→    recoverable: true,
   257→  });
   258→}
   259→
   260→type AbortableQuery<T> = {
   261→  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
   262→};
   263→
   264→function maybeAttachAbortSignal<T>(query: T, signal?: AbortSignal): T {
   265→  if (!signal) {
   266→    return query;
   267→  }
   268→
   269→  const abortable = query as AbortableQuery<T>;
   270→  if (typeof abortable.abortSignal === 'function') {
   271→    return abortable.abortSignal(signal);
   272→  }
   273→
   274→  return query;
   275→}
   276→
   277→async function raceWithAbort<T>(promise: Promise<T>, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
   278→  if (!signal) {
   279→    return promise;
   280→  }
   281→
   282→  throwIfAborted(signal);
   283→
   284→  return await new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
   285→    const onAbort = () => {
   286→      cleanup();
   287→      reject(new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   288→        recoverable: true,
   289→      }));
   290→    };
   291→
   292→    const cleanup = () => {
   293→      signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
   294→    };
   295→
   296→    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
   297→    promise.then(
   298→      (value) => {
   299→        cleanup();
   300→        resolve(value);
   301→      },
   302→      (error) => {
   303→        cleanup();
   304→        reject(error);
   305→      },
   306→    );
   307→  });
   308→}
   309→
   310→/** Fetch settings from all three scopes (user, project, shot) in parallel. */
   311→function fetchAllScopes(
   312→  supabaseClient: ReturnType<typeof getSupabaseClient>,
   313→  userId: string,
   314→  ctx: ToolSettingsContext,
   315→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   316→): Promise<[SettingsRow, SettingsRow, SettingsRow]> {
   317→  const userQuery = maybeAttachAbortSignal(
   318→    supabaseClient
   319→      .from('users')
   320→      .select('settings')
   321→      .eq('id', userId)
   322→      .maybeSingle(),
   323→    signal,
   324→  );
   325→
   326→  const projectQuery = ctx.projectId
   327→    ? maybeAttachAbortSignal(
   328→        supabaseClient
   329→          .from('projects')
   330→          .select('settings')
   331→          .eq('id', ctx.projectId)
   332→          .maybeSingle(),
   333→        signal,
   334→      )
   335→    : Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: null });
   336→
   337→  const shotQuery = ctx.shotId
   338→    ? maybeAttachAbortSignal(
   339→        supabaseClient
   340→          .from('shots')
   341→          .select('settings')
   342→          .eq('id', ctx.shotId)
   343→          .maybeSingle(),
   344→        signal,
   345→      )
   346→    : Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: null });
   347→
   348→  return Promise.all([
   349→    userQuery,
   350→    projectQuery,
   351→    shotQuery,
   352→  ]) as Promise<[SettingsRow, SettingsRow, SettingsRow]>;
   353→}
   354→
   355→/** Extract tool-specific settings from scope results and merge in priority order. */
   356→function extractAndMergeSettings(
   357→  userResult: SettingsRow,
   358→  projectResult: SettingsRow,
   359→  shotResult: SettingsRow,
   360→  toolId: string,
   361→  ctx: ToolSettingsContext,
   362→): SettingsFetchResult {
   363→  if (userResult.error) {
   364→    throw new ToolSettingsError(
   365→      'scope_fetch_failed',
   366→      `Failed to load user settings: ${describeScopeError(userResult.error)}`,
   367→      { recoverable: true, cause: userResult.error, metadata: { scope: 'user' } },
   368→    );
   369→  }
   370→  if (ctx.projectId && projectResult.error) {
   371→    throw new ToolSettingsError(
   372→      'scope_fetch_failed',
   373→      `Failed to load project settings: ${describeScopeError(projectResult.error)}`,
   374→      { recoverable: true, cause: projectResult.error, metadata: { scope: 'project', projectId: ctx.projectId } },
   375→    );
   376→  }
   377→  if (ctx.shotId && shotResult.error) {
   378→    throw new ToolSettingsError(
   379→      'scope_fetch_failed',
   380→      `Failed to load shot settings: ${describeScopeError(shotResult.error)}`,
   381→      { recoverable: true, cause: shotResult.error, metadata: { scope: 'shot', shotId: ctx.shotId } },
   382→    );
   383→  }
   384→
   385→  const userSettingsData = userResult.data?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   386→  const projectSettingsData = projectResult.data?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   387→  const shotSettingsData = shotResult.data?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   388→  const userSettings = (userSettingsData?.[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   389→  const projectSettings = (projectSettingsData?.[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   390→  const shotSettings = (shotSettingsData?.[toolId] as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {};
   391→  const defaultSettings = (toolDefaultsRegistry[toolId] as Record<string, unknown> | undefined) ?? {};
   392→
   393→  const hasShotSettings = shotSettings && Object.keys(shotSettings).length > 0;
   394→
   395→  // Merge in priority order: defaults -> user -> project -> shot
   396→  const merged = deepMerge(
   397→    {},
   398→    defaultSettings,
   399→    userSettings,
   400→    projectSettings,
   401→    shotSettings
   402→  );
   403→
   404→  return { settings: merged, hasShotSettings };
   405→}
   406→
   407→// ============================================================================
   408→// Settings fetch
   409→// ============================================================================
   410→
   411→/**
   412→ * Fetch and merge tool settings from all scopes using direct Supabase calls.
   413→ *
   414→ * Pipeline: dedup → auth → fetchAllScopes → extractAndMerge.
   415→ * Uses single-flight deduplication for concurrent identical requests.
   416→ *
   417→ * @returns `{ settings, hasShotSettings }` wrapper
   418→ */
   419→export async function fetchToolSettingsSupabase(
   420→  toolId: string,
   421→  ctx: ToolSettingsContext,
   422→  signal?: AbortSignal
   423→): Promise<OperationResult<SettingsFetchResult>> {
   424→  try {
   425→    reportUnknownSettingsId(toolId);
   426→
   427→    // Check if request was cancelled before starting
   428→    if (signal?.aborted) {
   429→      return toOperationFailure(new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   430→        recoverable: true,
   431→      }));
   432→    }
   433→
   434→    // Single-flight dedupe key for concurrent identical requests
   435→    const singleFlightKey = JSON.stringify({ toolId, projectId: ctx.projectId ?? null, shotId: ctx.shotId ?? null });
   436→    const existingPromise = inflightSettingsFetches.get(singleFlightKey);
   437→    if (existingPromise) {
   438→      const value = await raceWithAbort(existingPromise as Promise<SettingsFetchResult>, signal);
   439→      return operationSuccess(value);
   440→    }
   441→
   442→    const promise = (async (): Promise<SettingsFetchResult> => {
   443→      throwIfAborted(signal);
   444→
   445→      const { data: { user } } = await getCachedUserId();
   446→
   447→      if (!user) {
   448→        throw new ToolSettingsError('auth_required', 'Authentication required');
   449→      }
   450→
   451→      throwIfAborted(signal);
   452→
   453→      const supabaseClient = getSupabaseClient();
   454→      const [userResult, projectResult, shotResult] = await fetchAllScopes(supabaseClient, user.id, ctx, signal);
   455→      throwIfAborted(signal);
   456→      return extractAndMergeSettings(userResult, projectResult, shotResult, toolId, ctx);
   457→    })();
   458→
   459→    inflightSettingsFetches.set(singleFlightKey, promise);
   460→    // The .finally cleanup promise is intentionally not awaited or returned.
   461→    // Suppress its rejection to avoid unhandled rejection warnings — the original
   462→    // `promise` reference (returned below) handles propagation to the caller.
   463→    promise.finally(() => {
   464→      inflightSettingsFetches.delete(singleFlightKey);
   465→    }).catch(() => {});
   466→    const value = await raceWithAbort(promise, signal);
   467→    return operationSuccess(value);
   468→
   469→  } catch (error: unknown) {
   470→    if (isToolSettingsError(error) && error.code === 'cancelled') {
   471→      return toOperationFailure(error);
   472→    }
   473→    if (isCancellationError(error)) {
   474→      return toOperationFailure(new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
   475→        recoverable: true,
   476→        cause: error,
   477→      }));
   478→    }
   479→    // Build context info without calling getSession() — can block during token refresh
   480→    const errorMsg = getErrorMessage(error);
   481→    const contextInfo = {
   482→      visibilityState: typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.visibilityState : 'unknown',
   483→      hidden: typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.hidden : false,
   484→      online: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true,
   485→    };
   486→
   487→    if (errorMsg.includes('Auth timeout') || errorMsg.includes('Auth request was cancelled')) {
   488→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
   489→        context: 'fetchToolSettingsSupabase.authTimeout',
   490→        showToast: false,
   491→        logData: contextInfo,
   492→      });
   493→      return toOperationFailure(new ToolSettingsError(
   494→        'network',
   495→        'Authentication check timed out. Please retry.',
   496→        {
   497→          recoverable: true,
   498→          cause: error,
   499→          metadata: { ...contextInfo, reason: 'auth_timeout' },
   500→        },
   501→      ));
   502→    }
   503→
   504→    if (errorMsg.includes('Failed to fetch')) {
   505→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'fetchToolSettingsSupabase.network', showToast: false, logData: contextInfo });
   506→      return toOperationFailure(new ToolSettingsError(
   507→        'network',
   508→        'Network connection issue. Please check your internet connection.',
   509→        {
   510→          recoverable: true,
   511→          cause: error,
   512→          metadata: contextInfo,
   513→        },
   514→      ));
   515→    }
   516→
   517→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'fetchToolSettingsSupabase', showToast: false, logData: contextInfo });
   518→    return toOperationFailure(classifyToolSettingsError(error));
   519→  }
   520→}
   521→
   522→/**
   523→ * Fetch tool settings and throw `ToolSettingsError` on failure.
   524→ *
   525→ * Use this from hook boundaries that already model errors as thrown exceptions
   526→ * (for example React Query query functions and mutation pipelines) so read/write
   527→ * settings flows share the same error contract.
   528→ */
   529→export async function fetchToolSettingsSupabaseOrThrow(
   530→  toolId: string,
   531→  ctx: ToolSettingsContext,
   532→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   533→): Promise<SettingsFetchResult> {
   534→  const result = await fetchToolSettingsSupabase(toolId, ctx, signal);
   535→  if (!result.ok) {
   536→    throw toToolSettingsErrorFromOperationFailure(result);
   537→  }
   538→  return result.value;
   539→}
   540→
```

```
1→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→import { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     3→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     4→import { Task } from '@/types/tasks';
     5→import { taskQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/tasks';
     6→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     7→import { isTaskDbRow, mapTaskDbRowToTask } from '@/shared/lib/taskRowMapper';
     8→import {
     9→  getPrimaryTaskMappingsForGenerations,
    10→  toGenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry,
    11→  type GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry,
    12→  type GenerationTaskMappingStatus,
    13→} from '@/shared/lib/generationTaskRepository';
    14→
    15→interface PreloadGenerationTaskMappingsOptions {
    16→  batchSize?: number;
    17→  delayBetweenBatches?: number;
    18→  preloadFullTaskData?: boolean;
    19→  projectId?: string;
    20→}
    21→
    22→export interface GenerationWithTaskData extends GenerationRow {
    23→  taskId?: string | null;
    24→  taskData?: Task | null;
    25→  taskMappingStatus?: GenerationTaskMappingStatus;
    26→  taskMappingError?: string;
    27→}
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * Preload task mappings for a batch of generations in the background.
    31→ */
    32→export async function preloadGenerationTaskMappings(
    33→  generationIds: string[],
    34→  queryClient: QueryClient,
    35→  options: PreloadGenerationTaskMappingsOptions = {},
    36→) {
    37→  const supabase = getSupabaseClient();
    38→  const {
    39→    batchSize = 5,
    40→    delayBetweenBatches = 200,
    41→    preloadFullTaskData = false,
    42→    projectId,
    43→  } = options;
    44→
    45→  for (let i = 0; i < generationIds.length; i += batchSize) {
    46→    const batch = generationIds.slice(i, i + batchSize);
    47→
    48→    const mappings = await getPrimaryTaskMappingsForGenerations(batch, { projectId });
    49→    let reportedBatchQueryFailure = false;
    50→
    51→    const prefetchTasks: Array<Promise<unknown>> = [];
    52→
    53→    batch.forEach((generationId) => {
    54→      const mapping = mappings.get(generationId);
    55→      const cacheEntry = toGenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry(mapping);
    56→      queryClient.setQueryData<GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry>(
    57→        taskQueryKeys.generationMapping(generationId),
    58→        cacheEntry,
    59→      );
    60→
    61→      if (!reportedBatchQueryFailure && cacheEntry.status === 'query_failed' && cacheEntry.queryError) {
    62→        reportedBatchQueryFailure = true;
    63→        normalizeAndPresentError(new Error(cacheEntry.queryError), {
    64→          context: 'GenerationTaskCache',
    65→          showToast: false,
    66→        });
    67→      }
    68→
    69→      if (preloadFullTaskData && cacheEntry.taskId && cacheEntry.status === 'ok') {
    70→        prefetchTasks.push(queryClient.prefetchQuery({
    71→          [REDACTED](cacheEntry.taskId),
    72→          queryFn: async () => {
    73→            const { data, error } = await supabase
    74→              .from('tasks')
    75→              .select('*')
    76→              .eq('id', cacheEntry.taskId)
    77→              .single();
    78→
    79→            if (error) throw error;
    80→            if (!isTaskDbRow(data)) {
    81→              throw new Error('Task row has unexpected shape');
    82→            }
    83→            return mapTaskDbRowToTask(data);
    84→          },
    85→          staleTime: 2 * 60 * 1000,
    86→        }));
    87→      }
    88→    });
    89→
    90→    if (prefetchTasks.length > 0) {
    91→      await Promise.all(prefetchTasks);
    92→    }
    93→
    94→    if (i + batchSize < generationIds.length) {
    95→      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayBetweenBatches));
    96→    }
    97→  }
    98→}
    99→
   100→/**
   101→ * Enhance generations with task data from cache.
   102→ */
   103→export function enhanceGenerationsWithTaskData(
   104→  generations: GenerationRow[],
   105→  queryClient: QueryClient,
   106→): GenerationWithTaskData[] {
   107→  return generations.map((generation) => {
   108→    const generationId = generation.generation_id ?? generation.id;
   109→    const cachedMapping = queryClient.getQueryData<GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry>(
   110→      taskQueryKeys.generationMapping(generationId),
   111→    );
   112→    const taskId = cachedMapping?.taskId || null;
   113→    const taskData = taskId ? queryClient.getQueryData<Task>(taskQueryKeys.single(taskId)) : null;
   114→    const taskMappingStatus = cachedMapping?.status ?? (taskId ? 'ok' : 'not_loaded');
   115→
   116→    return {
   117→      ...generation,
   118→      taskId,
   119→      taskData,
   120→      taskMappingStatus,
   121→      ...(cachedMapping?.queryError ? { taskMappingError: cachedMapping.queryError } : {}),
   122→    };
   123→  });
   124→}
```

```
1→import React, { createContext, useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     3→import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     4→import type { Task } from '@/types/tasks';
     5→import {
     6→  preloadGenerationTaskMappings,
     7→  enhanceGenerationsWithTaskData,
     8→} from '@/shared/lib/generationTaskCache';
     9→
    10→// ================================================================
    11→// GENERATION-TASK INTEGRATION CONTEXT
    12→// ================================================================
    13→// This context provides a centralized way for components to work with
    14→// both generation and task data seamlessly. It handles background
    15→// preloading and cache management automatically.
    16→
    17→interface GenerationTaskContextValue {
    18→  // Methods for working with generation-task relationships
    19→  preloadTaskMappings: (generationIds: string[]) => Promise<void>;
    20→  enhanceWithTaskData: (generations: GenerationRow[]) => (GenerationRow & { taskId?: string | null; taskData?: Task | null })[];
    21→  
    22→  // Configuration
    23→  isPreloadingEnabled: boolean;
    24→  setPreloadingEnabled: (enabled: boolean) => void;
    25→}
    26→
    27→const GenerationTaskContext = createContext<GenerationTaskContextValue | null>(null);
    28→
    29→interface GenerationTaskProviderProps {
    30→  children: React.ReactNode;
    31→  
    32→  // Configuration options
    33→  enableBackgroundPreloading?: boolean;
    34→  preloadBatchSize?: number;
    35→  preloadDelay?: number;
    36→}
    37→
    38→export function GenerationTaskProvider({ 
    39→  children, 
    40→  enableBackgroundPreloading = true,
    41→  preloadBatchSize = 5,
    42→  preloadDelay = 200 
    43→}: GenerationTaskProviderProps) {
    44→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    45→  const [isPreloadingEnabled, setPreloadingEnabled] = React.useState(enableBackgroundPreloading);
    46→
    47→  const preloadTaskMappings = React.useCallback(async (generationIds: string[]) => {
    48→    if (!isPreloadingEnabled || generationIds.length === 0) return;
    49→
    50→    try {
    51→      await preloadGenerationTaskMappings(generationIds, queryClient, {
    52→        batchSize: preloadBatchSize,
    53→        delayBetweenBatches: preloadDelay,
    54→        preloadFullTaskData: true, // Preload full task data for better UX
    55→      });
    56→    } catch {
    57→      // Best effort preloading: the main UI can still function without prefetched task data.
    58→    }
    59→  }, [queryClient, isPreloadingEnabled, preloadBatchSize, preloadDelay]);
    60→
    61→  const enhanceWithTaskData = React.useCallback((generations: GenerationRow[]) => {
    62→    return enhanceGenerationsWithTaskData(generations, queryClient);
    63→  }, [queryClient]);
    64→
    65→  // Memoize context value to prevent unnecessary re-renders of consumers
    66→  const contextValue = useMemo<GenerationTaskContextValue>(() => ({
    67→    preloadTaskMappings,
    68→    enhanceWithTaskData,
    69→    isPreloadingEnabled,
    70→    setPreloadingEnabled,
    71→  }), [preloadTaskMappings, enhanceWithTaskData, isPreloadingEnabled, setPreloadingEnabled]);
    72→
    73→  return (
    74→    <GenerationTaskContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
    75→      {children}
    76→    </GenerationTaskContext.Provider>
    77→  );
    78→}
    79→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * GenerationTaskContext Tests
     3→ *
     4→ * Tests for generation-task integration context.
     5→ */
     6→
     7→import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
     8→import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
     9→import React from 'react';
    10→import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';

    12→// Mock dependencies

    14→const mockEnhanceGenerations = vi.fn().mockReturnValue([]);
    15→
    16→vi.mock('@/shared/lib/generationTaskCache', () => ({
    17→  preloadGenerationTaskMappings: (...args: unknown[]) => mockPreloadMappings(...args),
    18→  enhanceGenerationsWithTaskData: (...args: unknown[]) => mockEnhanceGenerations(...args),
    19→}));

    21→import { GenerationTaskProvider } from '../GenerationTaskContext';
    22→
    23→function _createWrapper(providerProps: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    24→  const queryClient = new QueryClient({
    25→    defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, gcTime: 0 } },
    26→  });
    27→  return ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => (
    28→    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
    29→      <GenerationTaskProvider {...providerProps}>
    30→        {children}
    31→      </GenerationTaskProvider>
    32→    </QueryClientProvider>
    33→  );
    34→}
    35→
    36→describe('GenerationTaskContext', () => {
    37→  beforeEach(() => {
    38→    vi.clearAllMocks();
    39→  });
    40→
    41→  describe('GenerationTaskProvider', () => {
    42→    it('renders children', () => {
    43→      const queryClient = new QueryClient({
    44→        defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, gcTime: 0 } },
    45→      });
    46→
    47→      render(
    48→        <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
    49→          <GenerationTaskProvider>
    50→            <div data-testid="child">Hello</div>
    51→          </GenerationTaskProvider>
    52→        </QueryClientProvider>
    53→      );
    54→
    55→      expect(screen.getByTestId('child')).toHaveTextContent('Hello');
    56→    });
    57→
    58→    it('defaults to background preloading enabled', () => {
    59→      const queryClient = new QueryClient({
    60→        defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, gcTime: 0 } },
    61→      });
    62→
    63→      // The provider defaults enableBackgroundPreloading to true
    64→      // We verify this by rendering without props and checking it works
    65→      render(
    66→        <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
    67→          <GenerationTaskProvider>
    68→            <div data-testid="child">Hello</div>
    69→          </GenerationTaskProvider>
    70→        </QueryClientProvider>
    71→      );
    72→
    73→      expect(screen.getByTestId('child')).toBeInTheDocument();
    74→    });
    75→
    76→    it('can be configured with custom batch size and delay', () => {
```

### 2. **describeScopeError**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/toolSettingsService.ts`

**Lines:** 237-248

**Function Code:**
```typescript
function describeScopeError(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof Error && error.message) {
    return error.message;
  }
  if (error && typeof error === 'object') {
    const message = (error as { message?: unknown }).message;
    if (typeof message === 'string' && message.length > 0) {
      return message;
    }
  }
  return 'unknown error';
}
```

**What it does:** A generic error message extractor that handles Error instances, objects with message properties, and fallback strings. The name is misleading - it has nothing to do with "scope" specifically; it's just extracting error messages.

**Usage sites (3 locations in same file):**

1. **Line 366** - in `extractAndMergeSettings` function:
   ```typescript
   `Failed to load user settings: ${describeScopeError(userResult.error)}`
   ```

2. **Line 373** - in `extractAndMergeSettings` function:
   ```typescript
   `Failed to load project settings: ${describeScopeError(projectResult.error)}`
   ```

3. **Line 380** - in `extractAndMergeSettings` function:
   ```typescript
   `Failed to load shot settings: ${describeScopeError(shotResult.error)}`
   ```

All three usages are in template strings creating error messages for `ToolSettingsError` exceptions.

**Note:** There's a public `getErrorMessage` function already imported from `@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils` (line 15) and used elsewhere (lines 136, 480), which likely serves the same purpose and has a better name.

**Recommendation:** Rename to `getErrorMessage` or inline the 11 lines of logic directly into the three call sites. The current function could be replaced with the imported `getErrorMessage` if they're equivalent.

### 3. **enhanceGenerationsWithTaskData**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/generationTaskCache.ts`

**Lines:** 103-124

**Function Signature:**
```typescript

  generations: GenerationRow[],
  queryClient: QueryClient,
): GenerationWithTaskData[]
```

**Function Code:**
```typescript
export function enhanceGenerationsWithTaskData(
  generations: GenerationRow[],
  queryClient: QueryClient,
): GenerationWithTaskData[] {
  return generations.map((generation) => {
    const generationId = generation.generation_id ?? generation.id;
    const cachedMapping = queryClient.getQueryData<GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry>(
      taskQueryKeys.generationMapping(generationId),
    );
    const taskId = cachedMapping?.taskId || null;
    const taskData = taskId ? queryClient.getQueryData<Task>(taskQueryKeys.single(taskId)) : null;
    const taskMappingStatus = cachedMapping?.status ?? (taskId ? 'ok' : 'not_loaded');

    return {
      ...generation,
      taskId,
      taskData,
      taskMappingStatus,
      ...(cachedMapping?.queryError ? { taskMappingError: cachedMapping.queryError } : {}),
    };
  });
}
```

**What it does:** Pure transformation function using `.map()` that enriches generation objects with cached task data. Despite the name "enhance" (which suggests mutation), it returns a new array via `.map()` — a pure functional pattern.

**Usage sites (3 files):**

1. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/generationTaskCache.ts`** (the definition)

2. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/contexts/GenerationTaskContext.tsx`**

   - Lines 61-63: Wrapped in a context callback

   ```typescript
     return enhanceGenerationsWithTaskData(generations, queryClient);
   }, [queryClient]);
   ```

3. **`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/__tests__/generationTaskCache.test.ts`**

   ```typescript
   }));
   ```

**Return type:** `GenerationWithTaskData[]` (defined in generationTaskCache.ts, lines 22-27):
```typescript

  taskId?: string | null;

}
```

**Recommendation:** Rename to `mergeGenerationsWithTaskData`, `withTaskData`, or `enrichGenerationsWithTaskData` to better reflect the pure transformation pattern. The naming should make clear it returns a new array and doesn't mutate. Update all three import/usage sites.

```
   115→
   116→function parseNumber(value: unknown): number | undefined {
   117→  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined;
   118→}
   119→
   120→function parseString(value: unknown): string | undefined {
   121→  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined;
   122→}
   123→
   124→function hasLegacyField(
   125→  value: unknown,
   126→  field: string,
   127→): boolean {
   128→  return Boolean(
   129→    value
   130→    && typeof value === 'object'
   131→    && !Array.isArray(value)
   132→    && field in (value as Record<string, unknown>)
   133→    && (value as Record<string, unknown>)[field] !== undefined,
   134→  );
   135→}
   136→
   137→export interface TravelStructureLegacyUsage {
   138→  topLevelFields: LegacyTopLevelField[];
   139→  structureVideoFields: LegacyStructureVideoField[];
   140→}
   141→
   142→export function collectTravelStructureLegacyUsage(
   143→  value: unknown,
   144→): TravelStructureLegacyUsage {
   145→  const record = value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
   146→    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
   147→    : {};
   148→
   149→  const topLevelFields = LEGACY_TRAVEL_TOP_LEVEL_FIELDS.filter(
   150→    (field) => record[field] !== undefined,
   151→  );

   154→    ? record.structure_videos
   155→    : [];

   162→
   163→export function enforceTravelStructureLegacyPolicy(
   164→  usage: TravelStructureLegacyUsage,
   165→  options?: {
   166→    context?: string;
   167→    enforcement?: 'warn' | 'throw' | 'auto';
   168→  },
   169→): boolean {

   175→  const details = usedFields.join(', ');
   176→  return signalPastRemovalTargetUsage({
   177→    alias: 'travel_between_images.legacy_structure_fields',
   178→    policy: legacyStructurePolicy,
   179→    remediation: `Migrate to structure_guidance + canonical structure_videos. Legacy fields seen: ${details}.` +
   180→      (options?.context ? ` Context: ${options.context}.` : ''),
   181→    enforcement: options?.enforcement ?? 'auto',
   182→  });
   183→}
   184→
   185→/**
   186→ * Canonical migration adapter for legacy structure-video settings.
   187→ * Preserves explicit structure_type values and only falls back to defaults
   188→ * when legacy payloads omit type information.
   189→ */
   190→export function migrateLegacyStructureVideos(
   191→  value: unknown,
   192→  options: LegacyStructureVideoMigrationOptions,
   193→): MigratedLegacyStructureVideo[] {
   194→  const settings = asRecord(value);
   195→  if (!settings) {
   196→    return [];
   197→  }
   198→
   199→  const topLevelStructureType = parseStructureType(
   200→    settings.structure_video_type ?? settings.structureType,
   201→  );
   202→  const topLevelTreatment = parseTreatment(
   203→    settings.structure_video_treatment ?? settings.treatment,
   204→  );
   205→  const topLevelMotionStrength = parseNumber(
   206→    settings.structure_video_motion_strength ?? settings.motionStrength,
   207→  );
   208→  const topLevelUni3cEndPercent = parseNumber(settings.uni3c_end_percent);
   209→
   210→  const rawStructureVideos = Array.isArray(settings.structure_videos)
   211→    ? settings.structure_videos
   212→    : null;
   213→  if (rawStructureVideos) {
   214→    return rawStructureVideos
   215→      .map((entry) => {
   216→        const record = asRecord(entry);
   217→        if (!record) {
   218→          return null;
   219→        }
   220→
   221→        const path = parseString(record.path);
   222→        if (!path) {
   223→          return null;
   224→        }
   225→
   226→        const metadata = asRecord(record.metadata);
   227→        const resourceId = parseString(record.resource_id);
   228→        return {
   229→          path,
   230→          start_frame: parseNumber(record.start_frame) ?? 0,
   231→          end_frame: parseNumber(record.end_frame) ?? options.defaultEndFrame,
   232→          treatment: parseTreatment(record.treatment) ?? topLevelTreatment ?? options.defaultVideoTreatment,
   233→          motion_strength: parseNumber(record.motion_strength) ?? topLevelMotionStrength ?? options.defaultMotionStrength,
   234→          structure_type: parseStructureType(record.structure_type) ?? topLevelStructureType ?? options.defaultStructureType,
   235→          uni3c_end_percent: parseNumber(record.uni3c_end_percent) ?? topLevelUni3cEndPercent ?? options.defaultUni3cEndPercent,
   236→          metadata: metadata as VideoMetadata | null ?? null,
   237→          resource_id: resourceId ?? null,
   238→        } satisfies MigratedLegacyStructureVideo;
   239→      })
   240→      .filter((entry): entry is MigratedLegacyStructureVideo => entry !== null);
   241→  }
   242→
   243→  const singlePath = parseString(settings.structure_video_path ?? settings.path);
   244→  if (!singlePath) {
   245→    return [];
   246→  }
   247→
   248→  const metadata = asRecord(settings.metadata);
   249→  const resourceId = parseString(settings.resource_id ?? settings.resourceId);
   250→  return [{
   251→    path: singlePath,
   252→    start_frame: 0,
   253→    end_frame: options.defaultEndFrame,
   254→    treatment: topLevelTreatment ?? options.defaultVideoTreatment,
   255→    motion_strength: topLevelMotionStrength ?? options.defaultMotionStrength,
   256→    structure_type: topLevelStructureType ?? options.defaultStructureType,
   257→    uni3c_end_percent: topLevelUni3cEndPercent ?? options.defaultUni3cEndPercent,
   258→    metadata: metadata as VideoMetadata | null ?? null,
   259→    resource_id: resourceId ?? null,
   260→  }];
   261→}
   262→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * useTimelinePositions - Single Source of Truth for Timeline Positions
     3→ *
     4→ * This hook serves the TIMELINE DRAG path: the user drags a node on the
     5→ * timeline ruler and positions update optimistically while the RPC runs.
     6→ *
     7→ * The BATCH EDITOR path (drag-to-reorder in the image grid) is handled
     8→ * separately by useTimelineFrameUpdates, which does a DB-first write and
     9→ * relies on the React Query cache refetch to update the UI. The two paths
    10→ * share the same serialization queue (runSerializedTimelineWrite) and the
    11→ * same RPC (persistTimelineFrameBatch) so their writes are ordered correctly.
    12→ *
    13→ * State machine:
    14→ * - ONE positions map (no displayPositions, stablePositions, framePositions confusion)
    15→ * - Optimistic updates with rollback on error
    16→ * - pendingUpdatesRef protects optimistic state across async DB syncs
    17→ * - Batch database operations for performance
    18→ */
    19→
    20→import {
    21→  useState,
    22→  useCallback,
    23→  useRef,
    24→  useEffect
    25→} from 'react';
    26→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

    33→  isTimelineWriteActive,

    49→// ============================================================================

    53→/**
    54→ * State machine for position management
    55→ */
    56→type PositionStatus = 
    57→  | { type: 'idle' }
    58→  | { type: 'updating'; operationId: string; description: string }

    71→

    78→  updatePositions: (newPositions: Map<string, number>, options?: UpdateOptions) => Promise<void>;
    79→  addItemsAtPositions: (items: Array<{ id: string; position: number }>) => () => void;
    80→  removeItems: (ids: string[]) => () => void;
    81→
    82→  /**
    83→   * Apply optimistic visual update immediately without entering the write queue
    84→   * or touching the DB. Returns a rollback function (or null if nothing changed).
    85→   * Intended to be called BEFORE runSerializedTimelineWrite, with the returned
    86→   * rollback passed as UpdateOptions.externalRollback so updatePositions can
    87→   * roll back on DB error.
    88→   */
    89→  applyOptimisticPositionUpdate: (newPositions: Map<string, number>) => (() => void) | null;
```
