```
   120→// ============================================================================
   121→// HOOK IMPLEMENTATION
   122→// ============================================================================
   123→
   124→export function useTimelinePositions({
   125→  shotId,
   126→  shotGenerations,
   127→  onPositionsChange,
   128→}: UseTimelinePositionsProps): UseTimelinePositionsReturn {
   129→
   130→  const _queryClient = useQueryClient();
   131→  const invalidateGenerations = useInvalidateGenerations();
   132→  
   133→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   134→  // STATE
   135→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   136→  
   137→  // The single source of truth for positions
   138→  const [positions, setPositions] = useState<Map<string, number>>(new Map());
   139→
   140→  // Ref mirror — lets applyOptimistic and updatePositions read current positions
   141→  // in callbacks without positions being in their dep arrays (which would cause
   142→  // recreation on every drag frame, cascading through the entire call chain).
   143→  const positionsRef = useRef<Map<string, number>>(new Map());
   144→  positionsRef.current = positions;
   145→
   146→  // State machine status
   147→  const [status, setStatus] = useState<PositionStatus>({ type: 'idle' });
   148→  
   149→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→  // REFS
   151→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   152→  
   153→  // Track pending updates for rollback support
   154→  const pendingUpdatesRef = useRef<Map<string, PendingUpdate>>(new Map());
   155→
   156→  // Operation counter for tracking concurrent operations
   157→  const operationIdRef = useRef(0);
   158→
   159→  // Snapshot for rollback on error
   160→  const snapshotRef = useRef<Map<string, number> | null>(null);
   161→
   162→  // Track the last sync to prevent redundant updates
   163→  const lastSyncRef = useRef<string>('');
   164→
   165→  // Refs that mirror isLocked/status for syncFromDatabase to read without
   166→  // being in its useCallback deps (changing these must NOT recreate the
   167→  // callback, which would spuriously re-run the sync useEffect with stale
   168→  // shotGenerations before the invalidation refetch returns).
   169→  const isLockedRef = useRef(false);
   170→  const isUpdatingRef = useRef(false);
   171→
   172→  // Count of in-flight DB writes (incremented synchronously by applyOptimistic,
   173→  // decremented in the updatePositions finally block). Acts as a hard guard in
   174→  // syncFromDatabase: even if isLockedRef/isUpdatingRef are mysteriously cleared
   175→  // by a race condition, this counter prevents old server data from overwriting
   176→  // in-flight optimistic positions.
   177→  const writeInFlightRef = useRef(0);
   178→
   179→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   180→  // LIFECYCLE + POSITIONS-CHANGE AUDIT
   181→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182→
   183→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   184→  // DATABASE SYNC
   185→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   186→
   187→  /**
   188→   * Sync positions from database data
   189→   * Only updates if we're idle and data has changed
   190→   */
   191→  const syncFromDatabase = useCallback(() => {
   192→    // Use refs so that toggling the lock/status does NOT recreate this callback
   193→    // (which would cause the useEffect below to fire with stale shotGenerations).
   194→    if (isLockedRef.current) {
   195→      return;
   196→    }
   197→
   198→    if (isUpdatingRef.current) {
   199→      return;
   200→    }
   201→
   202→    // Hard guard: block any sync while a DB write is in flight, even if the
   203→    // lock refs are somehow cleared by a race. writeInFlightRef is incremented
   204→    // synchronously by applyOptimistic and decremented only in the finally block.
   205→    if (writeInFlightRef.current > 0) {
   206→      return;
   207→    }
   208→
   209→    // Cross-path guard: suppress sync while ANY serialized write is active for
   210→    // this shot — including writes from the batch editor (useTimelineFrameUpdates),
   211→    // which does not set isLockedRef. Without this check, a background refetch
   212→    // fired during a slow midpoint RPC would apply stale positions and snap the
   213→    // timeline ruler back before the write completes.
   214→    //
   215→    // CRITICAL: Only suppress if we already have positions loaded (positionsRef.current.size > 0).
   216→    // On initial mount the positions map is empty — there is nothing to protect,
   217→    // and suppressing here leaves the timeline blank for the entire duration of
   218→    // any concurrent batch editor write.
   219→    if (shotId && isTimelineWriteActive(shotId) && positionsRef.current.size > 0) {
   220→      return;
   221→    }
   222→
   223→    const serverPositions = buildServerPositions(shotGenerations);
   224→    const { merged: mergedPositions, idsToClear } = mergePendingUpdates(serverPositions, pendingUpdatesRef.current);
   225→    if (idsToClear.length > 0) {
   226→      idsToClear.forEach(id => pendingUpdatesRef.current.delete(id));
   227→    }
   228→
   229→    // Check if positions actually changed
   230→    const [REDACTED](mergedPositions);
   231→    if (syncKey === lastSyncRef.current) {
   232→      return; // No change
   233→    }
   234→    lastSyncRef.current = syncKey;
   235→
   236→    // Sync positions immediately (not in transition) to prevent flicker
   237→    // when new items are added - the position must be available before render
   238→    setPositions(mergedPositions);
   239→
   240→    // Notify parent if callback provided
   241→    if (onPositionsChange) {
   242→      onPositionsChange(mergedPositions);
   243→    }
   244→
   245→  }, [shotGenerations, onPositionsChange, shotId]);
   246→  
   247→  // Auto-sync when shot generations change
   248→  useEffect(() => {
   249→    if (shotId && shotGenerations.length > 0) {
   250→      syncFromDatabase();
   251→    }
   252→  }, [shotId, shotGenerations, syncFromDatabase]);
   253→  
   254→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   255→  // OPTIMISTIC UPDATE HELPERS
   256→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   257→  
   258→  /**
   259→   * Apply optimistic update immediately and return a rollback function
   260→   */
   261→  const applyOptimistic = useCallback((
   262→    updates: Array<{ id: string; position: number; operation: 'add' | 'move' | 'remove' }>
   263→  ): (() => void) => {
   264→
   265→    // Take snapshot for rollback
   266→    snapshotRef.current = new Map(positionsRef.current);
   267→
   268→    // CRITICAL: Populate pendingUpdatesRef SYNCHRONOUSLY before setPositions.
   269→    // React state updaters run asynchronously (deferred in concurrent mode), so
   270→    // putting side effects inside them creates a race: syncFromDatabase can fire
   271→    // between setPositions() and the updater running, seeing pendingCount: 0 and
   272→    // overwriting optimistic state with stale server data.
   273→    const now = Date.now();
   274→    updates.forEach(({ id, position, operation }) => {
   275→      pendingUpdatesRef.current.set(id, {
   276→        id,
   277→        oldPosition: positionsRef.current.get(id) ?? null,
   278→        newPosition: position,
   279→        operation,
   280→        timestamp: now,
   281→      });
   282→    });
   283→
   284→    // Apply updates immediately (pure state update — no side effects inside)
   285→    setPositions(current => {
   286→      const next = new Map(current);
   287→      updates.forEach(({ id, position, operation }) => {
   288→        if (operation === 'remove') {
   289→          next.delete(id);
   290→        } else {
   291→          next.set(id, position);
   292→        }
   293→      });
   294→      return next;
   295→    });
   296→
   297→    // Return rollback function
   298→    return () => {
   299→      if (snapshotRef.current) {
   300→        setPositions(snapshotRef.current);
   301→        snapshotRef.current = null;
   302→      }
   303→      updates.forEach(({ id }) => {
   304→        pendingUpdatesRef.current.delete(id);
   305→      });
   306→    };
   307→
   308→  }, []);
   309→
   310→  /**
   311→   * Clear pending updates after successful database write
   312→   */
   313→  const clearPendingUpdates = useCallback((ids: string[]) => {
   314→    ids.forEach(id => pendingUpdatesRef.current.delete(id));
   315→    snapshotRef.current = null;
   316→  }, []);
   317→  
   318→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   319→  // CORE OPERATIONS
   320→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   321→  
   322→  /**
   323→   * Update positions with optimistic UI and database persistence
   324→   * This is the main entry point for position changes
   325→   * NOTE: Positions are automatically quantized to ensure 4N+1 gaps for Wan model compatibility
   326→   */
   327→  const updatePositions = useCallback(async (
   328→    newPositions: Map<string, number>,
   329→    options: UpdateOptions = {}
   330→  ) => {
   331→    const {
   332→      operation = 'reorder',
   333→      skipOptimistic = false,
   334→      skipDatabase = false,
   335→      metadata = {},
   336→      externalRollback,
   337→    } = options;
   338→
   339→    if (!shotId) {
   340→      return;
   341→    }
   342→
   343→    // Drag/tap paths already quantize in timeline utils; re-quantizing here can
   344→    // rewrite the full timeline and unnecessarily inflate persisted update count.
   345→    const shouldQuantize = operation !== 'drag';
   346→    const quantizedPositions = shouldQuantize ? quantizePositions(newPositions) : new Map(newPositions);
   347→
   348→    const operationId = `op-${++operationIdRef.current}-${operation}`;
   349→
   350→    // Calculate what changed (using quantized positions).
   351→    // When skipOptimistic=true an external caller already applied the optimistic
   352→    // update before this function runs, so positionsRef.current already reflects
   353→    // the new positions. Use the pre-optimistic snapshot (set synchronously by
   354→    // applyOptimistic) to correctly compute the diff; otherwise changes.length===0
   355→    // and the DB write is skipped entirely.
   356→    const basePositions =
   357→      skipOptimistic && snapshotRef.current ? snapshotRef.current : positionsRef.current;
   358→    const changes = detectPositionChanges(basePositions, quantizedPositions);
   359→
   360→    // Detect trailing endpoint removal (was in old positions, not in new)
   361→    const trailingRemoved = positionsRef.current.has(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY) && !quantizedPositions.has(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
   362→
   363→    if (changes.length === 0 && !trailingRemoved) {
   364→      return;
   365→    }
   366→
   367→    // Validate: no duplicate positions
   368→    if (hasDuplicatePositionValues(quantizedPositions)) {
   369→      toast.error('Position conflict detected - operation cancelled');
   370→      return;
   371→    }
   372→    
   373→    // Apply optimistic update.
   374→    // When skipOptimistic=true, the caller already applied the optimistic update
   375→    // externally (before entering the write queue) for instant visual feedback.
   376→    // In that case we reuse the caller's rollback function so errors still revert.
   377→    let rollback: (() => void) | null = null;
   378→    if (!skipOptimistic) {
   379→      const optimisticUpdates = changes.map(c => ({
   380→        id: c.id,
   381→        position: c.newPos,
   382→        operation: (c.oldPos === null ? 'add' : 'move') as 'add' | 'move' | 'remove',
   383→      }));
   384→      if (trailingRemoved) {
   385→        optimisticUpdates.push({
   386→          id: TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY,
   387→          position: 0,
   388→          operation: 'remove',
   389→        });
   390→      }
   391→      rollback = applyOptimistic(optimisticUpdates);
   392→    } else if (externalRollback !== undefined) {
   393→      rollback = externalRollback;
   394→    }
   395→    
   396→    // If skipping database, we're done
   397→    if (skipDatabase) {
   398→      clearPendingUpdates(changes.map(c => c.id));
   399→      return;
   400→    }
   401→    
   402→    // Mark a DB write in flight BEFORE the try block so the finally block always
   403→    // decrements it. This counter is the hard guard in syncFromDatabase — even if
   404→    // isLockedRef/isUpdatingRef are mysteriously cleared, writeInFlightRef > 0
   405→    // blocks any sync from applying stale server data over our optimistic state.
   406→    writeInFlightRef.current++;
   407→
   408→    // Set status to updating
   409→    isUpdatingRef.current = true;
   410→    setStatus({
   411→      type: 'updating',
   412→      operationId,
   413→      description: `${operation}: ${changes.length} items`,
   414→    });
   415→
   416→    // Lock positions to prevent sync interference
   417→    isLockedRef.current = true;
   418→
   419→    try {
   420→      // Partition changes: trailing endpoint vs. normal images
   421→      const trailingChange = changes.find(c => c.id === TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
   422→      const imageChanges = changes.filter(c => c.id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
   423→
   424→      // Build database updates for normal image changes
   425→      const dbUpdates: Array<{ shot_generation_id: string; timeline_frame: number }> = [];
   426→      const unmatchedChangeIds: string[] = [];
   427→
   428→      for (const change of imageChanges) {
   429→        // Find the shot_generation record for this item
   430→        // change.id is shot_generations.id which matches sg.id directly
   431→        const shotGen = shotGenerations.find(sg => {
   432→          // Direct match on shot_generations.id
   433→          return sg.id === change.id;
   434→        });
   435→
   436→        if (shotGen) {
   437→          dbUpdates.push({
   438→            shot_generation_id: shotGen.id,
   439→            timeline_frame: change.newPos
   440→          });
   441→        } else {
   442→          unmatchedChangeIds.push(change.id);
   443→        }
   444→      }
   445→
   446→      if (trailingChange) {
   447→        await setTrailingEndpointFrame(
   448→          {
   449→            shotId,
   450→            operationId,
   451→            shotGenerations,
   452→            logPrefix: '',
   453→            log: () => {},
   454→          },
   455→          trailingChange.newPos,
   456→        );
   457→      }
   458→
   459→      if (trailingRemoved) {
   460→        await clearTrailingEndpointFrame({
   461→          shotId,
   462→          operationId,
   463→          shotGenerations,
   464→          logPrefix: '',
   465→          log: () => {},
   466→        });
   467→      }
   468→
   469→      if (dbUpdates.length === 0 && !trailingChange && !trailingRemoved) {
   470→        clearPendingUpdates(changes.map(c => c.id));
   471→        return;
   472→      }
   473→
   474→      // Batch-update normal image positions (skip if only trailing changed)
   475→      if (dbUpdates.length > 0) {
   476→        await persistTimelineFrameBatch({
   477→          shotId,
   478→          updates: dbUpdates.map((update) => ({
   479→            shotGenerationId: update.shot_generation_id,
   480→            timelineFrame: update.timeline_frame,
   481→            metadata: {
   482→              user_positioned: true,
   483→              drag_source: operation,
   484→              ...metadata,
   485→            },
   486→          })),
   487→          operationLabel: `timeline-positions-${operation}`,
   488→          timeoutOperationName: 'timeline-positions-batch-rpc',
   489→          logPrefix: '',
   490→          log: () => {},
   491→        });
   492→      }
   493→      
   494→      // Success!
   495→      // NOTE: We do NOT clear pending updates here anymore.
   496→      // We leave them in pendingUpdatesRef until syncFromDatabase verifies that
   497→      // the server data has actually caught up. This prevents "flash of stale content".
   498→      // clearPendingUpdates(changes.map(c => c.id));
   499→      
   500→      // Invalidate cache after a short delay to allow UI to settle
   501→      invalidateGenerations(shotId, {
   502→        reason: 'timeline-position-batch-persist',
   503→        scope: 'images',
   504→        delayMs: 100
   505→      });
   506→      
   507→    } catch (error) {
   508→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'TimelinePositions', showToast: false, logData: { operationId } });
   509→
   510→      // Rollback optimistic update
   511→      if (rollback) {
   512→        rollback();
   513→      }
   514→
   515→      setStatus({
   516→        type: 'error',
   517→        message: (error as Error).message,
   518→        canRetry: true
   519→      });
   520→
   521→      toast.error('Failed to update positions');
   522→      throw error;
   523→
   524→    } finally {
   525→      // Decrement write-in-flight counter (incremented before the try block).
   526→      // Must happen before unlocking so syncFromDatabase stays blocked until
   527→      // both the counter AND the lock are cleared.
   528→      writeInFlightRef.current = Math.max(0, writeInFlightRef.current - 1);
   529→
   530→      // Unlock positions — clear ref first so syncFromDatabase guards fire
   531→      // correctly before the state re-render triggers the useEffect.
   532→      isLockedRef.current = false;
   533→
   534→      // Reset status
   535→      isUpdatingRef.current = false;
   536→      setTimeout(() => {
   537→        setStatus(current =>
   538→          current.type === 'updating' && current.operationId === operationId
   539→            ? { type: 'idle' }
   540→            : current,
   541→        );
   542→      }, 50);
   543→    }
   544→    
   545→  }, [
   546→    shotId,
   547→    shotGenerations,
   548→    applyOptimistic,
   549→    clearPendingUpdates,
   550→    invalidateGenerations,
   551→  ]);
   552→  
   553→  /**
   554→   * Add new items at specific positions (for drops)
   555→   * Returns a rollback function
   556→   */
   557→  const addItemsAtPositions = useCallback((
   558→    items: Array<{ id: string; position: number }>
   559→  ): (() => void) => {
   560→    
   561→    // Apply optimistic update
   562→    const rollback = applyOptimistic(
   563→      items.map(({ id, position }) => ({ 
   564→        id, 
   565→        position, 
   566→        operation: 'add' as const 
   567→      }))
   568→    );
   569→    
   570→    return rollback;
   571→  }, [applyOptimistic]);
   572→  
   573→  /**
   574→   * Remove items from positions (for deletions)
   575→   * Returns a rollback function
   576→   */
   577→  const removeItems = useCallback((ids: string[]): (() => void) => {
   578→    
   579→    // Apply optimistic update
   580→    const rollback = applyOptimistic(
   581→      ids.map(id => ({ 
   582→        id, 
   583→        position: 0, // Position doesn't matter for remove
   584→        operation: 'remove' as const 
   585→      }))
   586→    );
   587→    
   588→    return rollback;
   589→  }, [applyOptimistic]);
   590→  
   591→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   592→  // LOCK CONTROLS
   593→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   594→  
   595→  const lockPositions = useCallback(() => {
   596→    isLockedRef.current = true;
   597→  }, []);
   598→
   599→  const unlockPositions = useCallback(() => {
   600→    // Don't release the lock while a write is in progress — the updatePositions
   601→    // finally block owns that lock and will release it. Without this guard, the
   602→    // external isDragInProgress effect in usePositionManagement fires unlockPositions()
   603→    // on drop (after the render that follows the drop event), which races with and
   604→    // overrides the internal lock acquired synchronously by updatePositions.
   605→    if (isUpdatingRef.current) {
   606→      return;
   607→    }
   608→    isLockedRef.current = false;
   609→  }, []);
   610→  
   611→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   612→  // HELPERS
   613→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   614→  
   615→  const getPosition = useCallback((id: string): number | undefined => {
   616→    return positions.get(id);
   617→  }, [positions]);
   618→  
   619→  const hasPosition = useCallback((id: string): boolean => {
   620→    return positions.has(id);
   621→  }, [positions]);
   622→  
   623→  const hasPendingUpdate = useCallback((id: string): boolean => {
   624→    return pendingUpdatesRef.current.has(id);
   625→  }, []);
   626→  
   627→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   628→  // DERIVED STATE
   629→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   630→  
   631→  const isUpdating = status.type === 'updating';
   632→  const isIdle = status.type === 'idle';
   633→  
   634→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   635→  // PRE-QUEUE OPTIMISTIC HELPER
   636→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   637→
   638→  /**
   639→   * Apply an optimistic visual update immediately, without entering the write
   640→   * queue or touching the DB. Designed to be called in setFramePositions BEFORE
   641→   * runSerializedTimelineWrite so the user sees the drag result at once, even
   642→   * when the queue is blocked by a concurrent batch-editor RPC.
   643→   *
   644→   * Returns the rollback function from applyOptimistic (null if nothing changed).
   645→   * Pass this as UpdateOptions.externalRollback to the subsequent updatePositions
   646→   * call so errors can revert the visual state.
   647→   *
   648→   * Uses the drag-path rule: no quantization (matches updatePositions 'drag').
   649→   */
   650→  const applyOptimisticPositionUpdate = useCallback((
   651→    newPositions: Map<string, number>,
   652→  ): (() => void) | null => {
   653→    const updates: Array<{ id: string; position: number; operation: 'add' | 'move' | 'remove' }> = [];
   654→    for (const [id, newPos] of newPositions) {
   655→      const oldPos = positionsRef.current.get(id);
   656→      if (oldPos !== newPos) {
   657→        updates.push({
   658→          id,
   659→          position: newPos,
   660→          operation: oldPos === undefined ? 'add' : 'move',
   661→        });
   662→      }
   663→    }
   664→    if (updates.length === 0) return null;
   665→    return applyOptimistic(updates);
   666→  }, [applyOptimistic]);
   667→
   668→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   669→  // BACKWARDS COMPATIBILITY
   670→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   671→
   672→  // For backwards compatibility, expose the same interface as the old hook
   673→  const setFramePositions = useCallback(async (newPositions: Map<string, number>) => {
   674→    await updatePositions(newPositions, { operation: 'drag' });
   675→  }, [updatePositions]);
   676→  
   677→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   678→  // RETURN
   679→  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
   680→  
   681→  return {
   682→    // The single source of truth
   683→    positions,
   684→
   685→    // Status
   686→    status,
   687→    isUpdating,
   688→    isIdle,
   689→
   690→    // Core operations
   691→    updatePositions,
   692→    addItemsAtPositions,
   693→    removeItems,
   694→    applyOptimisticPositionUpdate,
   695→
   696→    // Sync control
   697→    syncFromDatabase,
   698→    lockPositions,
   699→    unlockPositions,
   700→
   701→    // Helpers
   702→    getPosition,
   703→    hasPosition,
   704→    hasPendingUpdate,
   705→
   706→    // Backwards compatibility
   707→    displayPositions: positions,
   708→    framePositions: positions,
   709→    setFramePositions,
   710→  };
   711→}
   712→
   713→// Export types
   714→export type { UseTimelinePositionsReturn, UpdateOptions, PositionStatus };
   715→
```

```
    23→import { useEmptyStateDrop } from "./hooks/drag/useEmptyStateDrop";
    24→
    25→interface TimelineCoreAdapter {
    26→  shotId: string;
    27→  projectId?: string;
    28→  frameSpacing: number;
    29→  readOnly?: boolean;
    30→  shotGenerations?: GenerationRow[];
    31→  images?: GenerationRow[];
    32→  allGenerations?: GenerationRow[];
    33→}
    34→
    35→interface TimelineInteractionAdapter {
    36→  onImageReorder: (orderedIds: string[], draggedItemId?: string) => void;
    37→  onFramePositionsChange?: (framePositions: Map<string, number>) => void;
    38→  onFileDrop?: (files: File[], targetFrame?: number) => Promise<void>;
    39→  onGenerationDrop?: (generationId: string, imageUrl: string, thumbUrl: string | undefined, targetFrame?: number) => Promise<void>;
    40→  onImageDelete: NonNullable<LightboxActionHandlers['onDelete']>;
    41→  onImageDuplicate?: (imageId: string, timeline_frame: number) => void;
    42→  duplicatingImageId?: string | null;
    43→  duplicateSuccessImageId?: string | null;
    44→  onPairClick?: (pairIndex: number, pairData: PairData) => void;
    45→  onClearEnhancedPrompt?: (pairIndex: number) => void;
    46→  onDragStateChange?: (isDragging: boolean) => void;
    47→  onNewShotFromSelection?: (selectedIds: string[]) => Promise<string | void>;
    48→  onSegmentFrameCountChange?: (pairShotGenerationId: string, frameCount: number) => void;
    49→  onRegisterTrailingUpdater?: (fn: (endFrame: number) => void) => void;
    50→}
    51→
    52→interface TimelineDisplayAdapter {
    53→  defaultPrompt?: string;
    54→  defaultNegativePrompt?: string;
    55→  projectAspectRatio?: string;
    56→  maxFrameLimit?: number;
    57→  selectedOutputId?: string | null;
    58→}
    59→
    60→interface TimelineUploadAdapter {
    61→  onImageUpload?: (files: File[]) => Promise<void>;
    62→  isUploadingImage?: boolean;
    63→  uploadProgress?: number;
    64→}
    65→
    66→interface TimelineShotWorkflowAdapter {
    67→  allShots?: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>;
    68→  selectedShotId?: string;
    69→  onShotChange?: (shotId: string) => void;
    70→  onAddToShot?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    71→  onAddToShotWithoutPosition?: (targetShotId: string, generationId: string, imageUrl?: string, thumbUrl?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
    72→  onCreateShot?: (shotName: string, files: File[]) => Promise<{ shotId?: string; shotName?: string } | void>;
    73→}
    74→
    75→interface TimelineSegmentNavigationAdapter {
    76→  segmentSlots?: SegmentSlot[];
    77→  isSegmentsLoading?: boolean;
    78→  hasPendingTask?: (pairShotGenerationId: string | null | undefined) => boolean;
    79→  onOpenSegmentSlot?: (pairIndex: number) => void;
    80→  pendingImageToOpen?: string | null;
    81→  pendingImageVariantId?: string | null;
    82→  onClearPendingImageToOpen?: () => void;
    83→  navigateWithTransition?: (doNavigation: () => void) => void;
    84→}
    85→
    86→interface TimelineProps {
    87→  core: TimelineCoreAdapter;
    88→  interactions: TimelineInteractionAdapter;
    89→  display?: TimelineDisplayAdapter;
    90→  uploads?: TimelineUploadAdapter;
    91→  shotWorkflow?: TimelineShotWorkflowAdapter;
    92→  segmentNavigation?: TimelineSegmentNavigationAdapter;
    93→}
    94→
    95→const Timeline: React.FC<TimelineProps> = ({
    96→  core,
    97→  interactions,
    98→  display,
    99→  uploads,
   100→  shotWorkflow,
   101→  segmentNavigation,
   102→}) => {
   103→  const {
   104→    shotId,
   105→    projectId,
   106→    frameSpacing,
   107→    readOnly = false,
   108→    shotGenerations: propShotGenerations,
   109→    images: propImages,
   110→    allGenerations: propAllGenerations,
   111→  } = core;
   112→  const {
   113→    onImageReorder,
   114→    onFramePositionsChange,
   115→    onFileDrop,
   116→    onGenerationDrop,
   117→    onImageDelete,
   118→    onImageDuplicate,
   119→    duplicatingImageId,
   120→    duplicateSuccessImageId,
   121→    onPairClick,
   122→    onClearEnhancedPrompt,
   123→    onDragStateChange,
   124→    onNewShotFromSelection,
   125→    onSegmentFrameCountChange,
   126→    onRegisterTrailingUpdater,
   127→  } = interactions;
   128→  const {
   129→    defaultPrompt,
   130→    defaultNegativePrompt,
   131→    projectAspectRatio,
   132→    maxFrameLimit = 81,
   133→    selectedOutputId,
   134→  } = display ?? {};
   135→  const {
   136→    onImageUpload,
   137→    isUploadingImage,
   138→    uploadProgress = 0,
   139→  } = uploads ?? {};
   140→  const {
   141→    allShots,
   142→    selectedShotId,
   143→    onShotChange,
   144→    onAddToShot,
   145→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   146→    onCreateShot,
   147→  } = shotWorkflow ?? {};
   148→  const {
   149→    segmentSlots,
   150→    isSegmentsLoading,
   151→    hasPendingTask,
   152→    onOpenSegmentSlot,
   153→    pendingImageToOpen,
   154→    pendingImageVariantId,
   155→    onClearPendingImageToOpen,
   156→    navigateWithTransition,
   157→  } = segmentNavigation ?? {};
   158→
   159→  const [isDragInProgress, setIsDragInProgress] = useState<boolean>(false);
   160→
   161→  // Notify parent when drag state changes - used to suppress query refetches
   162→  useEffect(() => {
   163→    onDragStateChange?.(isDragInProgress);
   164→  }, [isDragInProgress, onDragStateChange]);
   165→
   166→  // Keep data/query orchestration out of the view layer.
   167→  const {
   168→    images,
   169→    readOnlyGenerations,
   170→    displayPositions,
   171→    setFramePositions,
   172→    actualPairPrompts,
   173→    loadPositions,
   174→  } = useTimelineDomainService({
   175→    shotId,
   176→    projectId,
   177→    frameSpacing,
   178→    isDragInProgress,
   179→    onFramePositionsChange,
   180→    propShotGenerations,
   181→    propImages,
   182→    propAllGenerations,
   183→    readOnly,
   184→  });
   185→
   186→  const {
   187→    lightbox,
   188→    media,
   189→    external,
   190→    shotSelection,
   191→    taskDetails,
   192→  } = useTimelineLightboxOrchestrator({
   193→    shotId,
   194→    projectId,
   195→    images,
   196→    selectedShotId,
   197→    onAddToShot,
   198→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   199→    segmentSlots,
   200→    onOpenSegmentSlot,
   201→    pendingImageToOpen,
   202→    pendingImageVariantId,
   203→    onClearPendingImageToOpen,
   204→    navigateWithTransition,
   205→  });
   206→
   207→  // Listen for star updates and refetch shot data
   208→  useAppEventListener('generation-star-updated', useCallback(({ shotId: updatedShotId }) => {
   209→    // Only refetch if this event is for our current shot
   210→    if (updatedShotId === shotId) {
   211→      loadPositions?.({ silent: true, reason: 'shot_change' });
   212→    }
   213→  }, [shotId, loadPositions]));
   214→
   215→  // Handle resetting frames to evenly spaced intervals
   216→  // Gap values are quantized to 4N+1 format for Wan model compatibility
   217→  const handleResetFrames = useCallback(async (gap: number) => {
   218→    // Quantize the gap to 4N+1 format (5, 9, 13, 17, 21, 25, 29, 33, ...)
   219→    const quantizedGap = quantizeGap(gap, 5);
   220→    
   221→    // Then set the positions with the specified quantized gap
   222→    const newPositions = new Map<string, number>();
   223→    images.forEach((image, index) => {
   224→      // Use id (shot_generations.id) for position mapping - unique per entry
   225→      // First image at 0, subsequent images at quantized intervals
   226→      newPositions.set(image.id, index * quantizedGap);
   227→    });
   228→
   229→    await setFramePositions(newPositions);
   230→  }, [images, setFramePositions]);
   231→
   232→  // Check if timeline is empty
   233→  const hasNoImages = images.length === 0;
   234→
   235→  // Empty-state drag and drop (supports both files and internal generations)
   236→  const {
   237→    isDragOver,
   238→    dragType,
   239→    handleEmptyStateDragEnter,
   240→    handleEmptyStateDragOver,
   241→    handleEmptyStateDragLeave,
   242→    handleEmptyStateDrop,
   243→  } = useEmptyStateDrop({
   244→    onFileDrop,
   245→    onGenerationDrop,
   246→    onImageUpload,
   247→  });
   248→
   249→  const timelineLightboxProps = useMemo<MediaLightboxProps | null>(() => {
   250→    if (!shotSelection.lightboxShotState || !media.currentLightboxImage) {
   251→      return null;
   252→    }
   253→
   254→    const selectedLightboxShotId = shotSelection.lightboxShotState.isExternalGen
   255→      ? external.externalGenLightboxSelectedShot
   256→      : shotSelection.lightboxSelectedShotId;
   257→
   258→    return {
   259→      media: media.currentLightboxImage,
   260→      shotId,
   261→      onClose: () => {
   262→        lightbox.capturedVariantIdRef.current = null;
   263→        lightbox.closeLightbox();
   264→        shotSelection.setLightboxSelectedShotId(selectedShotId || shotId);
   265→      },
   266→      readOnly,
   267→      toolTypeOverride: TOOL_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES,
   268→      initialVariantId: lightbox.capturedVariantIdRef.current ?? undefined,
   269→      adjacentSegments: !shotSelection.lightboxShotState.isExternalGen
   270→        ? shotSelection.adjacentSegmentsData
   271→        : undefined,
   272→      onNext: images.length > 1 ? lightbox.goNext : undefined,
   273→      onPrevious: images.length > 1 ? lightbox.goPrev : undefined,
   274→      showNavigation: lightbox.showNavigation,
   275→      hasNext: lightbox.hasNext,
   276→      hasPrevious: lightbox.hasPrevious,
   277→      onNavigateToGeneration: (generationId: string) => {
   278→        const index = media.currentImages.findIndex((img) => img.id === generationId);
   279→        if (index !== -1) {
   280→          lightbox.openLightbox(index);
   281→        } else {
   282→          toast.info('This generation is not currently loaded');
   283→        }
   284→      },
   285→      onOpenExternalGeneration: external.handleOpenExternalGeneration,
   286→      allShots,
   287→      selectedShotId: selectedLightboxShotId,
   288→      onShotChange: shotSelection.lightboxShotState.isExternalGen
   289→        ? (shotId) => {
   290→            external.setExternalGenLightboxSelectedShot(shotId);
   291→          }
   292→        : (shotId) => {
   293→            shotSelection.setLightboxSelectedShotId(shotId);
   294→            onShotChange?.(shotId);
   295→          },
   296→      onAddToShot: shotSelection.lightboxShotState.isExternalGen
   297→        ? external.handleExternalGenAddToShot
   298→        : shotSelection.addToShot,
   299→      onAddToShotWithoutPosition: shotSelection.lightboxShotState.isExternalGen
   300→        ? external.handleExternalGenAddToShotWithoutPosition
   301→        : shotSelection.addToShotWithoutPosition,
   302→      onCreateShot,
   303→      positionedInSelectedShot: shotSelection.lightboxShotState.positionedInSelectedShot,
   304→      associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot: shotSelection.lightboxShotState.associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   305→      onDelete: !readOnly
   306→        ? (mediaId: string) => onImageDelete(mediaId)
   307→        : undefined,
   308→      starred: media.currentLightboxImage.starred ?? false,
   309→      onMagicEdit: (_imageUrl, _prompt, _numImages) => {
   310→        // TODO: Implement magic edit generation
   311→      },
   312→      showTaskDetails: true,
   313→      taskDetailsData: {
   314→        task: taskDetails.task ?? null,
   315→        isLoading: taskDetails.isLoadingTask,
   316→        error: taskDetails.taskError,
   317→        inputImages: taskDetails.inputImages,
   318→        taskId: taskDetails.task?.id || null,
   319→        onClose: lightbox.closeLightbox,
   320→      },
   321→      showMagicEdit: true,
   322→      initialEditActive: lightbox.initialEditActive,
   323→    };
   324→  }, [
   325→    allShots,
   326→    external.externalGenLightboxSelectedShot,
   327→    external.handleExternalGenAddToShot,
   328→    external.handleExternalGenAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   329→    external.handleOpenExternalGeneration,
   330→    external.setExternalGenLightboxSelectedShot,
   331→    images.length,
   332→    lightbox.initialEditActive,
   333→    lightbox.capturedVariantIdRef,
   334→    lightbox.closeLightbox,
   335→    lightbox.goNext,
   336→    lightbox.goPrev,
   337→    lightbox.hasNext,
   338→    lightbox.hasPrevious,
   339→    lightbox.openLightbox,
   340→    lightbox.showNavigation,
   341→    media.currentImages,
   342→    media.currentLightboxImage,
   343→    onCreateShot,
   344→    onImageDelete,
   345→    onShotChange,
   346→    readOnly,
   347→    selectedShotId,
   348→    shotId,
   349→    shotSelection.addToShot,
   350→    shotSelection.addToShotWithoutPosition,
   351→    shotSelection.adjacentSegmentsData,
   352→    shotSelection.lightboxSelectedShotId,
   353→    shotSelection.lightboxShotState,
   354→    shotSelection.setLightboxSelectedShotId,
   355→    taskDetails.inputImages,
   356→    taskDetails.isLoadingTask,
   357→    taskDetails.task,
   358→    taskDetails.taskError,
   359→  ]);
   360→
   361→  return (
   362→    <div className="w-full overflow-x-hidden relative" data-tour="timeline">
   363→      {/* Blur and overlay when no images */}
   364→      {hasNoImages && (
   365→        <TimelineEmptyState
   366→          isDragOver={isDragOver}
   367→          dragType={dragType}
   368→          shotId={shotId}
   369→          onImageUpload={onImageUpload}
   370→          isUploadingImage={isUploadingImage}
   371→          onDragEnter={handleEmptyStateDragEnter}
   372→          onDragOver={handleEmptyStateDragOver}
   373→          onDragLeave={handleEmptyStateDragLeave}
   374→          onDrop={handleEmptyStateDrop}
   375→          hasDropHandler={!!(onFileDrop || onGenerationDrop)}
   376→        />
   377→      )}
   378→      
   379→      {/* Timeline Container - includes both controls and timeline */}
   380→      <TimelineContainer
   381→        shotId={shotId}
   382→        projectId={projectId}
   383→        images={images}
   384→        framePositions={displayPositions}
   385→        onResetFrames={handleResetFrames}
   386→        setFramePositions={setFramePositions}
   387→        onImageReorder={onImageReorder}
   388→        onFileDrop={onFileDrop}
   389→        onGenerationDrop={onGenerationDrop}
   390→        setIsDragInProgress={setIsDragInProgress}
   391→        onPairClick={onPairClick}
   392→        pairPrompts={actualPairPrompts}
   393→        defaultPrompt={defaultPrompt}
   394→        defaultNegativePrompt={defaultNegativePrompt}
   395→        onClearEnhancedPrompt={readOnly ? undefined : onClearEnhancedPrompt}
   396→        onImageDelete={onImageDelete}
   397→        onImageDuplicate={onImageDuplicate || (() => {})}
   398→        duplicatingImageId={duplicatingImageId}
   399→        duplicateSuccessImageId={duplicateSuccessImageId}
   400→        projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   401→        handleDesktopDoubleClick={lightbox.handleDesktopDoubleClick}
   402→        handleMobileTap={lightbox.handleMobileTap}
   403→        handleInpaintClick={lightbox.openLightboxWithInpaint}
   404→        hasNoImages={hasNoImages}
   405→        readOnly={readOnly}
   406→        isUploadingImage={isUploadingImage}
   407→        uploadProgress={uploadProgress}
   408→        maxFrameLimit={maxFrameLimit}
   409→        selectedOutputId={selectedOutputId}
   410→        onSegmentFrameCountChange={onSegmentFrameCountChange}
   411→        segmentSlots={segmentSlots}
   412→        isSegmentsLoading={isSegmentsLoading}
   413→        hasPendingTask={hasPendingTask}
   414→        videoOutputs={readOnlyGenerations}
   415→        onNewShotFromSelection={onNewShotFromSelection}
   416→        onShotChange={onShotChange}
   417→        onRegisterTrailingUpdater={onRegisterTrailingUpdater}
   418→      />
   419→
   420→      {/* Lightbox */}
   421→      {timelineLightboxProps && (
   422→        <MediaLightbox {...timelineLightboxProps} />
   423→      )}
   424→    </div>
   425→  );
   426→};
   427→
   428→export default React.memo(Timeline);
   429→
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * Thin wrapper around useTimelinePositions for the timeline drag path.
     3→ * Adds the serialized write queue and the external drag-gesture lock
     4→ * (prevents realtime syncs from jittering positions mid-drag).
     5→ *
     6→ * For batch-mode image reorders, see useTimelineFrameUpdates.
     7→ */
     8→
     9→import { useEffect } from 'react';
    10→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    11→import { useTimelinePositions } from './timeline-core/useTimelinePositions';
    12→import { runSerializedTimelineWrite } from '@/shared/lib/timelineWriteQueue';
    13→
    14→interface PositionManagementProps {
    15→  shotId: string;
    16→  shotGenerations: GenerationRow[];
    17→  frameSpacing: number;
    18→  isDragInProgress: boolean;
    19→  onFramePositionsChange?: (framePositions: Map<string, number>) => void;
    20→}
    21→
    22→export function usePositionManagement({
    23→  shotId,
    24→  shotGenerations,
    25→  frameSpacing,
    26→  isDragInProgress,
    27→  onFramePositionsChange,
    28→}: PositionManagementProps) {
    29→
    30→  const positionSystem = useTimelinePositions({
    31→    shotId,
    32→    shotGenerations,
    33→    frameSpacing,
    34→    onPositionsChange: onFramePositionsChange,
    35→  });
    36→
    37→  // Lock/unlock positions during drag operations.
    38→  // Use the stable callback refs rather than the full positionSystem object —
    39→  // positionSystem is a new object on every render (it contains positions state),
    40→  // so depending on it would fire this effect on every render and call
    41→  // unlockPositions() in the middle of an active save, destroying the lock.
    42→  const { lockPositions, unlockPositions } = positionSystem;
    43→  useEffect(() => {
    44→    if (isDragInProgress) {
    45→      lockPositions();
    46→    } else {
    47→      unlockPositions();
    48→    }
    49→  }, [isDragInProgress, lockPositions, unlockPositions]);
    50→
    51→  const setFramePositions = async (newPositions: Map<string, number>) => {
    52→    // Apply optimistic visual update IMMEDIATELY — before entering the write queue.
    53→    // Without this, the optimistic update was gated behind runSerializedTimelineWrite,
    54→    // so if the queue was blocked by a concurrent batch-editor midpoint RPC (8-12s),
    55→    // the user would see the node snap back instantly with no visual feedback.
    56→    // We pre-apply here and pass the rollback to updatePositions so errors revert.
    57→    const preAppliedRollback = positionSystem.applyOptimisticPositionUpdate(newPositions);
    58→
    59→    await runSerializedTimelineWrite(
    60→      shotId,
    61→      'timeline-drag-positions',
    62→      async () => {
    63→        await positionSystem.updatePositions(newPositions, {
    64→          operation: 'drag',
    65→          skipOptimistic: true,       // already applied above
    66→          externalRollback: preAppliedRollback, // used for rollback on DB error
    67→        });
    68→      },
    69→
    70→    );
    71→  };
    72→
    73→  return {
    74→    displayPositions: positionSystem.positions,
    75→    setFramePositions,
    76→  };
    77→}
    78→
```

```
1→import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     3→import { filterTimelineEligiblePositionedImages } from '@/shared/lib/timelineEligibility';
     4→import { useTimelineCore } from '@/shared/hooks/useTimelineCore';
     5→import { useTimelinePositionUtils } from '../../../../hooks/timeline/useTimelinePositionUtils';
     6→import { usePositionManagement } from '../usePositionManagement';
     7→
     8→interface UseTimelineDomainServiceInput {
     9→  shotId: string;
    10→  projectId?: string;
    11→  frameSpacing: number;
    12→  isDragInProgress: boolean;
    13→  onFramePositionsChange?: (framePositions: Map<string, number>) => void;
    14→  propShotGenerations?: GenerationRow[];
    15→  propImages?: GenerationRow[];
    16→  propAllGenerations?: GenerationRow[];
    17→  readOnly: boolean;
    18→}
    19→
    20→interface UseTimelineDomainServiceResult {
    21→  shotGenerations: GenerationRow[];
    22→  images: GenerationRow[];
    23→  readOnlyGenerations: GenerationRow[] | undefined;
    24→  displayPositions: Map<string, number>;
    25→  setFramePositions: (positions: Map<string, number>) => Promise<void>;
    26→  actualPairPrompts: Record<number, { prompt: string; negativePrompt: string }>;
    27→  loadPositions: (opts?: { silent?: boolean; reason?: string }) => void | Promise<void>;
    28→}
    29→
    30→/**
    31→ * Domain/application service boundary for Timeline data.
    32→ * Consolidates position/query orchestration and keeps Timeline.tsx as view composition.
    33→ */
    34→export function useTimelineDomainService(input: UseTimelineDomainServiceInput): UseTimelineDomainServiceResult {
    35→  const {
    36→    shotId,
    37→    projectId,
    38→    frameSpacing,
    39→    isDragInProgress,
    40→    onFramePositionsChange,
    41→    propShotGenerations,
    42→    propImages,
    43→    propAllGenerations,
    44→    readOnly,
    45→  } = input;
    46→
    47→  const coreHookData = useTimelineCore(!propAllGenerations ? shotId : null);
    48→  const utilsHookData = useTimelinePositionUtils({
    49→    shotId: propAllGenerations ? shotId : null,
    50→    generations: propAllGenerations || [],
    51→    projectId,
    52→  });
    53→
    54→  const shotGenerations: GenerationRow[] = propShotGenerations
    55→    || (propAllGenerations ? propAllGenerations : coreHookData.positionedItems);
    56→
    57→  const loadPositions = propAllGenerations
    58→    ? utilsHookData.loadPositions
    59→    : async (_opts?: { silent?: boolean; reason?: string }) => {
    60→      coreHookData.refetch();
    61→    };
    62→
    63→  const actualPairPrompts = propAllGenerations ? utilsHookData.pairPrompts : coreHookData.pairPrompts;
    64→
    65→  const images = useMemo(() => {
    66→    let result: GenerationRow[] = propImages ?? shotGenerations;
    67→    result = filterTimelineEligiblePositionedImages(result);
    68→
    69→    result = result.sort((a, b) => {
    70→      const frameDiff = (a.timeline_frame ?? 0) - (b.timeline_frame ?? 0);
    71→      if (frameDiff !== 0) return frameDiff;
    72→      return String(a.id ?? '').localeCompare(String(b.id ?? ''));
    73→    });
    74→
    75→    return result;
    76→  }, [shotGenerations, propImages]);
    77→
    78→  const readOnlyGenerations = readOnly ? propAllGenerations : undefined;
    79→
    80→  const {
    81→    displayPositions,
    82→    setFramePositions,
    83→  } = usePositionManagement({
    84→    shotId,
    85→    shotGenerations,
    86→    frameSpacing,
    87→    isDragInProgress,
    88→    onFramePositionsChange,
    89→  });
    90→
    91→  return {
    92→    shotGenerations,
    93→    images,
    94→    readOnlyGenerations,
    95→    displayPositions,
    96→    setFramePositions,
    97→    actualPairPrompts,
    98→    loadPositions,
    99→  };
   100→}
   101→
```

Perfect! I have all the information you need. Here's the complete research on the backward-compatible aliases that need to be cleaned up:

## Backward-Compatible Aliases in `useTimelinePositions.ts`

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/timeline-core/useTimelinePositions.ts`

### Compat Alias Definitions (lines 101-104 and 707-709)

```typescript
// Lines 101-104 (in interface)
// For backwards compatibility during migration
displayPositions: Map<string, number>;
framePositions: Map<string, number>;
setFramePositions: (positions: Map<string, number>) => Promise<void>;

// Lines 707-709 (in return statement)
// Backwards compatibility
displayPositions: positions,
framePositions: positions,
setFramePositions,
```

### Canonical Names

- **`displayPositions`** → canonical name is **`positions`** (the main state)
- **`framePositions`** → canonical name is **`positions`** (same as above)
- **`setFramePositions`** → canonical name is **`updatePositions`** (the main function), but with `{ operation: 'drag' }` option

---

## Consumers of These Aliases

### 1. **`Timeline/index.tsx` (line 170)**

```typescript
// Line 170-171 - destructuring from useTimelineDomainService
displayPositions,
setFramePositions,

// Line 384 - usage in props
framePositions={displayPositions}
```

**What to replace:**
- `displayPositions` → rename to `positions`
- `setFramePositions` → rename to `updatePositions`

---

### 2. **`usePositionManagement.ts` (line 74)**

```typescript
// Line 74 - return statement destructuring
displayPositions: positionSystem.positions,
setFramePositions,
```

**What to replace:**
- Already correctly accessing `positionSystem.positions` directly and exposing it as `displayPositions` in return
- The `setFramePositions` wrapper (lines 51-71) is a custom wrapper around `positionSystem.updatePositions()` that:
  1. Pre-applies optimistic updates
  2. Queues writes with `runSerializedTimelineWrite()`
  
When removing the alias, this wrapper logic should stay but be renamed to `updatePositions`.

---

### 3. **`useTimelineDomainService.ts` (lines 81-82)**

```typescript
// Lines 80-89 - destructuring from usePositionManagement
const {
  displayPositions,
  setFramePositions,
} = usePositionManagement({
  ...
});

// Lines 95-96 - return statement
displayPositions,
setFramePositions,
```

**What to replace:**
- Rename `displayPositions` → `positions`
- Rename `setFramePositions` → `updatePositions`

---

## Summary of Changes Needed

1. **In `useTimelinePositions.ts`:**
   - Remove the compat aliases from the return interface definition
   - Remove `displayPositions`, `framePositions`, `setFramePositions` from the return object (lines 707-709)

2. **In `usePositionManagement.ts`:**
   - Return object should expose `positions` instead of `displayPositions`
   - Return object should expose `updatePositions` instead of `setFramePositions`

3. **In `useTimelineDomainService.ts`:**
   - Destructure `positions` instead of `displayPositions` from `usePositionManagement`
   - Destructure `updatePositions` instead of `setFramePositions`
   - Return `positions` and `updatePositions` in the result object

4. **In `Timeline/index.tsx`:**
   - Destructure `positions` instead of `displayPositions`
   - Destructure `updatePositions` instead of `setFramePositions`
   - Pass `positions` as the `framePositions` prop to `TimelineContainer` (line 384)
   - Pass `updatePositions` as the `setFramePositions` prop to `TimelineContainer` (line 386)

The `plan resolve` command would trigger review module imports, causing `ImportError: cannot import name '_do_run_batches'` or `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'desloppify.app.commands.review.importing.assessment_integrity'` or `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'desloppify.engine._plan.epic_triage_apply'`.

   - **Stash auto-reapplication**: Even after dropping stash@{0}, the desloppify source kept getting dirtied by the CLI's resolve path. This was because stash pop had left partially applied changes in the index that `git checkout -- .` couldn't fully clean.

   - **Queue ordering enforcement**: Attempted to resolve items out of order, got error "Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue"

```
    79→function useSegmentController(
    80→  props: ShotImageManagerProps,
    81→  currentImages: GenerationRow[],
    82→): SegmentController {
    83→  const localShotGenPositions = useMemo(() => {
    84→    if (props.generationMode === 'timeline') {
    85→      return undefined;
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    const orderedImages = currentImages.filter(
    89→      (image) => isPositioned(image) && !isVideoGeneration(image)
    90→    );
    91→    if (orderedImages.length === 0) {
    92→      return undefined;
    93→    }
    94→
    95→    const positions = new Map<string, number>();
    96→    orderedImages.forEach((image, index) => {
    97→      if (image.id) {
    98→        positions.set(image.id, index);
    99→      }
   100→    });
   101→    return positions;
   102→  }, [currentImages, props.generationMode]);
   103→
   104→  const shouldFetchSegments = props.generationMode !== 'timeline' &&
   105→    (!props.segmentSlots || (localShotGenPositions && localShotGenPositions.size > 0));
   106→
   107→  const hookResult = useSegmentOutputsForShot(
   108→    shouldFetchSegments ? props.shotId || null : null,
   109→    shouldFetchSegments ? props.projectId || null : null,
   110→    shouldFetchSegments ? localShotGenPositions : undefined
   111→  );
   112→
   113→  const segmentSlots = (localShotGenPositions && localShotGenPositions.size > 0)
   114→    ? (hookResult.segmentSlots.length > 0 ? hookResult.segmentSlots : props.segmentSlots ?? [])
   115→    : (props.segmentSlots ?? hookResult.segmentSlots);
   116→
   117→  const selectedParentId = hookResult.selectedParentId;
   118→  const { hasPendingTask } = usePendingSegmentTasks(
   119→    props.generationMode !== 'timeline' ? props.shotId || null : null,
   120→    props.generationMode !== 'timeline' ? props.projectId || null : null
   121→  );
   122→
   123→  const segmentLightbox = useSegmentLightboxState(segmentSlots, props.onPairClick);
   124→
   125→  return {
   126→    segmentSlots,
   127→    selectedParentId,
   128→    hasPendingTask,
   129→    segmentLightbox,
   130→  };
   131→}
   132→
   133→function useSegmentLightboxState(
   134→  segmentSlots: SegmentSlot[],
   135→  onPairClick?: ShotImageManagerProps['onPairClick']
   136→): SegmentLightboxState {
   137→  const [segmentLightboxIndex, setSegmentLightboxIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
   138→
   139→  const handleSegmentClick = useCallback((slotIndex: number) => {
   140→    const slot = segmentSlots[slotIndex];
   141→    if (onPairClick && slot) {
   142→      onPairClick(slot.index);
   143→      return;
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    setSegmentLightboxIndex(slotIndex);
   147→  }, [onPairClick, segmentSlots]);
   148→
   149→  const currentSegmentSlot = segmentLightboxIndex !== null ? segmentSlots[segmentLightboxIndex] : null;
   150→  const currentSegmentMedia = currentSegmentSlot?.type === 'child'
   151→    ? currentSegmentSlot.child
   152→    : null;
   153→
   154→  const segmentChildSlotIndices = useMemo(
   155→    () =>
   156→      segmentSlots
   157→        .map((slot, idx) => (slot.type === 'child' && slot.child.location ? idx : null))
   158→        .filter((idx): idx is number => idx !== null),
   159→    [segmentSlots]
   160→  );
   161→
   162→  const handleSegmentLightboxNext = useCallback(() => {
   163→    if (segmentLightboxIndex === null || segmentChildSlotIndices.length === 0) {
   164→      return;
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    const currentPos = segmentChildSlotIndices.indexOf(segmentLightboxIndex);
   168→    const nextPos = (currentPos + 1) % segmentChildSlotIndices.length;
   169→    setSegmentLightboxIndex(segmentChildSlotIndices[nextPos]);
   170→  }, [segmentChildSlotIndices, segmentLightboxIndex]);
   171→
   172→  const handleSegmentLightboxPrev = useCallback(() => {
   173→    if (segmentLightboxIndex === null || segmentChildSlotIndices.length === 0) {
   174→      return;
   175→    }
   176→
   177→    const currentPos = segmentChildSlotIndices.indexOf(segmentLightboxIndex);
   178→    const prevPos = (currentPos - 1 + segmentChildSlotIndices.length) % segmentChildSlotIndices.length;
   179→    setSegmentLightboxIndex(segmentChildSlotIndices[prevPos]);
   180→  }, [segmentChildSlotIndices, segmentLightboxIndex]);
   181→
   182→  const closeSegmentLightbox = useCallback(() => {
   183→    setSegmentLightboxIndex(null);
   184→  }, []);
   185→
   186→  return {
   187→    segmentLightboxIndex,
   188→    currentSegmentSlot,
   189→    currentSegmentMedia,
   190→    segmentChildSlotIndices,
   191→    handleSegmentClick,
   192→    handleSegmentLightboxNext,
   193→    handleSegmentLightboxPrev,
   194→    closeSegmentLightbox,
   195→  };
   196→}
```

```
    49→
    50→  // Queue for sequential frame extraction (prevents race conditions)
    51→  const extractionQueueRef = useRef<Array<{ time: number; callback: (frame: string | null) => void }>>([]);
    52→  const isExtractingRef = useRef(false);
    53→
    54→  // Handle video metadata loaded
    55→  const handleVideoLoaded = useCallback(() => {
    56→    setIsVideoReady(true);
    57→  }, []);
    58→
    59→  // Reset video ready state when URL changes
    60→  useEffect(() => {
    61→    setIsVideoReady(false);
    62→    setStartFrame(null);
    63→    setEndFrame(null);
    64→    // Clear any pending extractions
    65→    extractionQueueRef.current = [];
    66→    isExtractingRef.current = false;
    67→  }, [videoUrl]);
    68→
    69→  // Process extraction queue sequentially
    70→  const processExtractionQueue = useCallback(async () => {
    71→    if (isExtractingRef.current || extractionQueueRef.current.length === 0) return;
    72→    if (!frameExtractionVideoRef.current || !canvasRef.current) return;
    73→
    74→    isExtractingRef.current = true;
    75→    const { time, callback } = extractionQueueRef.current.shift()!;
    76→
    77→    const video = frameExtractionVideoRef.current;
    78→    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    79→    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    80→
    81→    if (!ctx) {
    82→      callback(null);
    83→      isExtractingRef.current = false;
    84→      processExtractionQueue();
    85→      return;
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Validate time is finite and within video duration
    89→    if (!Number.isFinite(time) || time < 0) {
    90→      callback(null);
    91→      isExtractingRef.current = false;
    92→      processExtractionQueue();
    93→      return;
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    // Clamp time to video duration
    97→    const clampedTime = Math.min(Math.max(0.001, time), video.duration - 0.01);
    98→
    99→    if (!Number.isFinite(clampedTime)) {
   100→      callback(null);
   101→      isExtractingRef.current = false;
   102→      processExtractionQueue();
   103→      return;
   104→    }
   105→
   106→    const handleSeeked = () => {
   107→      video.removeEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
   108→
   109→      // Set canvas size to match video aspect ratio
   110→      const aspectRatio = video.videoWidth / video.videoHeight;
   111→      if (!Number.isFinite(aspectRatio) || aspectRatio <= 0) {
   112→        callback(null);
   113→        isExtractingRef.current = false;
   114→        processExtractionQueue();
   115→        return;
   116→      }
   117→
   118→      canvas.width = 160;
   119→      canvas.height = Math.round(160 / aspectRatio);
   120→
   121→      ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
   122→      const frame = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
   123→      callback(frame);
   124→      isExtractingRef.current = false;
   125→      // Process next in queue
   126→      processExtractionQueue();
   127→    };
   128→
   129→    video.addEventListener('seeked', handleSeeked);
   130→    video.currentTime = clampedTime;
   131→  }, []);
   132→
   133→  // Queue a frame extraction (returns via callback to ensure sequential processing)
   134→  const queueFrameExtraction = useCallback((time: number, callback: (frame: string | null) => void) => {
   135→    extractionQueueRef.current.push({ time, callback });
   136→    processExtractionQueue();
   137→  }, [processExtractionQueue]);
   138→
   139→  // Throttle refs for live updates while dragging
   140→  const lastStartUpdateRef = useRef<number>(0);
   141→  const lastEndUpdateRef = useRef<number>(0);
   142→  const pendingStartUpdateRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   143→  const pendingEndUpdateRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
   144→  const prevStartTrimRef = useRef<number>(trimState.startTrim);
   145→  const prevEndTrimRef = useRef<number>(trimState.endTrim);
   146→  const initialExtractionDoneRef = useRef(false);
   147→
   148→  // Initial extraction - extract both frames sequentially when video becomes ready

   150→    if (!videoUrl || !isVideoReady || trimState.videoDuration === 0) return;
   151→    if (initialExtractionDoneRef.current) return;
   152→
   153→    initialExtractionDoneRef.current = true;
   154→
   155→    // Queue start frame first, then end frame (sequential processing)
   156→    const startTime = trimState.startTrim;
   157→    const endTime = Math.max(0.001, trimState.videoDuration - trimState.endTrim - 0.1);
   158→
   159→    if (Number.isFinite(startTime)) {
   160→      queueFrameExtraction(startTime, (frame) => {
   161→        setStartFrame(frame);
   162→        lastStartUpdateRef.current = Date.now();
   163→      });
   164→    }
   165→
   166→    if (Number.isFinite(endTime)) {
   167→      queueFrameExtraction(endTime, (frame) => {
   168→        setEndFrame(frame);
   169→        lastEndUpdateRef.current = Date.now();
   170→      });
   171→    }
   172→  }, [videoUrl, isVideoReady, trimState.videoDuration, trimState.startTrim, trimState.endTrim, queueFrameExtraction]);
   173→
   174→  // Reset initial extraction flag when URL changes
   175→  useEffect(() => {
   176→    initialExtractionDoneRef.current = false;
   177→  }, [videoUrl]);
   178→
   179→  // Update START frame when startTrim changes (after initial extraction)
   180→  useEffect(() => {
   181→    if (!videoUrl || !isVideoReady || trimState.videoDuration === 0) return;
   182→    if (!initialExtractionDoneRef.current) return; // Wait for initial extraction
   183→
   184→    // Skip if startTrim hasn't actually changed
   185→    if (prevStartTrimRef.current === trimState.startTrim) return;
   186→    prevStartTrimRef.current = trimState.startTrim;
   187→
   188→    const updateStartFrame = () => {
   189→      const keepStartTime = trimState.startTrim;
   190→
   191→      if (!Number.isFinite(keepStartTime)) return;
   192→
   193→      queueFrameExtraction(keepStartTime, (frame) => {
   194→        setStartFrame(frame);

   196→      });
   197→    };
   198→
   199→    // Clear any pending update
   200→    if (pendingStartUpdateRef.current) {
   201→      clearTimeout(pendingStartUpdateRef.current);
   202→      pendingStartUpdateRef.current = null;
   203→    }
   204→
   205→    const now = Date.now();
   206→    const timeSinceLastUpdate = now - lastStartUpdateRef.current;
   207→    const throttleMs = 60;
   208→
   209→    if (timeSinceLastUpdate >= throttleMs) {
   210→      updateStartFrame();
   211→    } else {
   212→      pendingStartUpdateRef.current = setTimeout(updateStartFrame, throttleMs - timeSinceLastUpdate);
   213→    }
   214→
   215→    return () => {
   216→      if (pendingStartUpdateRef.current) {
   217→        clearTimeout(pendingStartUpdateRef.current);
   218→        pendingStartUpdateRef.current = null;
   219→      }
   220→    };
   221→  }, [videoUrl, isVideoReady, trimState.startTrim, trimState.videoDuration, queueFrameExtraction]);
   222→
   223→  // Update END frame when endTrim changes (after initial extraction)
   224→  useEffect(() => {

   228→    // Skip if endTrim hasn't actually changed

   235→      if (!Number.isFinite(keepEndTime)) return;

   237→      queueFrameExtraction(Math.max(0.001, keepEndTime - 0.1), (frame) => {
   238→        setEndFrame(frame);
   239→        lastEndUpdateRef.current = Date.now();
   240→      });
   241→    };
   242→
   243→    // Clear any pending update
   244→    if (pendingEndUpdateRef.current) {
   245→      clearTimeout(pendingEndUpdateRef.current);
   246→      pendingEndUpdateRef.current = null;
   247→    }
   248→
   249→    const now = Date.now();
   250→    const timeSinceLastUpdate = now - lastEndUpdateRef.current;
   251→    const throttleMs = 60;
   252→
   253→    if (timeSinceLastUpdate >= throttleMs) {
   254→      updateEndFrame();
   255→    } else {
   256→      pendingEndUpdateRef.current = setTimeout(updateEndFrame, throttleMs - timeSinceLastUpdate);
   257→    }
   258→
   259→    return () => {

   264→    };
   265→  }, [videoUrl, isVideoReady, trimState.endTrim, trimState.videoDuration, queueFrameExtraction]);
```
