```
   102→  //                       Uses hasLiveTrailingVideo (segment slots + live imagePositions).
   103→  //
   104→  // The split exists because the orchestrator computes fullMax before we have
   105→  // live drag positions. We tell it to reserve space proactively (small cost:
   106→  // 17 extra frames when any video exists), then do precise detection after.
   107→  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   108→
   109→  const trailingEndFrame = framePositions.get(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
   110→
   111→  const handleTrailingEndFrameChange = useCallback((endFrame: number | undefined) => {
   112→    const next = new Map(framePositions);
   113→    if (endFrame === undefined) {
   114→      next.delete(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
   115→    } else {
   116→      next.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, endFrame);
   117→    }
   118→    setFramePositions(next);
   119→  }, [framePositions, setFramePositions]);
   120→
   121→  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   122→    onRegisterTrailingUpdater?.(handleTrailingEndFrameChange);
   123→  }, [onRegisterTrailingUpdater, handleTrailingEndFrameChange]);
   124→
   125→  // ReadOnly path: detect trailing video synchronously from preloaded generations.
   126→  // In editing mode videoOutputs is undefined — this returns { false, null }.
   127→  const { hasTrailing: hasExistingTrailingVideo, videoUrl: computedTrailingVideoUrl } = useMemo(() => {
   128→    if (!videoOutputs || videoOutputs.length === 0 || images.length === 0) {
   129→      return { hasTrailing: false, videoUrl: null };
   130→    }
   131→    const sortedEntries = [...framePositions.entries()]
   132→      .filter(([id]) => id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY)
   133→      .sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
   134→    const lastImageShotGenId = sortedEntries[sortedEntries.length - 1]?.[0] || null;
   135→    return findTrailingVideoInfo(videoOutputs, lastImageShotGenId);
   136→  }, [videoOutputs, framePositions, images.length]);
   137→
   138→  // Async path: strip reports trailing video URL via callback once the slot is visible.
   139→  // Safety net when parentSegmentSlots (React Query) hasn't refreshed yet.
   140→  const [callbackTrailingVideoUrl, setCallbackTrailingVideoUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
   141→  const hasCallbackTrailingVideo = hasExistingTrailingVideo || !!callbackTrailingVideoUrl;
   142→
   143→  // PRE-ORCHESTRATOR: does ANY image have a completed video segment?
   144→  // Proactive reservation — small cost (17 frames padding) eliminates chicken-and-egg.
   145→  const anyImageHasVideo = useMemo(() => {
   146→    if (parentSegmentSlots?.length) {
   147→      return parentSegmentSlots.some(slot =>
   148→        slot.type === 'child' &&
   149→        slot.child.type?.includes('video') &&
   150→        slot.child.location
   151→      );
   152→    }
   153→    return false;
   154→  }, [parentSegmentSlots]);
   155→
   156→  // --- Orchestrator hook (grouped domain view-model) ---
   157→  const orchestrator = useTimelineOrchestrator({
   158→    shotId,
   159→    projectId,
   160→    images,
   161→    framePositions,
   162→    setFramePositions,
   163→    onImageReorder,
   164→    onFileDrop,
   165→    onGenerationDrop,
   166→    setIsDragInProgress,
   167→    onImageDuplicate,
   168→    readOnly,
   169→    isUploadingImage,
   170→    maxFrameLimit,
   171→    structureVideos,
   172→    primaryStructureVideoType,
   173→    primaryStructureVideoTreatment,
   174→    primaryStructureVideoMotionStrength,
   175→    onAddStructureVideo,
   176→    onUpdateStructureVideo,
   177→    onPrimaryStructureVideoInputChange,
   178→    hasExistingTrailingVideo: hasCallbackTrailingVideo || anyImageHasVideo,
   179→  });
   180→  const { timelineRef, containerRef } = orchestrator.refs;
   181→  const {
   182→    fullMin,
   183→    fullMax,
   184→    fullRange,
   185→    containerWidth,
   186→    zoomLevel,
   187→    handleZoomInToCenter,
   188→    handleZoomOutFromCenter,
   189→    handleZoomReset,
   190→    handleZoomToStart,
   191→    handleTimelineDoubleClick,
   192→  } = orchestrator.viewport;
   193→  const {
   194→    state: dragState,
   195→    dragOffset,
   196→    currentDragFrame,
   197→    swapTargetId,
   198→    pushMode,
   199→    handleMouseDown,
   200→  } = orchestrator.drag;
   201→  const { selectedIds, showSelectionBar, isSelected, toggleSelection, clearSelection } = orchestrator.selection;
   202→  const {
   203→    pendingDropFrame,
   204→    pendingDuplicateFrame,
   205→    pendingExternalAddFrame,
   206→    activePendingFrame,
   207→    isInternalDropProcessing,
   208→  } = orchestrator.pending;
   209→  const {
   210→    isFileOver,
   211→    dropTargetFrame,
   212→    dragType,
   213→    handleDragEnter,
   214→    handleDragOver,
   215→    handleDragLeave,
   216→    handleDrop,
   217→  } = orchestrator.drop;
   218→  const { currentPositions, pairInfo, pairDataByIndex, localShotGenPositions, showPairLabels } = orchestrator.computed;
   219→  const { handleDuplicateInterceptor, handleTimelineTapToMove, handleVideoBrowserSelect, handleEndpointMouseDown } = orchestrator.actions;
   220→  const { endpointDragFrame, isEndpointDragging: isEndpointDraggingState } = orchestrator.endpoint;
   221→  const {
   222→    resetGap,
   223→    setResetGap,
   224→    maxGap,
   225→    showVideoBrowser,
   226→    setShowVideoBrowser,
   227→    isUploadingStructureVideo,
   228→    setIsUploadingStructureVideo,
   229→  } = orchestrator.uiState;
   230→  const { isMobile, isTablet, enableTapToMove, prefetchTaskData } = orchestrator.device;
   231→
   232→  // --- Post-orchestrator trailing endpoint (needs currentPositions) ---
   233→  const {
   234→    trailingVideoUrl,
   235→    handleExtractFinalFrame,
   236→    imagePositions,
   237→  } = useTrailingEndpoint({
   238→    currentPositions,
   239→    trailingEndFrame,
   240→    computedTrailingVideoUrl,
   241→    callbackTrailingVideoUrl,
   242→    setCallbackTrailingVideoUrl,
   243→    onFileDrop,
   244→  });
   245→
   246→  // Live trailing detection: check if any completed video is anchored to the current
   247→  // live last image. Auto-reveals trailing slot during drag without waiting for DB.
   248→  const liveLastImageShotGenId = useMemo(() => {
   249→    const sorted = [...imagePositions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
   250→    return sorted[sorted.length - 1]?.[0] ?? null;
   251→  }, [imagePositions]);
   252→
   253→  const hasLiveTrailingVideo = useMemo(() => {
   254→    if (!liveLastImageShotGenId) return false;
   255→    // In editing mode, videoOutputs is undefined — use parentSegmentSlots instead.
   256→    // Segment slots know which image each video is anchored to (pairShotGenerationId).
   257→    if (parentSegmentSlots?.length) {
   258→      return parentSegmentSlots.some(slot =>
   259→        slot.type === 'child' &&
   260→        slot.pairShotGenerationId === liveLastImageShotGenId &&
   261→        slot.child.type?.includes('video') &&
   262→        slot.child.location
   263→      );
   264→    }
   265→    // Fallback for readOnly mode where videoOutputs is available
   266→    if (videoOutputs) {
   267→      return findTrailingVideoInfo(videoOutputs, liveLastImageShotGenId).hasTrailing;
   268→    }
   269→    return false;
   270→  }, [liveLastImageShotGenId, parentSegmentSlots, videoOutputs]);
   271→
   272→  // --- Derived pair data (augmented with pending items) ---
   273→  const imagePositionsWithPending = useMemo(() => {
   274→    if (activePendingFrame === null) return imagePositions;
   275→    const realItemAtFrame = [...imagePositions.values()].some(pos => pos === activePendingFrame);
   276→    if (realItemAtFrame) return imagePositions;
   277→    const augmented = new Map(imagePositions);
   278→    augmented.set(PENDING_POSITION_KEY, activePendingFrame);
   279→    return augmented;
   280→  }, [imagePositions, activePendingFrame]);
   281→
   282→  const pairInfoWithPending = useMemo(() => {
   283→    if (activePendingFrame === null) return pairInfo;
   284→    return getPairInfo(imagePositionsWithPending);
   285→  }, [pairInfo, activePendingFrame, imagePositionsWithPending]);
   286→
   287→  const numPairs = Math.max(0, images.length - 1);
   288→  const maxAllowedGap = 81;
   289→
   290→  // --- Pair settings handler ---
   291→  const { handleOpenPairSettings } = usePairSettingsHandler({
   292→    images,
   293→    imagePositions,
   294→    trailingEndFrame,
   295→    pairInfo,
   296→    pairDataByIndex,
   297→    onPairClick,
   298→  });
   299→
   300→  // --- Simple handlers ---
   301→  const handleReset = useCallback(() => {
   302→    if (images.length === 1) {
   303→      const newPositions = new Map<string, number>();
   304→      newPositions.set(images[0].id, 0);
   305→      newPositions.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, resetGap);
   306→      setFramePositions(newPositions);
   307→    } else {
   308→      onResetFrames(resetGap);
   309→    }
   310→  }, [onResetFrames, resetGap, images, setFramePositions]);
   311→
   312→  const handleDeleteSelected = useCallback(() => {
   313→    selectedIds.forEach(id => onImageDelete(id));
   314→    clearSelection();
   315→  }, [selectedIds, onImageDelete, clearSelection]);
   316→
   317→  const handleNewShotFromSelection = useCallback(async () => {
   318→    if (!onNewShotFromSelection) return;
   319→    const newShotId = await onNewShotFromSelection(selectedIds);
   320→    return newShotId;
   321→  }, [onNewShotFromSelection, selectedIds]);
   322→
   323→  // --- JSX ---
   324→  return (
   325→    <div className="w-full overflow-x-hidden relative">
   326→      <div className="relative">
   327→        <TimelineControls
   328→          timeline={{
   329→            shotId,
   330→            projectId,
   331→            readOnly,
   332→            hasNoImages,
   333→            zoomLevel,
   334→            fullMax,
   335→            showDragHint: !!(dragState.isDragging && dragState.activeId && !isMobile),
   336→          }}
   337→          audio={{
   338→            audioUrl,
   339→            onAudioChange,
   340→          }}
   341→          guidance={{
   342→            primaryStructureVideoPath,
   343→            primaryStructureVideoType,
   344→            primaryStructureVideoTreatment,
   345→            primaryStructureVideoMotionStrength,
   346→            structureVideos,
   347→            onAddStructureVideo,
   348→            onUpdateStructureVideo,
   349→            onPrimaryStructureVideoInputChange,
   350→            onShowVideoBrowser: () => setShowVideoBrowser(true),
   351→            isUploadingStructureVideo,
   352→            setIsUploadingStructureVideo,
   353→          }}
   354→          zoom={{
   355→            onZoomIn: handleZoomInToCenter,
   356→            onZoomOut: handleZoomOutFromCenter,
   357→            onZoomReset: handleZoomReset,
   358→            onZoomToStart: handleZoomToStart,
   359→          }}
   360→          bottom={{
   361→            resetGap,
   362→            setResetGap,
   363→            maxGap,
   364→            onReset: handleReset,
   365→            onFileDrop,
   366→            isUploadingImage,
   367→            uploadProgress,
   368→            pushMode,
   369→          }}
   370→        />
   371→
   372→        <TimelineTrack
   373→          timelineRef={timelineRef}
   374→          containerRef={containerRef}
   375→          zoomLevel={zoomLevel}
   376→          isFileOver={isFileOver}
   377→          hasNoImages={hasNoImages}
   378→          enableTapToMove={enableTapToMove}
   379→          selectedCount={selectedIds.length}
   380→          onDragEnter={handleDragEnter}
   381→          onDragOver={handleDragOver}
   382→          onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
   383→          onDrop={handleDrop}
   384→          onContainerDoubleClick={handleTimelineDoubleClick}
   385→          onContainerClick={(e) => {
   386→            const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
   387→            if (!target.closest('[data-item-id]') && !target.closest('button')) {
   388→              if (enableTapToMove && selectedIds.length > 0) {
   389→                e.preventDefault();
   390→                e.stopPropagation();
   391→                handleTimelineTapToMove(e.clientX);
   392→                return;
   393→              }
   394→              if (selectedIds.length > 0) clearSelection();
   395→            }
   396→          }}
   397→          containerWidth={containerWidth}
   398→          prelude={(
   399→            <>
   400→              {/* Segment output strip */}
   401→              {shotId && (projectId || (readOnly && videoOutputs)) && (() => {
   402→            const sortedEntries = imagePositions.size > 0
   403→              ? [...imagePositions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1])
   404→              : [];
   405→            const lastEntry = sortedEntries[sortedEntries.length - 1];
   406→            const isMultiImage = images.length > 1;
   407→            const realLastFrame = lastEntry ? lastEntry[1] : undefined;
   408→            const pendingIsLast = activePendingFrame !== null &&
   409→              realLastFrame !== undefined &&
   410→              activePendingFrame > realLastFrame;
   411→            const effectiveLastFrame = pendingIsLast ? activePendingFrame : realLastFrame;
   412→
   413→            return (
   414→              <SegmentOutputStrip
   415→                shotId={shotId}
   416→                projectId={projectId}
   417→                readOnly={readOnly}
   418→                projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   419→                pairInfo={pairInfoWithPending}
   420→                fullMin={fullMin}
   421→                fullMax={fullMax}
   422→                fullRange={fullRange}
   423→                containerWidth={containerWidth}
   424→                zoomLevel={zoomLevel}
   425→                segmentSlots={parentSegmentSlots ?? []}
   426→                isLoading={isSegmentsLoading}
   427→                localShotGenPositions={localShotGenPositions}
   428→                hasPendingTask={parentHasPendingTask}
   429→                pairDataByIndex={pairDataByIndex}
   430→                onOpenPairSettings={onPairClick ? handleOpenPairSettings : undefined}
   431→                selectedParentId={selectedOutputId}
   432→                onSegmentFrameCountChange={onSegmentFrameCountChange}
   433→                trailingSegmentMode={lastEntry && (trailingEndFrame !== undefined || hasCallbackTrailingVideo || hasLiveTrailingVideo) ? (() => {
   434→                  const [imageId, imageFrame] = lastEntry;
   435→                  const resolvedEndFrame = trailingEndFrame ?? (imageFrame + (isMultiImage ? 17 : 49));
   436→                  const trailingDuration = resolvedEndFrame - imageFrame;
   437→                  const effectiveImageId = pendingIsLast ? PENDING_POSITION_KEY : imageId;
   438→                  const effectiveImageFrame = pendingIsLast ? activePendingFrame! : imageFrame;
   439→                  const effectiveEndFrame = pendingIsLast ? activePendingFrame! + trailingDuration : resolvedEndFrame;
   440→                  return { imageId: effectiveImageId, imageFrame: effectiveImageFrame, endFrame: effectiveEndFrame };
   441→                })() : undefined}
   442→                isMultiImage={isMultiImage}
   443→                lastImageFrame={effectiveLastFrame}
   444→                onAddTrailingSegment={realLastFrame !== undefined && lastEntry ? () => {
   445→                  handleTrailingEndFrameChange(realLastFrame + 17);
   446→                } : undefined}
   447→                onRemoveTrailingSegment={isMultiImage && trailingEndFrame !== undefined ? () => {
   448→                  handleTrailingEndFrameChange(undefined);
   449→                } : undefined}
   450→                onTrailingVideoInfo={setCallbackTrailingVideoUrl}
   451→              />
   452→                );
   453→              })()}
   454→
   455→              {/* Structure video strip(s) */}
   456→              {shotId && (projectId || readOnly) && (
   457→                structureVideos && onUpdateStructureVideo && onRemoveStructureVideo ? (
   458→                  <GuidanceVideosContainer
   459→                    structureVideos={structureVideos}
   460→                    isLoading={isStructureVideoLoading}
   461→                    cachedHasStructureVideo={cachedHasStructureVideo}
   462→                    shotId={shotId}
   463→                    onUpdateVideo={onUpdateStructureVideo}
   464→                    onRemoveVideo={onRemoveStructureVideo}
   465→                    fullMin={fullMin}
   466→                    fullMax={fullMax}
   467→                    fullRange={fullRange}
   468→                    containerWidth={containerWidth}
   469→                    zoomLevel={zoomLevel}
   470→                    timelineFrameCount={images.length}
   471→                    readOnly={readOnly}
   472→                  />
   473→                ) : onPrimaryStructureVideoInputChange && (
   474→                  primaryStructureVideoPath ? (
   475→                    <GuidanceVideoStrip
   476→                      videoUrl={primaryStructureVideoPath}
   477→                      videoMetadata={primaryStructureVideoMetadata || null}
   478→                      treatment={primaryStructureVideoTreatment}
   479→                      onTreatmentChange={(treatment) => onPrimaryStructureVideoInputChange(primaryStructureVideoPath, primaryStructureVideoMetadata ?? null, treatment, primaryStructureVideoMotionStrength, primaryStructureVideoType)}
   480→                      onRemove={() => onPrimaryStructureVideoInputChange(null, null, 'adjust', 1.0, 'flow')}
   481→                      onMetadataExtracted={(metadata) => onPrimaryStructureVideoInputChange(primaryStructureVideoPath, metadata, primaryStructureVideoTreatment, primaryStructureVideoMotionStrength, primaryStructureVideoType)}
   482→                      fullMin={fullMin}
   483→                      fullMax={fullMax}
   484→                      fullRange={fullRange}
   485→                      containerWidth={containerWidth}
   486→                      zoomLevel={zoomLevel}
   487→                      timelineFrameCount={images.length}
   488→                      readOnly={readOnly}
   489→                    />
   490→                  ) : isUploadingStructureVideo ? (
   491→                    <div className="relative h-28 -mt-1 mb-3" style={{ width: zoomLevel > 1 ? `${zoomLevel * 100}%` : '100%', minWidth: '100%' }}>
   492→                      <div className="absolute left-4 right-4 top-6 bottom-2 flex items-center justify-center bg-muted/50 dark:bg-muted-foreground/15 border border-border/30 rounded-sm">
   493→                        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-medium">Uploading video...</span>
   494→                      </div>
   495→                    </div>
   496→                  ) : !readOnly ? (
   497→                    <GuidanceVideoUploader
   498→                      shotId={shotId}
   499→                      projectId={projectId ?? ''}
   500→                      onVideoUploaded={(videoUrl, metadata) => {
   501→                        if (videoUrl && metadata) onPrimaryStructureVideoInputChange(videoUrl, metadata, primaryStructureVideoTreatment, primaryStructureVideoMotionStrength, primaryStructureVideoType);
   502→                      }}
   503→                      currentVideoUrl={primaryStructureVideoPath ?? null}
   504→                      compact={false}
   505→                      zoomLevel={zoomLevel}
   506→                      onZoomIn={handleZoomInToCenter}
   507→                      onZoomOut={handleZoomOutFromCenter}
   508→                      onZoomReset={handleZoomReset}
   509→                      onZoomToStart={handleZoomToStart}
   510→                      hasNoImages={hasNoImages}
   511→                    />
   512→                  ) : null
   513→                )
   514→              )}
   515→
   516→              {/* Audio strip */}
   517→              {onAudioChange && audioUrl && (
   518→                <div className="mt-1 mb-2">
   519→                  <AudioStrip
   520→                    audioUrl={audioUrl}
   521→                    audioMetadata={audioMetadata || null}
   522→                    onRemove={() => onAudioChange(null, null)}
   523→                    fullMin={fullMin}
   524→                    fullMax={fullMax}
   525→                    fullRange={fullRange}
   526→                    containerWidth={containerWidth}
   527→                    zoomLevel={zoomLevel}
   528→                    readOnly={readOnly}
   529→                    compact={!!primaryStructureVideoPath}
   530→                  />
   531→                </div>
   532→              )}
   533→            </>
   534→          )}
   535→        >
   536→            <DragLayer
   537→              isFileOver={isFileOver}
   538→              dropTargetFrame={dropTargetFrame}
   539→              fullMin={fullMin}
   540→              fullRange={fullRange}
   541→              containerWidth={containerWidth}
   542→              dragType={dragType}
   543→              activePendingFrame={activePendingFrame}
   544→              pendingDropFrame={pendingDropFrame}
   545→              pendingDuplicateFrame={pendingDuplicateFrame}
   546→              pendingExternalAddFrame={pendingExternalAddFrame}
   547→              isUploadingImage={isUploadingImage}
   548→              isInternalDropProcessing={isInternalDropProcessing}
   549→              projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   550→              imagesLength={images.length}
   551→            />
   552→
   553→            <TimelineRuler
   554→              fullMin={fullMin}
   555→              fullMax={fullMax}
   556→              fullRange={fullRange}
   557→              zoomLevel={zoomLevel}
   558→              containerWidth={containerWidth}
   559→              hasNoImages={hasNoImages}
   560→            />
   561→
   562→            {/* Pair regions */}
   563→            {(() => {
   564→              const sortedDynamicPositions = [...imagePositionsWithPending.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
   565→              return pairInfoWithPending.map((pair, index) => {
   566→              const [startEntry, endEntry] = [sortedDynamicPositions[index], sortedDynamicPositions[index + 1]];
   567→
   568→              const getPixel = (entry: [string, number] | undefined): number => {
   569→                if (!entry) return 0;
   570→                const [, framePos] = entry;
   571→                const effectiveWidth = containerWidth - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
   572→                return TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + ((framePos - fullMin) / fullRange) * effectiveWidth;
   573→              };
   574→
   575→              const startPixel = getPixel(startEntry);
   576→              const endPixel = getPixel(endEntry);
   577→              const actualStartFrame = startEntry?.[1] ?? pair.startFrame;
   578→              const actualEndFrame = endEntry?.[1] ?? pair.endFrame;
   579→              const actualFrames = actualEndFrame - actualStartFrame;
   580→
   581→              const startPercent = (startPixel / containerWidth) * 100;
   582→              const endPercent = (endPixel / containerWidth) * 100;
   583→
   584→              const contextStartFrameUnclipped = actualEndFrame;
   585→              const contextStartFrame = Math.max(0, contextStartFrameUnclipped);
   586→              const visibleContextFrames = Math.max(0, actualEndFrame - contextStartFrame);
   587→
   588→              const effectiveWidth = containerWidth - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
   589→              const contextStartPixel = TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + ((contextStartFrame - fullMin) / fullRange) * effectiveWidth;
   590→              const contextStartPercent = (contextStartPixel / containerWidth) * 100;
   591→
   592→              const generationStartPixel = TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET + ((pair.generationStart - fullMin) / fullRange) * effectiveWidth;
   593→              const generationStartPercent = (generationStartPixel / containerWidth) * 100;
   594→
   595→              const startImage = images.find(img => img.id === startEntry?.[0]);
   596→              const endImage = images.find(img => img.id === endEntry?.[0]);
   597→              const pairPromptData = pairPrompts?.[index];
   598→              const pairPromptFromMetadata = pairPromptData?.prompt || '';
   599→              const pairNegativePromptFromMetadata = pairPromptData?.negativePrompt || '';
   600→              const actualEnhancedPrompt = getEnhancedPromptFromMetadata(startImage?.metadata);
   601→
   602→              return (
   603→                <PairRegion
   604→                  key={`pair-${index}`}
   605→                  index={index}
   606→                  startPercent={startPercent}
   607→                  endPercent={endPercent}
   608→                  contextStartPercent={contextStartPercent}
   609→                  generationStartPercent={generationStartPercent}
   610→                  actualFrames={actualFrames}
   611→                  visibleContextFrames={visibleContextFrames}
   612→                  isDragging={dragState.isDragging}
   613→                  numPairs={numPairs}
   614→                  startFrame={pair.startFrame}
   615→                  endFrame={pair.endFrame}
   616→                  onPairClick={onPairClick ? (pairIndex, pairData) => {
   617→                    onPairClick(pairIndex, {
   618→                      ...pairData,
   619→                      startImage: startImage ? {
   620→                        id: startImage.id,
   621→                        generationId: startImage.generation_id || undefined,
   622→                        primaryVariantId: startImage.primary_variant_id || undefined,
   623→                        url: startImage.imageUrl || startImage.thumbUrl,
   624→                        thumbUrl: startImage.thumbUrl,
   625→                        position: index + 1
   626→                      } : null,
   627→                      endImage: endImage ? {
   628→                        id: endImage.id,
   629→                        generationId: endImage.generation_id || undefined,
   630→                        primaryVariantId: endImage.primary_variant_id || undefined,
   631→                        url: endImage.imageUrl || endImage.thumbUrl,
   632→                        thumbUrl: endImage.thumbUrl,
   633→                        position: index + 2
   634→                      } : null
   635→                    });
   636→                  } : undefined}
   637→                  pairPrompt={pairPromptFromMetadata}
   638→                  pairNegativePrompt={pairNegativePromptFromMetadata}
   639→                  enhancedPrompt={actualEnhancedPrompt}
   640→                  defaultPrompt={defaultPrompt}
   641→                  defaultNegativePrompt={defaultNegativePrompt}
   642→                  showLabel={showPairLabels}
   643→                  hidePairLabel={
   644→                    (enableTapToMove && selectedIds.length > 0) ||
   645→                    (isFileOver && dropTargetFrame !== null && dropTargetFrame > actualStartFrame && dropTargetFrame < actualEndFrame) ||
   646→                    (dragState.isDragging && currentDragFrame !== null && currentDragFrame > actualStartFrame && currentDragFrame < actualEndFrame)
   647→                  }
   648→                  onClearEnhancedPrompt={onClearEnhancedPrompt}
   649→                  readOnly={readOnly}
   650→                />
   651→              );
   652→            });
   653→            })()}
   654→
   655→            {/* Trailing endpoint - appears after last image */}
   656→            {imagePositions.size > 0 && (() => {
   657→              const sortedEntries = [...imagePositions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
   658→              const lastEntry = sortedEntries[sortedEntries.length - 1];
   659→              if (!lastEntry) return null;
   660→              const [id, imageFrame] = lastEntry;
   661→
   662→              const isMultiImage = images.length > 1;
   663→              const hasTrailingSegment = trailingEndFrame !== undefined;
   664→
   665→              if (isMultiImage && !hasTrailingSegment && !hasCallbackTrailingVideo && !hasLiveTrailingVideo) {
   666→                return null;
   667→              }
   668→
   669→              const defaultEndFrame = imageFrame + (isMultiImage ? 17 : 49);
   670→              const effectiveEndFrame = trailingEndFrame ?? defaultEndFrame;
   671→              const gapToImage = effectiveEndFrame - imageFrame;
   672→              const lastImageIndex = sortedEntries.length - 1;
   673→              const lastImage = images.find(img => {
   674→                const imgId = img.shot_generation_id || img.id;
   675→                return imgId === id;
   676→              }) || images[lastImageIndex];
   677→
   678→              const trailingPairIndex = Math.max(0, sortedEntries.length - 1);
   679→
   680→              return (
   681→                <TrailingEndpoint
   682→                  framePosition={effectiveEndFrame}
   683→                  imageFramePosition={imageFrame}
   684→                  isDragging={isEndpointDraggingState}
   685→                  dragOffset={null}
   686→                  onMouseDown={readOnly ? undefined : (e, endpointId) => handleEndpointMouseDown(e, endpointId)}
   687→                  timelineWidth={containerWidth}
   688→                  fullMinFrames={fullMin}
   689→                  fullRange={fullRange}
   690→                  currentDragFrame={isEndpointDraggingState ? endpointDragFrame : null}
   691→                  gapToImage={gapToImage}
   692→                  maxAllowedGap={maxAllowedGap}
   693→                  readOnly={readOnly}
   694→                  compact={isMultiImage}
   695→                  onDurationClick={onPairClick && lastImage ? () => {
   696→                    onPairClick(trailingPairIndex, {
   697→                      index: trailingPairIndex,
   698→                      frames: effectiveEndFrame - imageFrame,
   699→                      startFrame: imageFrame,
   700→                      endFrame: effectiveEndFrame,
   701→                      startImage: {
   702→                        id,
   703→                        generationId: lastImage.generation_id,
   704→                        url: lastImage.imageUrl || lastImage.thumbUrl,
   705→                        thumbUrl: lastImage.thumbUrl,
   706→                        position: sortedEntries.length,
   707→                      },
   708→                      endImage: null,
   709→                    });
   710→                  } : undefined}
   711→                  hasTrailingVideo={!!trailingVideoUrl}
   712→                  onExtractFinalFrame={trailingVideoUrl && onFileDrop ? handleExtractFinalFrame : undefined}
   713→                />
   714→              );
   715→            })()}
   716→
   717→            {/* Timeline items */}
   718→            {images.map((image, idx) => {
   719→              const imageKey = image.id;
   720→              const positionFromMap = currentPositions.get(imageKey);
   721→              const framePosition = positionFromMap ?? image.timeline_frame;
   722→              if (framePosition === undefined || framePosition === null) return null;
   723→              const isDragging = dragState.isDragging && dragState.activeId === imageKey;
   724→
   725→              return (
   726→                <TimelineItem
   727→                  key={imageKey}
   728→                  image={image}
   729→                  framePosition={framePosition}
   730→                  layout={{
   731→                    timelineWidth: containerWidth,
   732→                    fullMinFrames: fullMin,
   733→                    fullRange,
   734→                  }}
   735→                  interaction={{
   736→                    isDragging,
   737→                    isSwapTarget: swapTargetId === imageKey,
   738→                    dragOffset: isDragging ? dragOffset : null,
   739→                    onMouseDown: readOnly ? undefined : (e) => handleMouseDown(e, imageKey, containerRef),
   740→                    onDoubleClick: isMobile && !isTablet ? undefined : () => handleDesktopDoubleClick(idx),
   741→                    onMobileTap: isMobile ? () => handleMobileTap(idx) : undefined,
   742→                    currentDragFrame: isDragging ? currentDragFrame : null,
   743→                    originalFramePos: framePositions.get(imageKey) ?? 0,
   744→                    onPrefetch: !isMobile ? () => {
   745→                      const generationId = getGenerationId(image);
   746→                      if (generationId) prefetchTaskData(generationId);
   747→                    } : undefined,
   748→                  }}
   749→                  actions={{
   750→                    onDelete: onImageDelete,
   751→                    onDuplicate: handleDuplicateInterceptor,
   752→                    onInpaintClick: handleInpaintClick ? () => handleInpaintClick(idx) : undefined,
   753→                    duplicatingImageId: duplicatingImageId ?? undefined,
   754→                    duplicateSuccessImageId: duplicateSuccessImageId ?? undefined,
   755→                  }}
   756→                  selection={{
   757→                    isSelected: isSelected(imageKey),
   758→                    onSelectionClick: readOnly ? undefined : () => toggleSelection(imageKey),
   759→                    selectedCount: selectedIds.length,
   760→                  }}
   761→                  presentation={{
   762→                    projectAspectRatio,
   763→                    readOnly,
   764→                  }}
   765→                />
   766→              );
   767→            })}
   768→        </TimelineTrack>
   769→      </div>
   770→
   771→      {/* Video Browser Modal */}
   772→      <ResourceBrowserModalBase
   773→        isOpen={showVideoBrowser}
   774→        onOpenChange={setShowVideoBrowser}
   775→        resourceType="structure-video"
   776→        title="Browse Guidance Videos"
   777→        onResourceSelect={handleVideoBrowserSelect}
   778→      />
   779→
   780→      {/* Multi-select action bar */}
   781→      {showSelectionBar && selectedIds.length > 0 && !readOnly && (
   782→        <SelectionActionBar
   783→          selectedCount={selectedIds.length}
   784→          onDeselect={clearSelection}
   785→          onDelete={handleDeleteSelected}
   786→          onNewShot={onNewShotFromSelection ? handleNewShotFromSelection : undefined}
   787→          onJumpToShot={onShotChange}
   788→        />
   789→      )}
   790→    </div>
   791→  );
   792→};
   793→
   794→export default React.memo(TimelineContainer);
   795→
```

```
1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
     3→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     4→import { TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY } from '../../utils/timeline-utils';
     5→
     6→interface UseTrailingEndpointProps {
     7→  /** Drag-aware positions from the orchestrator */
     8→  currentPositions: Map<string, number>;
     9→  /** Source-of-truth trailing end frame (derived from framePositions before orchestrator) */
    10→  trailingEndFrame: number | undefined;
    11→  /** Computed trailing video URL (from synchronous computation before orchestrator) */
    12→  computedTrailingVideoUrl: string | null;
    13→  /** Callback-detected trailing video URL (owned by caller, lifted before orchestrator) */
    14→  callbackTrailingVideoUrl: string | null;
    15→  /** Setter for callbackTrailingVideoUrl — caller clears it when last image changes */
    16→  setCallbackTrailingVideoUrl: (url: string | null) => void;
    17→  onFileDrop?: (files: File[], targetFrame?: number) => Promise<void>;
    18→}
    19→
    20→interface UseTrailingEndpointReturn {
    21→  /** URL of the trailing video (computed or callback) */
    22→  trailingVideoUrl: string | null;
    23→  /** Extract the final frame from a trailing video and add as next image */
    24→  handleExtractFinalFrame: () => Promise<void>;
    25→  /** Image-only positions from currentPositions (excluding trailing endpoint) */
    26→  imagePositions: Map<string, number>;
    27→}
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * useTrailingEndpoint - Manages trailing endpoint runtime state:
    31→ * video URL tracking, frame extraction, and image-only positions.
    32→ *
    33→ * Pre-orchestrator values (trailingEndFrame, hasExistingTrailingVideo,
    34→ * handleTrailingEndFrameChange) are computed inline in TimelineContainer
    35→ * since they only depend on framePositions and must be available before
    36→ * the orchestrator hook runs.
    37→ *
    38→ * callbackTrailingVideoUrl is also owned by the caller (before the orchestrator)
    39→ * so it can influence fullMax via hasCallbackTrailingVideo. This hook receives it as
    40→ * a prop and handles the clear-on-last-image-change effect.
    41→ *
    42→ * This hook handles post-orchestrator logic that needs currentPositions:
    43→ * - Image-only positions (excluding trailing endpoint key)
    44→ * - Last-image tracking to clear stale trailing video URLs
    45→ * - Frame extraction from trailing videos
    46→ */
    47→export function useTrailingEndpoint({
    48→  currentPositions,
    49→  trailingEndFrame,
    50→  computedTrailingVideoUrl,
    51→  callbackTrailingVideoUrl,
    52→  setCallbackTrailingVideoUrl,
    53→  onFileDrop,
    54→}: UseTrailingEndpointProps): UseTrailingEndpointReturn {
    55→  const trailingVideoUrl = computedTrailingVideoUrl || callbackTrailingVideoUrl;
    56→
    57→  // Image-only positions (excluding trailing endpoint) for sorts that need the "last image"
    58→  const imagePositions = useMemo(() => {
    59→    const filtered = new Map(currentPositions);
    60→    filtered.delete(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    61→    return filtered;
    62→  }, [currentPositions]);
    63→
    64→  // Track the last image ID to detect when it changes (new image added at end)
    65→  // When the last image changes, clear trailingVideoUrl to prevent showing stale video
    66→  // Note: trailingEndFrame is handled by derivedEndFrame validation (end_frame > lastImageFrame)
    67→  const lastImageIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    68→  useEffect(() => {
    69→    if (imagePositions.size === 0) return;
    70→
    71→    // Find the current last image (highest frame position)
    72→    const sortedEntries = [...imagePositions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    73→    const currentLastImageId = sortedEntries[sortedEntries.length - 1]?.[0] || null;
    74→
    75→    // If the last image changed, clear trailing video URL
    76→    if (lastImageIdRef.current !== null &&
    77→        currentLastImageId !== lastImageIdRef.current) {
    78→      if (trailingVideoUrl) {
    79→        setCallbackTrailingVideoUrl(null);
    80→      }
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    lastImageIdRef.current = currentLastImageId;
    84→  }, [imagePositions, trailingVideoUrl, setCallbackTrailingVideoUrl]);
    85→
    86→  // Handler to extract final frame from trailing video and add as next image
    87→  const handleExtractFinalFrame = useCallback(async () => {
    88→    if (!trailingVideoUrl || !onFileDrop) return;
    89→
    90→    try {
    91→      // Create video element to extract frame
    92→      const video = document.createElement('video');
    93→      video.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    94→      video.muted = true;
    95→      video.playsInline = true;
    96→      video.preload = 'auto';
    97→      video.src = trailingVideoUrl;
    98→
    99→      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
   100→        video.onloadedmetadata = () => resolve();
   101→        video.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to load video'));
   102→        setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('Video load timeout')), 10000);
   103→      });
   104→
   105→      // Seek to the last frame (duration - small epsilon)
   106→      video.currentTime = Math.max(0, video.duration - 0.01);
   107→
   108→      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
   109→        video.onseeked = () => resolve();
   110→        setTimeout(resolve, 2000);
   111→      });
   112→
   113→      // Extract frame to canvas
   114→      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
   115→      canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
   116→      canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
   117→      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
   118→      if (!ctx) throw new Error('Failed to get canvas context');
   119→
   120→      ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
   121→
   122→      // Convert to blob
   123→      const blob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   124→        canvas.toBlob(
   125→          (b) => (b ? resolve(b) : reject(new Error('Failed to create blob'))),
   126→          'image/png',
   127→          1.0
   128→        );
   129→      });
   130→
   131→      // Create File object
   132→      const file = new File([blob], `extracted-frame-${Date.now()}.png`, { type: 'image/png' });
   133→
   134→      // Calculate target frame (after the trailing endpoint)
   135→      const sortedPositions = [...currentPositions.values()].sort((a, b) => a - b);
   136→      const lastImageFrame = sortedPositions[sortedPositions.length - 1] ?? 0;
   137→      const effectiveEndFrame = trailingEndFrame ?? (lastImageFrame + 17);
   138→      const targetFrame = effectiveEndFrame + 5; // Add small gap after endpoint
   139→
   140→      // Drop the file at the target position
   141→      await onFileDrop([file], targetFrame);
   142→
   143→      // Note: The trailing segment state will be automatically cleared by the useEffect
   144→      // that watches for last image changes
   145→
   146→      // Clean up
   147→      video.src = '';
   148→    } catch (error) {
   149→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'extractAndDropFinalFrame' });
   150→      toast.error('Failed to extract frame from video');
   151→    }
   152→  }, [trailingVideoUrl, onFileDrop, currentPositions, trailingEndFrame]);
   153→
   154→  return {
   155→    trailingVideoUrl,
   156→    handleExtractFinalFrame,
   157→    imagePositions,
   158→  };
   159→}
   160→
```

```
1→import { quantizeGap } from "./time-utils";
     2→
     3→// The trailing endpoint key — lives in the positions map but is not a real image
     4→export const [REDACTED];
     5→
     6→// Phantom key for a pending item (duplicate/drop/external add) — used in augmented
     7→// positions maps so pair regions and segment strip can update before the real item arrives
     8→export const PENDING_POSITION_KEY = '__pending__';
     9→
    10→// Minimum gap between frames (4N+1 format, starting at 5)
    11→const QUANTIZE_MIN_GAP = 5;
    12→
    13→/**
    14→ * Quantize all positions to ensure gaps between adjacent items are in 4N+1 format.
    15→ * First item remains at position 0, subsequent items are adjusted to have quantized gaps.
    16→ * The trailing endpoint is extracted before quantizing and re-added after (its gap has
    17→ * different constraints — quantization happens in the drag handler).
    18→ */
    19→export const quantizePositions = (positions: Map<string, number>): Map<string, number> => {
    20→  // Extract trailing endpoint before quantizing
    21→  const trailingValue = positions.get(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    22→  const imagePositions = new Map(positions);
    23→  imagePositions.delete(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    24→
    25→  const entries = [...imagePositions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    26→
    27→  if (entries.length === 0) {
    28→    // If only trailing existed, return it as-is
    29→    const result = new Map<string, number>();
    30→    if (trailingValue !== undefined) result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
    31→    return result;
    32→  }
    33→
    34→  const result = new Map<string, number>();
    35→
    36→  // First item always at 0
    37→  result.set(entries[0][0], 0);
    38→
    39→  // Subsequent items: quantize the gap from previous
    40→  for (let i = 1; i < entries.length; i++) {
    41→    const [id, pos] = entries[i];
    42→    const prevPos = result.get(entries[i - 1][0]) ?? 0;
    43→    const originalGap = pos - entries[i - 1][1];
    44→    const quantizedGap = quantizeGap(Math.max(originalGap, QUANTIZE_MIN_GAP), QUANTIZE_MIN_GAP);
    45→    result.set(id, prevPos + quantizedGap);
    46→  }
    47→
    48→  // Re-add trailing endpoint unchanged
    49→  if (trailingValue !== undefined) {
    50→    result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  return result;
    54→};
    55→
    56→// Calculate max gap based on context frames (81 frames default)
    57→const calculateMaxGap = (contextFrames: number = 0, baseMax: number = 81): number => {
    58→  return Math.max(1, baseMax - (contextFrames * 2));
    59→};
    60→
    61→// Minimum gap between frames (4N+1 format, starting at 5)
    62→const MIN_GAP = 5;
    63→
    64→
    65→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→// Shrink oversized gaps and enforce frame 0 constraint.
    67→// First item is always placed at position 0.
    68→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    69→const shrinkOversizedGaps = (
    70→  positions: Map<string, number>,
    71→  excludeId?: string,
    72→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
    73→): Map<string, number> => {
    74→  const maxGap = calculateMaxGap();
    75→
    76→  // Preserve trailing endpoint separately (not subject to gap shrinking)
    77→  const trailingValue = positions.get(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    78→
    79→  const entries = [...positions.entries()].filter(([id]) => id !== excludeId && id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    80→
    81→  // Ensure frame 0 is included if it exists
    82→  if (!entries.some(([_, pos]) => pos === 0)) {
    83→    const zeroId = [...positions.entries()].find(([_, pos]) => pos === 0)?.[0];
    84→    if (zeroId) entries.push([zeroId, 0]);
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  entries.sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    88→
    89→  const result = new Map<string, number>();
    90→
    91→  // First item always at position 0 (this enforces the frame 0 constraint)
    92→  for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
    93→    const [id, originalPos] = entries[i];
    94→
    95→    if (i === 0) {
    96→      result.set(id, 0);
    97→    } else {
    98→      const prevPos = result.get(entries[i - 1][0]) ?? 0;
    99→      const prevOriginalPos = entries[i - 1][1];
   100→
   101→      const originalGap = originalPos - prevOriginalPos;
   102→      const desiredGap = Math.max(originalGap, MIN_GAP);
   103→      const quantizedDesiredGap = skipQuantization ? desiredGap : quantizeGap(desiredGap, MIN_GAP);
   104→      const constrainedGap = Math.min(quantizedDesiredGap, maxGap);
   105→      const finalGap = skipQuantization ? constrainedGap : quantizeGap(constrainedGap, MIN_GAP);
   106→
   107→      result.set(id, prevPos + finalGap);
   108→    }
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  // Re-add excluded id unchanged
   112→  if (excludeId && positions.has(excludeId)) {
   113→    result.set(excludeId, positions.get(excludeId)!);
   114→  }
   115→
   116→  // Re-add trailing endpoint unchanged
   117→  if (trailingValue !== undefined) {
   118→    result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
   119→  }
   120→
   121→  return result;
   122→};
   123→
   124→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   125→// Apply fluid timeline behavior with movement limiting
   126→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→export const applyFluidTimeline = (
   128→  positions: Map<string, number>,
   129→  draggedId: string,
   130→  targetFrame: number,
   131→  excludeId?: string,
   132→  _fullMin: number = 0,
   133→  _fullMax: number = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
   134→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
   135→): Map<string, number> => {
   136→  const originalPos = positions.get(draggedId) ?? 0;
   137→  const movementAmount = targetFrame - originalPos;
   138→
   139→  // Limit movement to 50 frames per drag for natural feel
   140→  const maxSingleMove = 50;
   141→  const limitedMovement = Math.max(-maxSingleMove, Math.min(maxSingleMove, movementAmount));
   142→  const limitedTargetFrame = originalPos + limitedMovement;
   143→
   144→  const result = new Map(positions);
   145→  result.set(draggedId, limitedTargetFrame);
   146→
   147→  // Apply gap constraints (this also handles frame 0 enforcement)
   148→  return shrinkOversizedGaps(result, excludeId, skipQuantization);
   149→};
   150→
   151→// Convert pixel position to frame number
   152→export const pixelToFrame = (pixelX: number, containerWidth: number, fullMin: number, fullRange: number): number => {
   153→  const fraction = pixelX / containerWidth;
   154→  return Math.round(fullMin + fraction * fullRange);
   155→};
   156→
   157→// Calculate timeline dimensions
   158→const MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX = 30;
   159→
   160→export const getTimelineDimensions = (
   161→  framePositions: Map<string, number>,
   162→  pendingFrames?: (number | null)[]
   163→) => {
   164→  const positions = Array.from(framePositions.values());
   165→  const validPendingFrames = (pendingFrames || []).filter((f): f is number => f !== null && f !== undefined);
   166→  const allPositions = [...positions, ...validPendingFrames];
   167→
   168→  if (allPositions.length === 0) {
   169→    return { fullMin: 0, fullMax: MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX, fullRange: MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX };
   170→  }
   171→
   172→  const staticMax = Math.max(...allPositions, 0);
   173→  const staticMin = Math.min(...allPositions, 0);
   174→  const fullMax = Math.max(staticMax, MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX);
   175→  const fullMin = Math.min(0, staticMin);
   176→  const fullRange = Math.max(fullMax - fullMin, 1);
   177→
   178→  return { fullMin, fullMax, fullRange };
   179→};
   180→
   181→interface TrailingEffectiveEndInput {
   182→  framePositions: Map<string, number>;
   183→  imagesCount: number;
   184→  hasExistingTrailingVideo: boolean;
   185→  singleImageOffset?: number;
   186→  multiImageOffset?: number;
   187→}
   188→
   189→export function getTrailingEffectiveEnd({
   190→  framePositions,
   191→  imagesCount,
   192→  hasExistingTrailingVideo,
   193→  singleImageOffset = 49,
   194→  multiImageOffset = 17,
   195→}: TrailingEffectiveEndInput): number | null {
   196→  if (imagesCount === 0) {
   197→    return null;
   198→  }
   199→
   200→  const isMultiImage = imagesCount > 1;
   201→  if (isMultiImage && !hasExistingTrailingVideo) {
   202→    return null;
   203→  }
   204→
   205→  const trailingDefaultOffset = isMultiImage ? multiImageOffset : singleImageOffset;
   206→  const imageValues = [...framePositions.entries()]
   207→    .filter(([id]) => id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY)
   208→    .map(([, value]) => value);
   209→  if (imageValues.length === 0) {
   210→    return null;
   211→  }
   212→  return Math.max(...imageValues) + trailingDefaultOffset;
   213→}
   214→
   215→
   216→// Get pair information from positions (excludes trailing endpoint)
   217→export const getPairInfo = (framePositions: Map<string, number>) => {
   218→  const sortedPositions = [...framePositions.entries()]
   219→    .filter(([id]) => id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY)
   220→    .map(([id, pos]) => ({ id, pos }))
   221→    .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
   222→
   223→  const pairs = [];
   224→  for (let i = 0; i < sortedPositions.length - 1; i++) {
   225→    const startFrame = sortedPositions[i].pos;
   226→    const endFrame = sortedPositions[i + 1].pos;
   227→    const pairFrames = endFrame - startFrame;
   228→
   229→    pairs.push({
   230→      index: i,
   231→      startId: sortedPositions[i].id,
   232→      endId: sortedPositions[i + 1].id,
   233→      startFrame,
   234→      endFrame,
   235→      frames: pairFrames,
   236→      generationStart: startFrame,
   237→      contextStart: endFrame,
   238→      contextEnd: endFrame,
   239→    });
   240→  }
   241→
   242→  return pairs;
   243→};
   244→
   245→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→// Multi-select bundling: bundle selected items 5 frames apart
   247→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→const BUNDLE_GAP = 5;
   249→
   250→export const applyFluidTimelineMulti = (
   251→  positions: Map<string, number>,
   252→  selectedIds: string[],
   253→  targetFrame: number,
   254→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
   255→): Map<string, number> => {
   256→  if (selectedIds.length === 0) return positions;
   257→  if (selectedIds.length === 1) {
   258→    return applyFluidTimeline(positions, selectedIds[0], targetFrame, undefined, 0, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, skipQuantization);
   259→  }
   260→
   261→  const result = new Map(positions);
   262→
   263→  // Sort selected items by current position to preserve relative order
   264→  const selectedWithPositions = selectedIds
   265→    .map(id => ({ id, frame: positions.get(id) ?? 0 }))
   266→    .sort((a, b) => a.frame - b.frame);
   267→
   268→  // Place selected items 5 frames apart
   269→  selectedWithPositions.forEach((item, index) => {
   270→    result.set(item.id, targetFrame + (index * BUNDLE_GAP));
   271→  });
   272→
   273→  // Push out non-selected items that are within the bundle range
   274→  const bundleEnd = targetFrame + ((selectedIds.length - 1) * BUNDLE_GAP);
   275→  const nonSelectedItems = [...positions.entries()]
   276→    .filter(([id]) => !selectedIds.includes(id));
   277→
   278→  nonSelectedItems.forEach(([id, pos]) => {
   279→    if (pos >= targetFrame && pos <= bundleEnd) {
   280→      result.set(id, bundleEnd + BUNDLE_GAP);
   281→    }
   282→  });
   283→
   284→  // Apply gap constraints (handles frame 0 enforcement)
   285→  return shrinkOversizedGaps(result, undefined, skipQuantization);
   286→};
   287→
   288→/**
   289→ * Calculate placement for a new structure video on the timeline.
   290→ * Places after the last existing video, clipping the last video if needed to make space.
   291→ *
   292→ * @returns Object with start_frame, end_frame, and optional lastVideoUpdate if clipping is needed
   293→ */
   294→interface VideoPlacementResult {
   295→  start_frame: number;
   296→  end_frame: number;
   297→  lastVideoUpdate?: { index: number; newEndFrame: number };
   298→}
   299→
   300→export const calculateNewVideoPlacement = (
   301→  videoFrameCount: number,
   302→  existingVideos: Array<{ path: string; start_frame: number; end_frame: number }> | undefined,
   303→  fullMax: number
   304→): VideoPlacementResult => {
   305→  let start_frame = 0;
   306→  let end_frame = videoFrameCount;
   307→  let lastVideoUpdate: { index: number; newEndFrame: number } | undefined;
   308→
   309→  if (existingVideos && existingVideos.length > 0) {
   310→    const sorted = [...existingVideos].sort((a, b) => a.start_frame - b.start_frame);
   311→    const lastVideo = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
   312→    const lastVideoIndex = existingVideos.findIndex(
   313→      v => v.path === lastVideo.path && v.start_frame === lastVideo.start_frame
   314→    );
   315→
   316→    start_frame = lastVideo.end_frame;
   317→    end_frame = start_frame + videoFrameCount;
   318→
   319→    // If no space on timeline, clip 1/5 of the last video's range
   320→    if (start_frame >= fullMax && lastVideoIndex >= 0) {
   321→      const lastVideoRange = lastVideo.end_frame - lastVideo.start_frame;
   322→      const clipAmount = Math.max(10, Math.floor(lastVideoRange / 5));
   323→      const newLastVideoEnd = lastVideo.end_frame - clipAmount;
   324→
   325→      if (newLastVideoEnd > lastVideo.start_frame + 10) {
   326→        lastVideoUpdate = { index: lastVideoIndex, newEndFrame: newLastVideoEnd };
   327→        start_frame = newLastVideoEnd;
   328→        end_frame = start_frame + videoFrameCount;
   329→      }
   330→    }
   331→  }
   332→
   333→  return { start_frame, end_frame, lastVideoUpdate };
   334→};
   335→
   336→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   337→// Trailing video detection utilities
   338→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   339→
   340→/**
   341→ * Extract pair_shot_generation_id from a generation row.
   342→ * Checks the FK column first, then falls back to params for legacy data.
   343→ */
   344→const getPairShotGenerationId = (
   345→  generation: {
   346→    pair_shot_generation_id?: string | null;
   347→    params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   348→  }
   349→): string | null => {
   350→  // Check FK column first (new format)
   351→  if (generation.pair_shot_generation_id) {
   352→    return generation.pair_shot_generation_id;
   353→  }
   354→
   355→  // Fallback to params (legacy format)
   356→  const params = generation.params;
   357→  if (!params) return null;
   358→
   359→  const individualParams = params.individual_segment_params as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   360→  return (
   361→    (individualParams?.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
   362→    (params.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
   363→    null
   364→  );
   365→};
   366→
   367→/**
   368→ * Find trailing video info from a list of video outputs.
   369→ * A trailing video is one where pair_shot_generation_id matches the last image's shot_generation_id.
   370→ *
   371→ * @param videoOutputs - Array of generation rows (video outputs)
   372→ * @param lastImageShotGenId - The shot_generation_id of the last image on the timeline
   373→ * @returns Object with hasTrailing boolean and optional videoUrl
   374→ */
   375→export const findTrailingVideoInfo = (
   376→  videoOutputs: Array<{
   377→    type?: string | null;
   378→    location?: string | null;
   379→    pair_shot_generation_id?: string | null;
   380→    params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   381→  }>,
   382→  lastImageShotGenId: string | null
   383→): { hasTrailing: boolean; videoUrl: string | null } => {
   384→  if (!videoOutputs || videoOutputs.length === 0 || !lastImageShotGenId) {
   385→    return { hasTrailing: false, videoUrl: null };
   386→  }
   387→
   388→  const trailingVideo = videoOutputs.find(gen => {
   389→    // Must be a video with a location (completed)
   390→    if (!gen.type?.includes('video') || !gen.location) return false;
   391→    // Check if pair_shot_generation_id matches the last image
   392→    const pairId = getPairShotGenerationId(gen);
   393→    return pairId === lastImageShotGenId;
   394→  });
   395→
   396→  return {
   397→    hasTrailing: !!trailingVideo,
   398→    videoUrl: trailingVideo?.location || null,
   399→  };
   400→};
   401→
```

```
    15→/**
    16→ * usePairSettingsHandler - Builds the pair click handler for SegmentOutputStrip

    19→ * Handles three cases:

    22→ * 3. Regular pair segments

    24→export function usePairSettingsHandler({

    30→  onPairClick,

    33→

    35→

    43→        const startFrame = passedFrameData?.startFrame ?? imageFrame;

    52→            generationId: image.generation_id,

    54→            thumbUrl: image.thumbUrl,
    55→            position: 1,
    56→          },
    57→          endImage: null,
    58→        });
    59→      }
    60→      return;
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    // Handle multi-image trailing segment (pairIndex is after all regular pairs)
```

### 4. **Start-frame and end-frame throttle effects are duplicated**

**The Code (START effect):**
```tsx
useEffect(() => {
  if (!videoUrl || !isVideoReady || trimState.videoDuration === 0) return;
  if (!initialExtractionDoneRef.current) return;
  if (prevStartTrimRef.current === trimState.startTrim) return;
  prevStartTrimRef.current = trimState.startTrim;

  const updateStartFrame = () => {
    const keepStartTime = trimState.startTrim;
    if (!Number.isFinite(keepStartTime)) return;
    queueFrameExtraction(keepStartTime, (frame) => {
      setStartFrame(frame);
      lastStartUpdateRef.current = Date.now();
    });
  };

  if (pendingStartUpdateRef.current) {
    clearTimeout(pendingStartUpdateRef.current);
    pendingStartUpdateRef.current = null;
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  const timeSinceLastUpdate = now - lastStartUpdateRef.current;
  const throttleMs = 60;

  if (timeSinceLastUpdate >= throttleMs) {
    updateStartFrame();
  } else {
    pendingStartUpdateRef.current = setTimeout(updateStartFrame, throttleMs - timeSinceLastUpdate);
  }

  return () => {
    if (pendingStartUpdateRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(pendingStartUpdateRef.current);
      pendingStartUpdateRef.current = null;
    }
  };
}, [videoUrl, isVideoReady, trimState.startTrim, trimState.videoDuration, queueFrameExtraction]);
```

**The Code (END effect):**
```tsx
useEffect(() => {
  if (!videoUrl || !isVideoReady || trimState.videoDuration === 0) return;
  if (!initialExtractionDoneRef.current) return;
  if (prevEndTrimRef.current === trimState.endTrim) return;
  prevEndTrimRef.current = trimState.endTrim;

  const updateEndFrame = () => {
    const keepEndTime = trimState.videoDuration - trimState.endTrim;
    if (!Number.isFinite(keepEndTime)) return;
    queueFrameExtraction(Math.max(0.001, keepEndTime - 0.1), (frame) => {
      setEndFrame(frame);
      lastEndUpdateRef.current = Date.now();
    });
  };

  if (pendingEndUpdateRef.current) {
    clearTimeout(pendingEndUpdateRef.current);
    pendingEndUpdateRef.current = null;
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  const timeSinceLastUpdate = now - lastEndUpdateRef.current;
  const throttleMs = 60;

  if (timeSinceLastUpdate >= throttleMs) {
    updateEndFrame();
  } else {
    pendingEndUpdateRef.current = setTimeout(updateEndFrame, throttleMs - timeSinceLastUpdate);
  }

  return () => {
    if (pendingEndUpdateRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(pendingEndUpdateRef.current);
      pendingEndUpdateRef.current = null;
    }
  };
}, [videoUrl, isVideoReady, trimState.endTrim, trimState.videoDuration, queueFrameExtraction]);
```

**Assessment:** These effects are **nearly identical** — 95% duplication:
- Same guards, same throttle logic (60ms), same cleanup
- Only differences:
  - Which trim value they watch (`startTrim` vs `endTrim`)
  - How the time is calculated (`trimState.startTrim` vs `trimState.videoDuration - trimState.endTrim`)
  - Which refs they use (`lastStartUpdateRef`/`pendingStartUpdateRef` vs `lastEndUpdateRef`/`pendingEndUpdateRef`)
  - Minor calculation difference in end (`Math.max(0.001, keepEndTime - 0.1)` vs plain time)

**Fix Difficulty:** **MODERATE REFACTOR** - Doable but needs care:

**Option A (Extract a factory):**
```ts
const createThrottledFrameExtractor = (
  trimValue: number,
  lastUpdateRef: React.MutableRefObject<number>,
  pendingTimeoutRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>,
  prevValueRef: React.MutableRefObject<number>,
  updateFn: () => void
) => {
  // ... shared throttle logic
}

useEffect(() => {
  // ... guards
  const updateStartFrame = () => { /* ... */ };
  prevStartTrimRef.current = trimState.startTrim;
  createThrottledFrameExtractor(trimState.startTrim, lastStartUpdateRef, pendingStartUpdateRef, prevStartTrimRef, updateStartFrame);
}, [videoUrl, isVideoReady, trimState.startTrim, trimState.videoDuration, queueFrameExtraction]);
```

This works but passes many refs, which is verbose.

**Option B (Higher-order hook):**
```ts
const useThrottledFrameExtraction = (
  trimValue: number,
  calculateTime: (trimValue: number) => number,
  videoUrl: string | null,
  isVideoReady: boolean,
  videoDuration: number,
  queueFrameExtraction: (time: number, cb: (frame: string | null) => void) => void,
  setFrame: (frame: string | null) => void,
  lastUpdateRef: React.MutableRefObject<number>,
  pendingRef: React.MutableRefObject<NodeJS.Timeout | null>,
  prevValueRef: React.MutableRefObject<number>
) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    // ... shared logic
  }, [trimValue, videoUrl, isVideoReady, videoDuration, ...]);
}
```

Also verbose but more modular.

**Option C (Accept the duplication):**
The two effects are so close that attempting extraction may actually harm readability. The 42-line duplication is visible and obvious; a factory with 10+ parameters is harder to follow. If performance or correctness isn't suffering, leaving them as-is might be the pragmatic choice — especially since they're in the same file and their divergence is clear (startTrim vs endTrim).

**Risk:** 
- Moderate if you extract — easy to break the dependency array or pass wrong refs.
- Low if you leave it — it's working, just redundant.

```
1→import { isAbortError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errorUtils';
     2→
     3→type TimelineWritePhase = 'queued' | 'start' | 'end';
     4→
     5→export interface TimelineWriteEventMeta {
     6→  shotId: string;
     7→  operation: string;
     8→  waitMs: number;
     9→  durationMs: number;
    10→  queueDepth: number;
    11→  requestId: number;
    12→  blockedByOperation?: string | null;
    13→  blockedByDurationMs?: number | null;
    14→}
    15→
    16→export type TimelineWriteEventHandler = (phase: TimelineWritePhase, meta: TimelineWriteEventMeta) => void;
    17→
    18→interface TimelineWriteLoggerOptions {
    19→  logPrefix: string;
    20→  log?: (...args: Parameters<typeof console.log>) => void;
    21→}
    22→
    23→function shortTimelineId(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
    24→  return value ? value.slice(0, 8) : null;
    25→}
    26→
    27→/**
    28→ * Shared queue-phase logger for timeline write serialization.
    29→ * Keeps queue observability structure consistent across timeline hooks.
    30→ */
    31→export function createTimelineWriteQueueLogger(options: TimelineWriteLoggerOptions): TimelineWriteEventHandler {
    32→  const log = options.log ?? console.log;
    33→  return (phase, meta) => {
    34→    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
    35→      shotId: shortTimelineId(meta.shotId),
    36→      requestId: meta.requestId,
    37→      operation: meta.operation,
    38→      waitMs: meta.waitMs,
    39→      queueDepth: meta.queueDepth,
    40→      blockedByOperation: meta.blockedByOperation ?? null,
    41→      blockedByDurationMs: meta.blockedByDurationMs ?? null,
    42→    };
    43→
    44→    if (phase !== 'start') {
    45→      payload.durationMs = meta.durationMs;
    46→    }
    47→
    48→    log(`${options.logPrefix} write queue ${phase}`, payload);
    49→  };
    50→}
    51→
    52→const DEFAULT_TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;
    53→const TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_CODE = 'TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT';
    54→
    55→interface TimelineWriteTimeoutOptions {
    56→  timeoutMs?: number;
    57→  onTimeout?: (meta: { operation: string; timeoutMs: number; pendingMs: number }) => void;
    58→}
    59→
    60→class TimelineWriteTimeoutError extends Error {
    61→  readonly code = TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_CODE;
    62→
    63→  constructor(operation: string, timeoutMs: number, cause?: unknown) {
    64→    super(`Timeline write "${operation}" timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`);
    65→    this.name = 'TimelineWriteTimeoutError';
    66→    if (cause !== undefined) {
    67→      (this as { cause?: unknown }).cause = cause;
    68→    }
    69→  }
    70→}
    71→
    72→export function isTimelineWriteTimeoutError(error: unknown): error is TimelineWriteTimeoutError {
    73→  return (
    74→    error instanceof TimelineWriteTimeoutError
    75→    || (
    76→      typeof error === 'object'
    77→      && error !== null
    78→      && 'code' in error
    79→      && (error as { code?: unknown }).code === TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_CODE
    80→    )
    81→  );
    82→}
    83→
    84→/**
    85→ * Guard a write request with an abortable timeout so stalled network calls
    86→ * cannot block the serialized per-shot queue forever.
    87→ */
    88→export async function runTimelineWriteWithTimeout<T>(
    89→  operation: string,
    90→  task: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>,
    91→  options?: TimelineWriteTimeoutOptions,
    92→): Promise<T> {
    93→  const timeoutMs = options?.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMELINE_WRITE_TIMEOUT_MS;
    94→  const startedAt = Date.now();
    95→  const controller = new AbortController();
    96→  let timedOut = false;
    97→
    98→  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
    99→    timedOut = true;
   100→    options?.onTimeout?.({
   101→      operation,
   102→      timeoutMs,
   103→      pendingMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
   104→    });
   105→    controller.abort();
   106→  }, timeoutMs);
   107→
   108→  try {
   109→    return await task(controller.signal);
   110→  } catch (error) {
   111→    if (timedOut || (controller.signal.aborted && isAbortError(error))) {
   112→      throw new TimelineWriteTimeoutError(operation, timeoutMs, error);
   113→    }
   114→    throw error;
   115→  } finally {
   116→    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
   117→  }
   118→}
   119→
   120→const shotWriteTail = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
   121→const shotWriteDepth = new Map<string, number>();
   122→const shotWriteActive = new Map<string, { requestId: number; operation: string; startedAt: number }>();
   123→let writeRequestCounter = 0;
   124→const DEFAULT_SERIALIZED_TIMELINE_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
   125→
   126→interface TimelineSerializedWriteOptions {
   127→  timeoutMs?: number;
   128→  onTimeout?: (meta: {
   129→    shotId: string;
   130→    operation: string;
   131→    timeoutMs: number;
   132→    pendingMs: number;
   133→    requestId: number;
   134→  }) => void;
   135→}
   136→
   137→function runSerializedTaskWithTimeout<T>(
   138→  operation: string,
   139→  task: () => Promise<T>,
   140→  timeoutMs: number,
   141→  onTimeout: () => void,
   142→): Promise<T> {
   143→  if (timeoutMs <= 0) {
   144→    return task();
   145→  }
   146→
   147→  return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
   148→    let settled = false;
   149→    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
   150→      if (settled) {
   151→        return;
   152→      }
   153→      settled = true;
   154→      onTimeout();
   155→      reject(new TimelineWriteTimeoutError(operation, timeoutMs));
   156→    }, timeoutMs);
   157→
   158→    Promise.resolve()
   159→      .then(task)
   160→      .then(
   161→        (result) => {
   162→          if (settled) {
   163→            return;
   164→          }
   165→          settled = true;
   166→          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
   167→          resolve(result);
   168→        },
   169→        (error) => {
   170→          if (settled) {
   171→            return;
   172→          }
   173→          settled = true;
   174→          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
   175→          reject(error);
   176→        },
   177→      );
   178→  });
   179→}
   180→
   181→/**
   182→ * Returns true if any serialized timeline write is active or queued for the
   183→ * given shot. Both the timeline drag path and the batch editor path go through
   184→ * runSerializedTimelineWrite, so this covers all writes across both paths.
   185→ * Used by useTimelinePositions.syncFromDatabase to suppress stale syncs while
   186→ * the batch editor is writing (the batch editor does not set isLockedRef, so
   187→ * without this check a background refetch during the midpoint RPC would snap
   188→ * positions back to pre-edit values).
   189→ */
   190→export function isTimelineWriteActive(shotId: string): boolean {
   191→  return (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 0) > 0;
   192→}
   193→
   194→/**
   195→ * Serialize timeline writes per shot to avoid overlapping updates from
   196→ * different UI flows (batch reorder, timeline drag, etc.).
   197→ */
   198→export async function runSerializedTimelineWrite<T>(
   199→  shotId: string,
   200→  operation: string,
   201→  task: () => Promise<T>,
   202→  onEvent?: TimelineWriteEventHandler,
   203→  options?: TimelineSerializedWriteOptions,
   204→): Promise<T> {
   205→  const requestId = ++writeRequestCounter;
   206→  const queuedAt = Date.now();
   207→  const previousTail = shotWriteTail.get(shotId) ?? Promise.resolve();
   208→  const nextDepth = (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 0) + 1;
   209→  shotWriteDepth.set(shotId, nextDepth);
   210→  const blockedBy = shotWriteActive.get(shotId);
   211→  const blockedByDurationMs = blockedBy ? Math.max(0, queuedAt - blockedBy.startedAt) : null;
   212→
   213→  onEvent?.('queued', {
   214→    shotId,
   215→    operation,
   216→    waitMs: 0,
   217→    durationMs: 0,
   218→    queueDepth: Math.max(0, nextDepth - 1),
   219→    requestId,
   220→    blockedByOperation: blockedBy?.operation ?? null,
   221→    blockedByDurationMs,
   222→  });
   223→
   224→  let releaseTail!: () => void;
   225→  const currentTail = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
   226→    releaseTail = resolve;
   227→  });
   228→
   229→  const chainedTail = previousTail.catch(() => undefined).then(() => currentTail);
   230→  shotWriteTail.set(shotId, chainedTail);
   231→
   232→  await previousTail.catch(() => undefined);
   233→
   234→  const startedAt = Date.now();
   235→  shotWriteActive.set(shotId, {
   236→    requestId,
   237→    operation,
   238→    startedAt,
   239→  });
   240→  onEvent?.('start', {
   241→    shotId,
   242→    operation,
   243→    waitMs: startedAt - queuedAt,
   244→    durationMs: 0,
   245→    queueDepth: Math.max(0, (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 1) - 1),
   246→    requestId,
   247→    blockedByOperation: blockedBy?.operation ?? null,
   248→    blockedByDurationMs,
   249→  });
   250→
   251→  try {
   252→    const timeoutMs = options?.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_SERIALIZED_TIMELINE_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS;
   253→    return await runSerializedTaskWithTimeout(
   254→      operation,
   255→      task,
   256→      timeoutMs,
   257→      () => {
   258→        options?.onTimeout?.({
   259→          shotId,
   260→          operation,
   261→          timeoutMs,
   262→          pendingMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
   263→          requestId,
   264→        });
   265→      },
   266→    );
   267→  } finally {
   268→    releaseTail();
   269→
   270→    const remainingDepth = Math.max(0, (shotWriteDepth.get(shotId) ?? 1) - 1);
   271→    if (remainingDepth === 0) {
   272→      shotWriteDepth.delete(shotId);
   273→    } else {
   274→      shotWriteDepth.set(shotId, remainingDepth);
   275→    }
   276→
   277→    if (shotWriteTail.get(shotId) === chainedTail) {
   278→      shotWriteTail.delete(shotId);
   279→    }
   280→    if (shotWriteActive.get(shotId)?.requestId === requestId) {
   281→      shotWriteActive.delete(shotId);
   282→    }
   283→
   284→    onEvent?.('end', {
   285→      shotId,
   286→      operation,
   287→      waitMs: startedAt - queuedAt,
   288→      durationMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
   289→      queueDepth: Math.max(0, remainingDepth - 1),
   290→      requestId,
   291→      blockedByOperation: blockedBy?.operation ?? null,
   292→      blockedByDurationMs,
   293→    });
   294→  }
   295→}
   296→
```
