1→import type { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     2→import {
     3→  extractUnifiedProjectGenerationsPage,
     4→  isUnifiedProjectGenerationsKey,
     5→  unifiedGenerationQueryKeys,
     6→} from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/unified';
     7→import { fetchGenerations, type GenerationFilters } from '@/shared/hooks/projects/useProjectGenerations';
     8→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     9→import {
    10→  clearLoadedImages,
    11→  preloadingService,
    12→  PRIORITY_VALUES,
    13→} from '@/shared/lib/preloading';
    14→
    15→interface PaginatedResponse {
    16→  items?: Array<{ id?: string; url?: string; thumbUrl?: string }>;
    17→}
    18→
    19→interface AdjacentPreloadContext {
    20→  queryClient: QueryClient;
    21→  projectId: string;
    22→  currentPage: number;
    23→  totalPages: number | null;
    24→  hasMorePages: boolean;
    25→  itemsPerPage: number;
    26→  filters?: GenerationFilters;
    27→  filtersString: string | null;
    28→  keepRange?: number;
    29→  onError: (context: string, error: unknown, logData: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
    30→}
    31→
    32→interface ScheduleAdjacentGenerationPagePreloadOptions {
    33→  queryClient: QueryClient;
    34→  projectId: string;
    35→  currentPage: number;
    36→  totalPages: number | null;
    37→  hasMorePages: boolean;
    38→  itemsPerPage: number;
    39→  filters?: GenerationFilters;
    40→  filtersString: string | null;
    41→  keepRange?: number;
    42→}
    43→
    44→function extractLoadedImageIds(data: unknown): Array<{ id: string }> {
    45→  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
    46→    return [];
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  const maybeItems = (data as { items?: unknown }).items;
    50→  if (!Array.isArray(maybeItems)) {
    51→    return [];
    52→  }
    53→
    54→  return maybeItems
    55→    .map((item) => {
    56→      if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') {
    57→        return null;
    58→      }
    59→      const id = (item as { id?: unknown }).id;
    60→      return typeof id === 'string' ? { id } : null;
    61→    })
    62→    .filter((item): item is { id: string } => item !== null);
    63→}
    64→
    65→export function cleanupDistantGenerationPages(
    66→  queryClient: QueryClient,
    67→  projectId: string,
    68→  currentPage: number,
    69→  keepRange: number = 2,
    70→): void {
    71→  const allQueries = queryClient.getQueryCache().getAll();
    72→
    73→  allQueries.forEach((query) => {
    74→    const key = query.queryKey;
    75→    if (!Array.isArray(key)) return;
    76→
    77→    if (!isUnifiedProjectGenerationsKey(key, projectId)) {
    78→      return;
    79→    }
    80→
    81→    const page = extractUnifiedProjectGenerationsPage(key, projectId);
    82→    if (page !== null && Math.abs(page - currentPage) > keepRange) {
    83→      const loadedImageIds = extractLoadedImageIds(query.state?.data);
    84→      if (loadedImageIds.length > 0) {
    85→        clearLoadedImages(loadedImageIds);
    86→      }
    87→      queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: query.queryKey });
    88→    }
    89→  });
    90→}
    91→
    92→export function scheduleAdjacentGenerationPagePreload({
    93→  queryClient,
    94→  projectId,
    95→  currentPage,
    96→  totalPages,
    97→  hasMorePages,
    98→  itemsPerPage,
    99→  filters,
   100→  filtersString,
   101→  keepRange = 2,
   102→}: ScheduleAdjacentGenerationPagePreloadOptions): () => void {
   103→  const config = preloadingService.getConfig();
   104→
   105→  const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
   106→    await prefetchAdjacentGenerationPages({
   107→      queryClient,
   108→      projectId,
   109→      currentPage,
   110→      totalPages,
   111→      hasMorePages,
   112→      itemsPerPage,
   113→      filters,
   114→      filtersString,
   115→      keepRange,
   116→      onError: (context, error, logData) => {
   117→        normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
   118→          context,
   119→          showToast: false,
   120→          logData,
   121→        });
   122→      },
   123→    });
   124→  }, config.debounceMs);
   125→
   126→  return () => {
   127→    clearTimeout(timer);
   128→  };
   129→}
   130→
   131→async function prefetchPage(
   132→  context: AdjacentPreloadContext,
   133→  page: number,
   134→  priority: number,
   135→  errorContext: string,
   136→): Promise<void> {
   137→  const {
   138→    queryClient,
   139→    projectId,
   140→    itemsPerPage,
   141→    filters,
   142→    filtersString,
   143→    onError,
   144→  } = context;
   145→
   146→  try {
   147→    await queryClient.prefetchQuery({
   148→      [REDACTED](
   149→        projectId,
   150→        page,
   151→        itemsPerPage,
   152→        filtersString,
   153→      ),
   154→      queryFn: () =>
   155→        fetchGenerations(
   156→          projectId,
   157→          itemsPerPage,
   158→          (page - 1) * itemsPerPage,
   159→          filters,
   160→        ),
   161→      staleTime: 30_000,
   162→    });
   163→
   164→    const cached = queryClient.getQueryData<PaginatedResponse>(
   165→      unifiedGenerationQueryKeys.byProject(projectId, page, itemsPerPage, filtersString),
   166→    );
   167→    if (cached?.items) {
   168→      preloadingService.preloadImages(cached.items, priority);
   169→    }
   170→  } catch (error) {
   171→    onError(errorContext, error, { projectId, page });
   172→  }
   173→}
   174→
   175→export async function prefetchAdjacentGenerationPages(context: AdjacentPreloadContext): Promise<void> {
   176→  const {
   177→    currentPage,
   178→    totalPages,
   179→    hasMorePages,
   180→    keepRange = 2,
   181→  } = context;
   182→
   183→  const hasPrevPage = currentPage > 1;
   184→  const hasNextPage = totalPages === null
   185→    ? hasMorePages
   186→    : currentPage < totalPages;
   187→
   188→  if (hasNextPage) {
   189→    await prefetchPage(context, currentPage + 1, PRIORITY_VALUES.high, 'useAdjacentPagePreloader.prefetchNext');
   190→  }
   191→
   192→  if (hasPrevPage) {
   193→    await prefetchPage(context, currentPage - 1, PRIORITY_VALUES.normal, 'useAdjacentPagePreloader.prefetchPrevious');
   194→  }
   195→
   196→  cleanupDistantGenerationPages(context.queryClient, context.projectId, context.currentPage, keepRange);
   197→}
   198→

1→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     2→import { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     3→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     4→import { Task } from '@/types/tasks';
     5→import { taskQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/tasks';
     6→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     7→import { isTaskDbRow, mapTaskDbRowToTask } from '@/shared/lib/taskRowMapper';
     8→import {
     9→  getPrimaryTaskMappingsForGenerations,
    10→  toGenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry,
    11→  type GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry,
    12→  type GenerationTaskMappingStatus,
    13→} from '@/shared/lib/generationTaskRepository';
    14→
    15→interface PreloadGenerationTaskMappingsOptions {
    16→  batchSize?: number;
    17→  delayBetweenBatches?: number;
    18→  preloadFullTaskData?: boolean;
    19→  projectId?: string;
    20→}
    21→
    22→export interface GenerationWithTaskData extends GenerationRow {
    23→  taskId?: string | null;
    24→  taskData?: Task | null;
    25→  taskMappingStatus?: GenerationTaskMappingStatus;
    26→  taskMappingError?: string;
    27→}
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * Preload task mappings for a batch of generations in the background.
    31→ */
    32→export async function preloadGenerationTaskMappings(
    33→  generationIds: string[],
    34→  queryClient: QueryClient,
    35→  options: PreloadGenerationTaskMappingsOptions = {},
    36→) {
    37→  const supabase = getSupabaseClient();
    38→  const {
    39→    batchSize = 5,
    40→    delayBetweenBatches = 200,
    41→    preloadFullTaskData = false,
    42→    projectId,
    43→  } = options;
    44→
    45→  for (let i = 0; i < generationIds.length; i += batchSize) {
    46→    const batch = generationIds.slice(i, i + batchSize);
    47→
    48→    const mappings = await getPrimaryTaskMappingsForGenerations(batch, { projectId });
    49→    let reportedBatchQueryFailure = false;
    50→
    51→    const prefetchTasks: Array<Promise<unknown>> = [];
    52→
    53→    batch.forEach((generationId) => {
    54→      const mapping = mappings.get(generationId);
    55→      const cacheEntry = toGenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry(mapping);
    56→      queryClient.setQueryData<GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry>(
    57→        taskQueryKeys.generationMapping(generationId),
    58→        cacheEntry,
    59→      );
    60→
    61→      if (!reportedBatchQueryFailure && cacheEntry.status === 'query_failed' && cacheEntry.queryError) {
    62→        reportedBatchQueryFailure = true;
    63→        normalizeAndPresentError(new Error(cacheEntry.queryError), {
    64→          context: 'GenerationTaskCache',
    65→          showToast: false,
    66→        });
    67→      }
    68→
    69→      if (preloadFullTaskData && cacheEntry.taskId && cacheEntry.status === 'ok') {
    70→        prefetchTasks.push(queryClient.prefetchQuery({
    71→          [REDACTED](cacheEntry.taskId),
    72→          queryFn: async () => {
    73→            const { data, error } = await supabase
    74→              .from('tasks')
    75→              .select('*')
    76→              .eq('id', cacheEntry.taskId)
    77→              .single();
    78→
    79→            if (error) throw error;
    80→            if (!isTaskDbRow(data)) {
    81→              throw new Error('Task row has unexpected shape');
    82→            }
    83→            return mapTaskDbRowToTask(data);
    84→          },
    85→          staleTime: 2 * 60 * 1000,
    86→        }));
    87→      }
    88→    });
    89→
    90→    if (prefetchTasks.length > 0) {
    91→      await Promise.all(prefetchTasks);
    92→    }
    93→
    94→    if (i + batchSize < generationIds.length) {
    95→      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, delayBetweenBatches));
    96→    }
    97→  }
    98→}
    99→
   100→export function mergeGenerationsWithTaskData(
   101→  generations: GenerationRow[],
   102→  queryClient: QueryClient,
   103→): GenerationWithTaskData[] {
   104→  return generations.map((generation) => {
   105→    const generationId = generation.generation_id ?? generation.id;
   106→    const cachedMapping = queryClient.getQueryData<GenerationTaskMappingCacheEntry>(
   107→      taskQueryKeys.generationMapping(generationId),
   108→    );
   109→    const taskId = cachedMapping?.taskId || null;
   110→    const taskData = taskId ? queryClient.getQueryData<Task>(taskQueryKeys.single(taskId)) : null;
   111→    const taskMappingStatus = cachedMapping?.status ?? (taskId ? 'ok' : 'not_loaded');
   112→
   113→    return {
   114→      ...generation,
   115→      taskId,
   116→      taskData,
   117→      taskMappingStatus,
   118→      ...(cachedMapping?.queryError ? { taskMappingError: cachedMapping.queryError } : {}),
   119→    };
   120→  });
   121→}
   122→

1→import {
     2→  validateRequiredFields,
     3→  TaskValidationError,
     4→} from "../taskCreation";
     5→import type { TaskCreationResult } from "../taskCreation";
     6→import { composeTaskRequest } from './taskRequestComposer';
     7→import { runTaskCreationPipeline } from './taskCreatorPipeline';
     8→
     9→// ============================================================================
    10→// Video Enhancement API Param Interfaces (Single Source of Truth)
    11→// ============================================================================
    12→// These interfaces define the snake_case params sent to the backend.
    13→// They match the fal.ai API parameters for FILM and FlashVSR.
    14→
    15→/**
    16→ * FILM frame interpolation parameters
    17→ * Model: fal-ai/film/video
    18→ */
    19→interface FilmInterpolationApiParams {
    20→  /** Number of frames to add between each pair of input frames (1-4) */
    21→  num_frames?: number;
    22→  /** Whether to auto-calculate FPS to maintain playback speed */
    23→  use_calculated_fps?: boolean;
    24→  /** Output FPS (only used if use_calculated_fps is false) */
    25→  fps?: number;
    26→  /** Split video into scenes before interpolation */
    27→  use_scene_detection?: boolean;
    28→  /** Create seamless loop */
    29→  loop?: boolean;
    30→  /** Output quality: low, medium, high, maximum */
    31→  video_quality?: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'maximum';
    32→  /** Write mode: fast, balanced, small */
    33→  video_write_mode?: 'fast' | 'balanced' | 'small';
    34→}
    35→
    36→/**
    37→ * FlashVSR video upscaling parameters
    38→ * Model: fal-ai/flashvsr/upscale/video
    39→ */
    40→interface FlashVsrUpscaleApiParams {
    41→  /** Scaling multiplier (1-4) */
    42→  upscale_factor?: number;
    43→  /** VAE decoding mode: regular (quality), high (balanced), full (fastest) */
    44→  acceleration?: 'regular' | 'high' | 'full';
    45→  /** Tile blending quality (0-100) */
    46→  quality?: number;
    47→  /** Enable color correction */
    48→  color_fix?: boolean;
    49→  /** Output format */
    50→  output_format?: 'X264 (.mp4)' | 'VP9 (.webm)' | 'PRORES4444 (.mov)' | 'GIF';
    51→  /** Output quality: low, medium, high, maximum */
    52→  output_quality?: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'maximum';
    53→  /** Write mode: fast, balanced, small */
    54→  output_write_mode?: 'fast' | 'balanced' | 'small';
    55→  /** Preserve original audio */
    56→  preserve_audio?: boolean;
    57→}
    58→
    59→/**
    60→ * Default values for FILM interpolation (internal use only - not exported)
    61→ */
    62→const DEFAULT_FILM_PARAMS: Required<Pick<
    63→  FilmInterpolationApiParams,
    64→  'num_frames' | 'use_calculated_fps' | 'video_quality'
    65→>> = {
    66→  num_frames: 1,
    67→  use_calculated_fps: true,
    68→  video_quality: 'high',
    69→};
    70→
    71→/**
    72→ * Default values for FlashVSR upscaling (internal use only - not exported)
    73→ */
    74→const DEFAULT_FLASHVSR_PARAMS: Required<Pick<
    75→  FlashVsrUpscaleApiParams,
    76→  'upscale_factor' | 'color_fix' | 'output_quality'
    77→>> = {
    78→  upscale_factor: 2,
    79→  color_fix: true,
    80→  output_quality: 'high',
    81→};
    82→
    83→/**
    84→ * Combined video enhancement task parameters
    85→ */
    86→export interface VideoEnhanceTaskParams {
    87→  // Required
    88→  project_id: string;
    89→  video_url: string;
    90→
    91→  // Mode selection - at least one must be true
    92→  enable_interpolation: boolean;
    93→  enable_upscale: boolean;
    94→
    95→  // FILM interpolation params (used when enable_interpolation is true)
    96→  interpolation?: FilmInterpolationApiParams;
    97→
    98→  // FlashVSR upscale params (used when enable_upscale is true)
    99→  upscale?: FlashVsrUpscaleApiParams;
   100→
   101→  // Task metadata
   102→  shot_id?: string;
   103→  /** Generation ID this enhancement is based on - creates variant when set */
   104→  based_on?: string;
   105→  /** Source variant ID - track which variant was enhanced */
   106→  source_variant_id?: string;
   107→}
   108→
   109→/**
   110→ * Validates video enhancement task parameters
   111→ */
   112→function validateVideoEnhanceParams(params: VideoEnhanceTaskParams): void {
   113→  validateRequiredFields(params, ['project_id', 'video_url']);
   114→
   115→  if (!params.enable_interpolation && !params.enable_upscale) {
   116→    throw new TaskValidationError(
   117→      "At least one enhancement mode (interpolation or upscale) must be enabled",
   118→      'enable_interpolation'
   119→    );
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  // Validate interpolation params if enabled
   123→  if (params.enable_interpolation && params.interpolation?.num_frames !== undefined) {
   124→    if (params.interpolation.num_frames < 1 || params.interpolation.num_frames > 4) {
   125→      throw new TaskValidationError(
   126→        "num_frames must be between 1 and 4",
   127→        'interpolation.num_frames'
   128→      );
   129→    }
   130→  }
   131→
   132→  // Validate upscale params if enabled
   133→  if (params.enable_upscale && params.upscale?.upscale_factor !== undefined) {
   134→    if (params.upscale.upscale_factor < 1 || params.upscale.upscale_factor > 4) {
   135→      throw new TaskValidationError(
   136→        "upscale_factor must be between 1 and 4",
   137→        'upscale.upscale_factor'
   138→      );
   139→    }
   140→  }
   141→}
   142→
   143→/**
   144→ * Builds the task payload for video enhancement
   145→ */
   146→function buildVideoEnhancePayload(
   147→  params: VideoEnhanceTaskParams
   148→): Record<string, unknown> {
   149→  const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
   150→    video_url: params.video_url,
   151→    enable_interpolation: params.enable_interpolation,
   152→    enable_upscale: params.enable_upscale,
   153→  };
   154→
   155→  // Add interpolation params with defaults
   156→  if (params.enable_interpolation) {
   157→    payload.interpolation = {
   158→      num_frames: params.interpolation?.num_frames ?? DEFAULT_FILM_PARAMS.num_frames,
   159→      use_calculated_fps: params.interpolation?.use_calculated_fps ?? DEFAULT_FILM_PARAMS.use_calculated_fps,
   160→      video_quality: params.interpolation?.video_quality ?? DEFAULT_FILM_PARAMS.video_quality,
   161→      // Optional params only if provided
   162→      ...(params.interpolation?.use_scene_detection !== undefined && {
   163→        use_scene_detection: params.interpolation.use_scene_detection,
   164→      }),
   165→      ...(params.interpolation?.loop !== undefined && {
   166→        loop: params.interpolation.loop,
   167→      }),
   168→      ...(params.interpolation?.fps !== undefined && {
   169→        fps: params.interpolation.fps,
   170→      }),
   171→      ...(params.interpolation?.video_write_mode !== undefined && {
   172→        video_write_mode: params.interpolation.video_write_mode,
   173→      }),
   174→    };
   175→  }
   176→
   177→  // Add upscale params with defaults
   178→  if (params.enable_upscale) {
   179→    payload.upscale = {
   180→      upscale_factor: params.upscale?.upscale_factor ?? DEFAULT_FLASHVSR_PARAMS.upscale_factor,
   181→      color_fix: params.upscale?.color_fix ?? DEFAULT_FLASHVSR_PARAMS.color_fix,
   182→      output_quality: params.upscale?.output_quality ?? DEFAULT_FLASHVSR_PARAMS.output_quality,
   183→      // Optional params only if provided
   184→      ...(params.upscale?.quality !== undefined && {
   185→        quality: params.upscale.quality,
   186→      }),
   187→      ...(params.upscale?.acceleration !== undefined && {
   188→        acceleration: params.upscale.acceleration,
   189→      }),
   190→      ...(params.upscale?.output_format !== undefined && {
   191→        output_format: params.upscale.output_format,
   192→      }),
   193→      ...(params.upscale?.output_write_mode !== undefined && {
   194→        output_write_mode: params.upscale.output_write_mode,
   195→      }),
   196→      ...(params.upscale?.preserve_audio !== undefined && {
   197→        preserve_audio: params.upscale.preserve_audio,
   198→      }),
   199→    };
   200→  }
   201→
   202→  // Add metadata
   203→  if (params.shot_id) {
   204→    payload.shot_id = params.shot_id;
   205→  }
   206→  // based_on tells complete_task to create a variant on the source generation
   207→  if (params.based_on) {
   208→    payload.based_on = params.based_on;
   209→    payload.parent_generation_id = params.based_on;
   210→    // is_primary=true makes the enhanced version the main display variant
   211→    payload.is_primary = true;
   212→  }
   213→  // source_variant_id tracks which variant was enhanced (for UI display)
   214→  if (params.source_variant_id) {
   215→    payload.source_variant_id = params.source_variant_id;
   216→  }
   217→
   218→  return payload;
   219→}
   220→
   221→/**
   222→ * Result of creating a video enhancement task
   223→ */
   224→interface VideoEnhanceTaskResult {
   225→  task: TaskCreationResult;
   226→}
   227→
   228→/**
   229→ * Creates a video enhancement task
   230→ *
   231→ * @param params - Video enhancement task parameters
   232→ * @returns Promise resolving to the created task
   233→ */
   234→export async function createVideoEnhanceTask(
   235→  params: VideoEnhanceTaskParams
   236→): Promise<VideoEnhanceTaskResult> {
   237→  return runTaskCreationPipeline({
   238→    params,
   239→    context: 'VideoEnhance',
   240→    validate: validateVideoEnhanceParams,
   241→    buildTaskRequest: (requestParams) => {
   242→      const payload = buildVideoEnhancePayload(requestParams);
   243→
   244→      return composeTaskRequest({
   245→        source: requestParams,
   246→        taskType: 'video_enhance',
   247→        params: {
   248→          tool_type: 'video-enhance',
   249→          ...payload,
   250→        },
   251→      });
   252→    },
   253→    onCreated: (task) => ({ task }),
   254→  });
   255→}
   256→
   257→// TaskValidationError is used internally - import from taskCreation.ts if needed externally
   258→

    17→export async function refetchTimelineFrameCaches(

    40→interface PersistSingleTimelineFrameInput {

    42→  shotGenerationId: string;
    43→  frame: number;

    47→
    48→export async function persistSingleTimelineFrame({
    49→  shotId,
    50→  shotGenerationId,
    51→  frame,
    52→  logPrefix,
    53→  log,
    54→}: PersistSingleTimelineFrameInput): Promise<void> {
    55→  await runTimelineWriteWithTimeout(
    56→    'timeline-frame-single-update-write',
    57→    async (signal) => {
    58→      const query = supabase().from('shot_generations')
    59→        .update({ timeline_frame: frame })
    60→        .eq('id', shotGenerationId)
    61→        .eq('shot_id', shotId);
    62→      const request = 'abortSignal' in query
    63→        ? query.abortSignal(signal)
    64→        : query;
    65→      const { error } = await request;
    66→      if (error) {
    67→        throw error;
    68→      }
    69→    },
    70→    {
    71→      onTimeout: ({ pendingMs, timeoutMs }) => {
    72→        log(`${logPrefix} updateTimelineFrame timed out`, {
    73→          shotId: shortNullableId(shotId),
    74→          shotGenerationId: shortNullableId(shotGenerationId),
    75→          targetFrame: frame,
    76→          timeoutMs,
    77→          pendingMs,
    78→        });
    79→      },
    80→    },
    81→  );
    82→}
    83→

    89→

    94→
    95→export function buildCanonicalTimelineUpdates({
    96→  updates,
    97→  shotGenerations,
    98→}: BuildCanonicalUpdatesInput): CanonicalTimelineFrameUpdate[] {
    99→  return updates
   100→    .map(({ shotGenerationId, newFrame }) => {
   101→      const shotGen = shotGenerations.find((sg) => sg.id === shotGenerationId);
   102→      if (!shotGen?.id || !shotGen.generation_id) {
   103→        return null;
   104→      }
   105→      return {
   106→        shotGenerationId: shotGen.id,
   107→        generationId: shotGen.generation_id,
   108→        newFrame,
   109→      } satisfies CanonicalTimelineFrameUpdate;
   110→    })
   111→    .filter((update): update is CanonicalTimelineFrameUpdate => update !== null);
   112→}

   129→export async function persistTimelineFrameBatchUpdates({
   130→  shotId,

   135→  logPrefix,

   158→  syncShotData: (generationId: string, targetShotId: string, frame: number) => Promise<void>;
   159→  logPrefix: string;
   160→  log: TimelineLogFn;
   161→  ignoreTimeout?: boolean;
   162→}
   163→
   164→export async function syncTimelineGenerationFrames({
   165→  shotId,
   166→  targets,
   167→  syncShotData,
   168→  logPrefix,
   169→  log,
   170→  ignoreTimeout = false,
   171→}: SyncTimelineGenerationFramesInput): Promise<void> {
   172→  if (targets.length === 0) {
   173→    return;
   174→  }
   175→
   176→  try {
   177→    await runTimelineWriteWithTimeout(
   178→      'timeline-frame-sync-shot-data',
   179→      async () => {
   180→        await Promise.all(
   181→          targets.map(({ generationId, frame }) => syncShotData(generationId, shotId, frame)),
   182→        );
   183→      },
   184→      {
   185→        timeoutMs: 10_000,
   186→        onTimeout: ({ pendingMs, timeoutMs }) => {
   187→          log(`${logPrefix} shot_data sync timed out`, {
   188→            shotId: shortNullableId(shotId),
   189→            syncCount: targets.length,
   190→            timeoutMs,
   191→            pendingMs,
   192→          });
   193→        },
   194→      },
   195→    );
   196→  } catch (error) {
   197→    if (ignoreTimeout && isTimelineWriteTimeoutError(error)) {
   198→      log(`${logPrefix} shot_data sync timeout ignored`, {
   199→        shotId: shortNullableId(shotId),
   200→        syncCount: targets.length,
   201→      });
   202→      return;
   203→    }
   204→    throw error;
   205→  }
   206→}

1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     3→import { useRenderLogger } from '@/shared/lib/debug/debugRendering';
     4→import type { Json } from '@/integrations/supabase/types';
     5→import { updateToolSettingsSupabase } from '@/shared/hooks/useToolSettings';
     6→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     7→import { SETTINGS_IDS } from '@/shared/lib/settingsIds';
     8→
     9→// Module-level request deduplication cache for the raw `users.settings` DB fetch.
    10→//
    11→// WHY THIS EXISTS (instead of React Query):
    12→// This hook reads a single key from `users.settings.ui`, but many instances
    13→// mount concurrently for different keys (generationMethods, privacyDefaults,
    14→// theme, etc.). Without deduplication, each mount fires an identical
    15→// `SELECT settings FROM users WHERE id = ?` query.
    16→//
    17→// WHY NOT React Query: The hook's read path has complex per-key logic
    18→// (fallback merging, normalization, auto-backfill of missing DB fields)
    19→// that runs after fetching the raw settings row. Migrating to React Query
    20→// would require either (a) a single shared query that all instances subscribe
    21→// to (losing per-key granularity for loading/error states), or (b) per-key
    22→// queries that each fetch the same row (defeating deduplication). The current
    23→// approach deduplicates at the network layer while keeping per-key logic local.
    24→//
    25→// WRITES already go through useToolSettings' global write queue, so there is
    26→// no write-side duplication -- only this read-side cache is parallel.
    27→interface CachedUserSettingsRow {
    28→  settings?: Json;
    29→}
    30→interface CachedSettingsResult {
    31→  data: CachedUserSettingsRow | null;
    32→  error: unknown;
    33→}
    34→interface SettingsCacheEntry {
    35→  data: CachedSettingsResult | null;
    36→  timestamp: number;
    37→  loading: Promise<CachedSettingsResult> | null;
    38→}
    39→const settingsCache = new Map<string, SettingsCacheEntry>();
    40→const CACHE_DURATION = 30000; // 30 seconds
    41→
    42→interface UISettings {
    43→  paneLocks: {
    44→    shots: boolean;
    45→    tasks: boolean;
    46→    gens: boolean;
    47→  };
    48→  settingsModal: {
    49→    activeTab: string;
    50→  };
    51→  videoTravelWidescreen: {
    52→    enabled: boolean;
    53→  };
    54→  imageDeletion?: {
    55→    skipConfirmation: boolean;
    56→  };
    57→  generationMethods: {
    58→    onComputer: boolean;
    59→    inCloud: boolean;
    60→  };
    61→  aiInputMode: {
    62→    mode: 'voice' | 'text';
    63→  };
    64→  privacyDefaults: {
    65→    resourcesPublic: boolean;
    66→    generationsPublic: boolean;
    67→  };
    68→  theme: {
    69→    darkMode: boolean; // Defaults to true (dark mode) for new users
    70→  };
    71→  productTour?: {
    72→    completed: boolean;
    73→    skipped: boolean;
    74→  };
    75→}
    76→
    77→function toRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
    78→  if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) {
    79→    return value as Record<string, unknown>;
    80→  }
    81→  return undefined;
    82→}
    83→
    84→// Cached settings loader to prevent duplicate database calls  
    85→const loadUserSettingsCached = async (userId: string) => {
    86→  const cacheKey = `user_settings_${userId}`;
    87→  const cached = settingsCache.get(cacheKey);
    88→  
    89→  // Return fresh cached data
    90→  if (cached && (Date.now() - cached.timestamp) < CACHE_DURATION) {
    91→    return cached.data;
    92→  }
    93→  
    94→  // If there's already a loading request, wait for it
    95→  if (cached?.loading) {
    96→    return await cached.loading;
    97→  }
    98→  
    99→  // Make new database call and cache the promise
   100→  const loadingPromise: Promise<CachedSettingsResult> = (async () => {
   101→    try {
   102→      const result = await supabase().from('users')
   103→        .select('settings')
   104→        .eq('id', userId)
   105→        .single();
   106→      const typedResult = result as unknown as CachedSettingsResult;
   107→      settingsCache.set(cacheKey, {
   108→        data: typedResult,
   109→        timestamp: Date.now(),
   110→        loading: null
   111→      });
   112→      return typedResult;
   113→    } catch (error) {
   114→      settingsCache.delete(cacheKey);
   115→      throw error;
   116→    }
   117→  })();
   118→  
   119→  // Cache the loading promise to prevent duplicate requests
   120→  settingsCache.set(cacheKey, {
   121→    data: cached?.data || null,
   122→    timestamp: cached?.timestamp || 0,
   123→    loading: loadingPromise
   124→  });
   125→  
   126→  return await loadingPromise;
   127→};
   128→
   129→/**
   130→ * Hook for user-scoped UI preferences (theme, pane locks, generation methods, etc.).
   131→ *
   132→ * Scope: user-only (stored in `users.settings.ui`).
   133→ * Auto-save: yes, 200ms debounce via the global settings write queue.
   134→ *
   135→ * Use this for preferences that follow the user across all projects/shots.
   136→ * For project/shot-scoped settings, use `useAutoSaveSettings` instead.
   137→ *
   138→ * @see docs/structure_detail/settings_system.md for the full settings hook decision tree
   139→ */
   140→export function useUserUIState<K extends keyof UISettings>(
   141→  key: K,
   142→  fallback: UISettings[K]
   143→) {
   144→  // Normalize logic specific to generationMethods to avoid ambiguous "both true" state.
   145→  // Allowed states:
   146→  // - inCloud: true, onComputer: false
   147→  // - inCloud: false, onComputer: true
   148→  // - inCloud: false, onComputer: false (explicitly disabled, used for warnings)
   149→  // Not allowed:
   150→  // - inCloud: true, onComputer: true → normalize to cloud by default
   151→  const normalizeIfGenerationMethods = useCallback((val: UISettings[K]): UISettings[K] => {
   152→    if ((key as string) !== 'generationMethods') return val;
   153→    if (!val || typeof val !== 'object') return val;
   154→    const valObj = val as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
   155→    const inCloud = Boolean(valObj.inCloud);
   156→    const onComputer = Boolean(valObj.onComputer);
   157→    if (inCloud && onComputer) {
   158→      return { inCloud: true, onComputer: false } as UISettings[K];
   159→    }
   160→    return { inCloud, onComputer } as UISettings[K];
   161→  }, [key]);
   162→
   163→  // Stabilize fallback using a ref so callers can pass inline objects without causing
   164→  // infinite render loops. The load effect reads fallbackRef.current instead of depending
   165→  // on fallback directly, preventing re-runs when the reference changes but values don't.
   166→  const fallbackRef = useRef<UISettings[K]>(fallback);
   167→  fallbackRef.current = fallback;
   168→
   169→  useRenderLogger(`UserUIState:${key}`);
   170→
   171→  const [value, setValue] = useState<UISettings[K]>(fallback);
   172→  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
   173→  const userIdRef = useRef<string>();
   174→  const debounceRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout>();
   175→  // Ref so that `update` can read the latest value without depending on it
   176→  const valueRef = useRef(value);
   177→  valueRef.current = value;
   178→
   179→  // Helper function to save fallback values to database (preserves all existing settings)
   180→  // This automatically backfills existing users with default values when they first load the app
   181→  // IMPORTANT: Only runs when the key is completely missing from database (undefined)
   182→  // Does NOT override explicit user choices (including both options set to false)
   183→  const saveFallbackToDatabase = useCallback(async (userId: string, _currentSettings: Record<string, unknown>) => {
   184→    try {
   185→      const fallbackToSave = normalizeIfGenerationMethods(fallbackRef.current);
   186→
   187→      // Use the global queue to save - it handles merging
   188→      await updateToolSettingsSupabase({
   189→        scope: 'user',
   190→        id: userId,
   191→        toolId: SETTINGS_IDS.USER_UI_STATE,
   192→        patch: { [key]: fallbackToSave },
   193→      }, 'immediate');
   194→
   195→      // Invalidate cache so other components see the backfilled values
   196→      const cacheKey = `user_settings_${userId}`;
   197→      settingsCache.delete(cacheKey);
   198→      setValue(fallbackToSave); // Update local state after successful save
   199→    } catch (error) {
   200→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useUserUIState.saveFallbackToDatabase', showToast: false });
   201→    }
   202→  }, [key, normalizeIfGenerationMethods]);
   203→
   204→  // Load initial value from database
   205→  useEffect(() => {
   206→    const loadUserSettings = async () => {
   207→      try {
   208→        const { data: { user } } = await supabase().auth.getUser();
   209→        if (!user) {
   210→          // Skip loading for unauthenticated users (e.g., on public share pages)
   211→          setIsLoading(false);
   212→          return;
   213→        }
   214→
   215→        userIdRef.current = user.id;
   216→
   217→        const cachedResult = await loadUserSettingsCached(user.id);
   218→        const data = cachedResult?.data ?? null;
   219→        const error = cachedResult?.error ?? null;
   220→
   221→        if (error) {
   222→          normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useUserUIState.loadSettings', showToast: false });
   223→          setIsLoading(false);
   224→          return;
   225→        }
   226→
   227→        const settingsRecord = toRecord(data?.settings);
   228→        const uiSettings = toRecord(settingsRecord?.ui);
   229→        const keyValue = uiSettings?.[key];
   230→        
   231→        if (keyValue !== undefined) {
   232→          // Key exists in database - merge with fallback to backfill any missing fields
   233→          const currentFallback = fallbackRef.current;
   234→          const mergedValue = (typeof currentFallback === 'object' && currentFallback !== null)
   235→            ? ({ ...currentFallback, ...(keyValue as object) } as UISettings[K])
   236→            : (keyValue as UISettings[K]);
   237→
   238→          const normalizedValue = normalizeIfGenerationMethods(mergedValue);
   239→          setValue(normalizedValue);
   240→
   241→          // If any fields from the fallback are missing in the stored value OR
   242→          // normalization changed the value, backfill them in DB
   243→          if (
   244→            (typeof keyValue === 'object' && keyValue !== null &&
   245→            typeof currentFallback === 'object' && currentFallback !== null &&
   246→            Object.keys(currentFallback as object).some((k) => (keyValue as Record<string, unknown>)[k] === undefined)) ||
   247→            JSON.stringify(normalizedValue) !== JSON.stringify(keyValue)
   248→          ) {
   249→            // Use the global queue to save - it handles merging
   250→            updateToolSettingsSupabase({
   251→              scope: 'user',
   252→              id: user.id,
   253→              toolId: SETTINGS_IDS.USER_UI_STATE,
   254→              patch: { [key]: normalizedValue },
   255→            }, 'immediate').then(() => {
   256→              const cacheKey = `user_settings_${user.id}`;
   257→              settingsCache.delete(cacheKey);
   258→            }).catch(e => {
   259→              normalizeAndPresentError(e, { context: 'useUserUIState.backfillFields', showToast: false });
   260→            });
   261→          }
   262→        } else {
   263→          // Key doesn't exist in database - this is an existing user who hasn't set preferences yet
   264→          // Save fallback values to backfill them (only runs when completely empty)
   265→          const normalizedFallback = normalizeIfGenerationMethods(fallbackRef.current);
   266→          setValue(normalizedFallback); // Set normalized fallback immediately for responsive UI
   267→
   268→          // Save to database in background (don't block loading)
   269→          saveFallbackToDatabase(user.id, settingsRecord || {}).catch(error => {
   270→            normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useUserUIState.saveFallback', showToast: false });
   271→          });
   272→        }
   273→
   274→        setIsLoading(false);
   275→      } catch (error) {
   276→        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useUserUIState.loadUserSettings', showToast: false });
   277→        setIsLoading(false);
   278→      }
   279→    };
   280→
   281→    loadUserSettings();
   282→  }, [key, normalizeIfGenerationMethods, saveFallbackToDatabase]);
   283→
   284→  // Debounced update function
   285→  // Uses the global settings write queue (via updateToolSettingsSupabase) to prevent
   286→  // race conditions with other hooks that write to users.settings (e.g., useToolSettings
   287→  // writing user-preferences). The write queue serializes writes and uses an atomic RPC.
   288→  const update = useCallback((patch: Partial<UISettings[K]>) => {
   289→    // Immediately update local state for responsive UI (with normalization)
   290→    const normalizedPatch = normalizeIfGenerationMethods({
   291→      ...(valueRef.current as object),
   292→      ...(patch as object)
   293→    } as UISettings[K]);
   294→    setValue(normalizedPatch);
   295→
   296→    // Clear existing timeout
   297→    if (debounceRef.current) {
   298→      clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
   299→    }
   300→
   301→    // Debounce the database write via the global settings write queue
   302→    debounceRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {
   303→      const userId = userIdRef.current;
   304→      if (!userId) return;
   305→
   306→      try {
   307→        // Use the write queue which handles atomic DB updates and merging.
   308→        // This prevents race conditions with other hooks writing to users.settings.
   309→        await updateToolSettingsSupabase({
   310→          scope: 'user',
   311→          id: userId,
   312→          toolId: SETTINGS_IDS.USER_UI_STATE,
   313→          patch: { [key]: normalizedPatch },
   314→        });
   315→
   316→        // Invalidate local cache so other useUserUIState instances see the update
   317→        const cacheKey = `user_settings_${userId}`;
   318→        settingsCache.delete(cacheKey);
   319→      } catch (error) {
   320→        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useUserUIState.update', showToast: false });
   321→      }
   322→    }, 200);
   323→  }, [key, normalizeIfGenerationMethods]);
   324→
   325→  // Cleanup timeout on unmount
   326→  useEffect(() => {
   327→    return () => {
   328→      if (debounceRef.current) {
   329→        clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
   330→      }
   331→    };
   332→  }, []);
   333→
   334→  return useMemo(() => ({
   335→    value,
   336→    update,
   337→    isLoading
   338→  }), [value, update, isLoading]);
   339→} 
   340→

    16→function findCoveringStructureVideo(

    29→function getVideoThumb(slot: SegmentSlot | undefined): string | null {

    31→  return getDisplayUrl(slot.child.thumbUrl || slot.child.location);
    32→}
    33→
    34→type SegmentChild = Extract<SegmentSlot, { type: 'child' }>['child'];
    35→
    36→function buildAdjacentVideoThumbnails(
    37→  segmentVideo: SegmentChild | null,
    38→  pairSlot: SegmentSlot | undefined,
    39→  segmentSlotLightboxIndex: number,
    40→  slotByIndex: Map<number, SegmentSlot>,
    41→  totalPairs: number,
    42→) {
    43→  if (!segmentVideo) return undefined;
    44→
    45→  const currentThumb = getVideoThumb(pairSlot);
    46→  if (!currentThumb) return undefined;
    47→
    48→  let prevInfo: { thumbUrl: string; pairIndex: number } | undefined;
    49→  for (let i = segmentSlotLightboxIndex - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
    50→    const thumb = getVideoThumb(slotByIndex.get(i));
    51→    if (thumb) {
    52→      prevInfo = { thumbUrl: thumb, pairIndex: i };
    53→      break;
    54→    }
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  let nextInfo: { thumbUrl: string; pairIndex: number } | undefined;
    58→  for (let i = segmentSlotLightboxIndex + 1; i < totalPairs; i += 1) {
    59→    const thumb = getVideoThumb(slotByIndex.get(i));
    60→    if (thumb) {
    61→      nextInfo = { thumbUrl: thumb, pairIndex: i };
    62→      break;
    63→    }
    64→  }
    65→
    66→  if (!prevInfo && !nextInfo) return undefined;
    67→
    68→  return {
    69→    prev: prevInfo,
    70→    current: { thumbUrl: currentThumb, pairIndex: segmentSlotLightboxIndex },
    71→    next: nextInfo,
    72→  };
    73→}
    74→
    75→function resolveOverlappingStructureVideos(
    76→  structureVideos: NonNullable<UseSegmentSlotModeResolvedProps['structureVideos']>,
    77→  newVideo: NonNullable<UseSegmentSlotModeResolvedProps['structureVideos']>[number],
    78→) {
    79→  const newStart = newVideo.start_frame ?? 0;
    80→  const newEnd = newVideo.end_frame ?? Infinity;
    81→

    88→      if (existingStart >= newStart && existingEnd <= newEnd) return [];
    89→      if (existingStart < newStart && existingEnd > newEnd) {

    94→      return [existing];
    95→    })
    96→    .filter((video) => (video.end_frame ?? Infinity) > (video.start_frame ?? 0));
    97→}

   142→  };
   143→}

   163→export function useSegmentSlotPresentationAdapter({

1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';

     8→/**
     9→ * Status states for the auto-save settings lifecycle.
    10→ *
    11→ * State machine:
    12→ *   idle → loading → ready ⇄ saving
    13→ *                  ↘ error ↗
    14→ *   Entity change: any → idle (flush pending first via useDebouncedSettingsSave cleanup)
    15→ */
    16→type AutoSaveStatus = 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'saving' | 'error';
    17→
    18→/**
    19→ * Custom load/save functions for non-React-Query persistence.

    22→interface CustomLoadSave<T> {

    24→  load: (entityId: string) => Promise<T | null>;
    25→  /** Save data for the given entity. */
    26→  save: (entityId: string, data: T) => Promise<void>;
    27→  /** The entity ID to load/save for. When this changes, state is reset and reloaded. */
    28→  entityId: string | null;

    30→  onFlush?: (entityId: string, data: T) => void;
    31→}

    40→  shotId?: string | null;

    87→  /** Update multiple fields at once */
    88→  updateFields: (updates: Partial<T>) => void;
    89→  /** Manual save - flushes debounce immediately */
    90→  save: () => Promise<void>;
    91→  /** Force an immediate save (bypasses debounce) */
    92→  saveImmediate: (dataToSave?: T) => Promise<void>;

   100→   * Only available in customLoadSave mode; no-op otherwise.
   101→   */
   102→  initializeFrom: (data: Partial<T>) => void;
   103→}
   104→
   105→// ============================================================================
   106→// Load sub-hooks (extracted for readability)
   107→// ============================================================================
   108→
   109→/** Custom mode load: imperatively fetches settings via customLoadSave.load(). */
   110→function useCustomModeLoad<T extends object>(ctx: {
   111→  isCustomMode: boolean;
   112→  entityId: string | null;
   113→  enabled: boolean;
   114→  statusRef: React.MutableRefObject<AutoSaveStatus>;
   115→  defaults: T;
   116→  debounceMs: number;
   117→  debouncedSave: ReturnType<typeof useDebouncedSettingsSave<T>>;
   118→  customLoadRef: React.MutableRefObject<((entityId: string) => Promise<T | null>) | undefined>;
   119→  currentEntityIdRef: React.MutableRefObject<string | null>;
   120→  isLoadingRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
   121→  transitionReadyWithPendingSave: () => void;
   122→  applyLoadedData: (data: T, hadPersistedData: boolean) => void;
   123→  setStatus: (s: AutoSaveStatus) => void;
   124→  setError: (e: Error | null) => void;
   125→}) {
   126→  const {
   127→    isCustomMode, entityId, enabled, statusRef, defaults, debouncedSave,
   128→    customLoadRef, currentEntityIdRef, isLoadingRef,
   129→    transitionReadyWithPendingSave, applyLoadedData, setStatus, setError,
   130→  } = ctx;
   131→
   132→  useEffect(() => {
   133→    if (!isCustomMode) return;
   134→    if (!entityId || !enabled) return;
   135→    // Accept both 'idle' (first mount) and 'loading' (entity change)
   136→    // Uses statusRef to avoid depending on status and re-running on every transition.
   137→    if (statusRef.current !== 'idle' && statusRef.current !== 'loading') return;
   138→
   139→    if (debouncedSave.hasPendingFor(entityId)) {
   140→      transitionReadyWithPendingSave();
   141→      return;
   142→    }
   143→
   144→    setStatus('loading');
   145→    isLoadingRef.current = true;
   146→
   147→    customLoadRef.current!(entityId)
   148→      .then(loaded => {
   149→        if (currentEntityIdRef.current !== entityId) return;
   150→
   151→        if (debouncedSave.hasPendingFor(entityId)) {
   152→          isLoadingRef.current = false;
   153→          transitionReadyWithPendingSave();
   154→          return;
   155→        }
   156→
   157→        isLoadingRef.current = false;
   158→        applyLoadedData(
   159→          loaded ? { ...defaults, ...loaded } : defaults,
   160→          !!loaded
   161→        );
   162→      })
   163→      .catch(err => {
   164→        normalizeAndPresentError(err, { context: 'useAutoSaveSettings.load', showToast: false });
   165→        setStatus('error');
   166→        isLoadingRef.current = false;
   167→        setError(err as Error);
   168→      });
   169→    // statusRef used instead of status to prevent re-running on every status transition.
   170→    // The guard reads statusRef.current at execution time, which is more correct than
   171→    // capturing status at effect creation time.
   172→  }, [isCustomMode, entityId, enabled, defaults, debouncedSave]);
   173→}
   174→
   175→/** React Query mode load: reactively syncs from useToolSettings query data. */
   176→function useReactQueryModeLoad<T extends object>(ctx: {
   177→  isCustomMode: boolean;
   178→  entityId: string | null;
   179→  enabled: boolean;
   180→  statusRef: React.MutableRefObject<AutoSaveStatus>;
   181→  defaults: T;
   182→  toolId: string;
   183→  dbSettings: T | undefined;
   184→  rqIsLoading: boolean;
   185→  debounceMs: number;
   186→  debouncedSave: ReturnType<typeof useDebouncedSettingsSave<T>>;
   187→  loadedSettingsRef: React.MutableRefObject<T | null>;
   188→  transitionReadyWithPendingSave: () => void;
   189→  setSettings: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<T>>;
   190→  setStatus: (s: AutoSaveStatus) => void;
   191→  setError: (e: Error | null) => void;
   192→}) {
   193→  const {
   194→    isCustomMode, entityId, enabled, statusRef, defaults,
   195→    dbSettings, rqIsLoading, debouncedSave, loadedSettingsRef,
   196→    transitionReadyWithPendingSave, setSettings, setStatus, setError,
   197→  } = ctx;
   198→
   199→  useEffect(() => {
   200→    if (isCustomMode) return;
   201→    if (!entityId || !enabled) {
   202→      return;
   203→    }
   204→
   205→    // Use statusRef.current throughout — this lets us remove `status` from deps,
   206→    // preventing the effect from re-running on every status transition.
   207→    const currentStatus = statusRef.current;
   208→
   209→    if (rqIsLoading) {
   210→      if (currentStatus === 'idle') {
   211→        setStatus('loading');
   212→      }
   213→      return;
   214→    }
   215→
   216→    if (currentStatus === 'saving') return;
   217→
   218→    if (debouncedSave.hasPendingFor(entityId)) {
   219→      if (currentStatus !== 'ready') {
   220→        transitionReadyWithPendingSave();
   221→      }
   222→      return;
   223→    }
   224→
   225→    const loadedSettings: T = {
   226→      ...defaults,
   227→      ...(dbSettings || {}),
   228→    };
   229→
   230→    const clonedSettings = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(loadedSettings));
   231→
   232→    if (loadedSettingsRef.current && deepEqual(clonedSettings, loadedSettingsRef.current)) {
   233→      if (currentStatus !== 'ready') {
   234→        setStatus('ready');
   235→      }
   236→      return;
   237→    }
   238→
   239→    setSettings(clonedSettings);
   240→    loadedSettingsRef.current = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(clonedSettings));
   241→    setStatus('ready');
   242→    setError(null);
   243→    // statusRef used instead of status to prevent re-running on every status transition.
   244→    // The effect only needs to react to query data changes (rqIsLoading, dbSettings),
   245→    // entity changes, and enabled/defaults changes — not internal status transitions.
   246→  }, [isCustomMode, entityId, rqIsLoading, dbSettings, defaults, enabled, debouncedSave]);
   247→}
   248→
   249→/**
   250→ * Recommended hook for auto-saving settings to the database.
   251→ *
   252→ * This is the default choice for new features that need persisted settings.
   253→ * Builds on `useToolSettings` (cascade resolution) and adds auto-save, dirty tracking,
   254→ * entity-change handling, and unmount flushing.
   255→ *
   256→ * Features:
   257→ * - Loads settings from DB with scope cascade (defaults -> user -> project -> shot)
   258→ * - Debounced auto-save on field changes (default 300ms)
   259→ * - Flushes pending saves on unmount/navigation
   260→ * - Dirty tracking for unsaved changes indicator
   261→ * - Status machine for loading states
   262→ * - Optional customLoadSave mode for non-React-Query persistence
   263→ *
   264→ * CRITICAL: During loading (status !== 'ready'), updates only affect local UI state.
   265→ * This prevents auto-initialization effects from blocking DB values.
   266→ *
   267→ * @see docs/structure_detail/settings_system.md for the full settings hook decision tree
   268→ *
   269→ * @example
   270→ * ```typescript
   271→ * // React Query mode (tool settings)
   272→ * const settings = useAutoSaveSettings({
   273→ *   toolId: 'my-tool',
   274→ *   shotId: selectedShotId,
   275→ *   scope: 'shot',
   276→ *   defaults: { prompt: '', mode: 'basic' },
   277→ * });
   278→ *
   279→ * // Custom load/save mode
   280→ * const settings = useAutoSaveSettings({
   281→ *   defaults: { prompt: '', mode: 'basic' },
   282→ *   customLoadSave: {
   283→ *     entityId: generationId,
   284→ *     load: (id) => fetchFromDB(id),
   285→ *     save: (id, data) => saveToDB(id, data),
   286→ *   },
   287→ * });
   288→ *
   289→ * // Update a field (auto-saves after debounce)
   290→ * settings.updateField('prompt', 'new prompt');
   291→ *
   292→ * // Check if ready before rendering
   293→ * if (settings.status !== 'ready') return <Loading />;
   294→ * ```
   295→ */
   296→export function useAutoSaveSettings<T extends object>(
   297→  options: UseAutoSaveSettingsOptions<T>
   298→): UseAutoSaveSettingsReturn<T> {
   299→  const {
   300→    toolId = '',
   301→    shotId,
   302→    projectId,
   303→    scope = 'shot',
   304→    debounceMs = 300,
   305→    defaults,
   306→    enabled = true,
   307→    onSaveSuccess,
   308→    onSaveError,
   309→    customLoadSave,
   310→  } = options;
   311→
   312→  const isCustomMode = !!customLoadSave;
   313→
   314→  // Determine the entity ID based on mode
   315→  const entityId = isCustomMode
   316→    ? customLoadSave.entityId
   317→    : (scope === 'shot' ? shotId : projectId) ?? null;
   318→  const isEntityValid = !!entityId;
   319→
   320→  // Local state - single source of truth for UI
   321→  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<T>(defaults);
   322→  const [status, setStatusRaw] = useState<AutoSaveStatus>('idle');
   323→  const [error, setError] = useState<Error | null>(null);
   324→  const [hasPersistedData, setHasPersistedData] = useState(false);
   325→
   326→  // Ref for status — load effects read this instead of depending on `status` directly.
   327→  // This prevents unnecessary effect re-runs on every status transition (idle→loading→ready→saving).
   328→  // Updated both at render time and immediately when setStatus is called, so effects
   329→  // running in the same commit (after entity-change) see the correct value.
   330→  const statusRef = useRef(status);
   331→  statusRef.current = status;
   332→  const setStatus = useCallback((s: AutoSaveStatus) => {
   333→    setStatusRaw(s);
   334→    statusRef.current = s;
   335→  }, []);
   336→
   337→  useRenderLogger(`AutoSaveSettings:${toolId}`, { entityId, status });
   338→
   339→  // Refs for tracking state without triggering re-renders
   340→  const loadedSettingsRef = useRef<T | null>(null);
   341→  const currentEntityIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
   342→  const isLoadingRef = useRef(false);
   343→
   344→  // Stable refs for custom callbacks to avoid effect dependency churn
   345→  const customLoadRef = useRef(customLoadSave?.load);
   346→  const customSaveRef = useRef(customLoadSave?.save);
   347→  const onFlushRef = useRef(customLoadSave?.onFlush);
   348→  customLoadRef.current = customLoadSave?.load;
   349→  customSaveRef.current = customLoadSave?.save;
   350→  onFlushRef.current = customLoadSave?.onFlush;
   351→
   352→  // Fetch settings from database (React Query mode only)
   353→  const {
   354→    settings: dbSettings,
   355→    isLoading: rqIsLoading,
   356→    update: updateSettings,
   357→    hasShotSettings,
   358→  } = useToolSettings<T>(toolId, {
   359→    shotId: scope === 'shot' ? (shotId || undefined) : undefined,
   360→    projectId: projectId || undefined,
   361→    enabled: !isCustomMode && enabled && isEntityValid,
   362→  });
   363→
   364→  // Refs for React Query state — read by entity-change effect to detect cache hits
   365→  // without adding reactive query values to its deps (which would re-run on every refetch).
   366→  const rqIsLoadingRef = useRef(rqIsLoading);
   367→  rqIsLoadingRef.current = rqIsLoading;
   368→  const dbSettingsRef = useRef(dbSettings);
   369→  dbSettingsRef.current = dbSettings;
   370→
   371→  // Dirty flag - has user changed anything since load?
   372→  const isDirty = useMemo(
   373→    () => (loadedSettingsRef.current ? !deepEqual(settings, loadedSettingsRef.current) : false),
   374→    [settings]
   375→  );
   376→
   377→  // Ref for current settings — used by saveImmediate to avoid capturing `settings`
   378→  // in the closure, which would make saveImmediate unstable (recreated on every settings change).
   379→  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
   380→  settingsRef.current = settings;
   381→
   382→  // Save implementation
   383→  // Uses currentEntityIdRef instead of entityId to keep this callback stable across entity changes.
   384→  // This prevents cascading re-renders through the entire settings/context tree on navigation.
   385→  const saveImmediate = useCallback(async (settingsToSave?: T): Promise<void> => {
   386→    const currentEntityId = currentEntityIdRef.current;
   387→    if (!currentEntityId) {
   388→      return;
   389→    }
   390→
   391→    const toSave = settingsToSave ?? settingsRef.current;
   392→
   393→    // Don't save if nothing changed
   394→    if (deepEqual(toSave, loadedSettingsRef.current)) {
   395→      return;
   396→    }
   397→
   398→    setStatus('saving');
   399→
   400→    try {
   401→      if (isCustomMode) {
   402→        await customSaveRef.current!(currentEntityId, toSave);
   403→      } else {
   404→        await updateSettings(scope, toSave);
   405→      }
   406→
   407→      // Update our "clean" reference
   408→      loadedSettingsRef.current = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(toSave));
   409→
   410→      if (isCustomMode) {
   411→        setHasPersistedData(true);
   412→      }
   413→
   414→      // NOTE: Don't clear pendingSettingsRef here - it's now handled in the timeout callback
   415→      // with edit version checking to avoid race conditions when user types fast
   416→
   417→      setStatus('ready');
   418→      setError(null);
   419→
   420→      onSaveSuccess?.();
   421→    } catch (err) {
   422→      normalizeAndPresentError(err, { context: 'useAutoSaveSettings.save', showToast: false });
   423→      setStatus('error');
   424→      setError(err as Error);
   425→      onSaveError?.(err as Error);
   426→      throw err;
   427→    }
   428→  }, [isCustomMode, updateSettings, scope, onSaveSuccess, onSaveError]);
   429→
   430→  // Ref to hold latest saveImmediate to avoid effect dependency churn
   431→  const saveImmediateRef = useRef(saveImmediate);
   432→  saveImmediateRef.current = saveImmediate;
   433→
   434→  // Helper to read the latest settings from React state (via setSettings identity trick)
   435→  const getLatestSettings = useCallback((): Promise<T> => {
   436→    return new Promise<T>((resolve) => {
   437→      setSettings(current => {
   438→        resolve(current);
   439→        return current; // Don't modify, just read
   440→      });
   441→    });
   442→  }, []);
   443→
   444→  // Debounced save sub-hook — manages scheduling, pending tracking, edit versioning, and flush effects
   445→  const debouncedSave = useDebouncedSettingsSave<T>({
   446→    entityId,
   447→    debounceMs,
   448→    status,
   449→    isCustomMode,
   450→    scope,
   451→    toolId,
   452→    projectId,
   453→    customSaveRef,
   454→    onFlushRef,
   455→    saveImmediateRef,
   456→    getLatestSettings,
   457→  });
   458→
   459→  // Update single field
   460→  // Uses currentEntityIdRef instead of entityId to keep this callback stable across entity changes.
   461→  const updateField = useCallback(<K extends keyof T>(key: K, value: T[K]) => {
   462→    debouncedSave.incrementEditVersion();
   463→
   464→    setSettings(prev => {
   465→      const updated = { ...prev, [key]: value };
   466→
   467→      // Always track pending settings - this protects user input from being overwritten by DB load
   468→      debouncedSave.trackPendingUpdate(updated, currentEntityIdRef.current);
   469→
   470→      // Schedule auto-save (no-ops during loading - just keeps pending tracking)
   471→      debouncedSave.scheduleSave(currentEntityIdRef.current);
   472→
   473→      return updated;
   474→    });
   475→  }, [debouncedSave]);
   476→
   477→  // Update multiple fields at once
   478→  // Uses currentEntityIdRef instead of entityId to keep this callback stable across entity changes.
   479→  const updateFields = useCallback((updates: Partial<T>) => {
   480→    debouncedSave.incrementEditVersion();
   481→
   482→    setSettings(prev => {
   483→      const updated = { ...prev, ...updates };
   484→
   485→      // Always track pending settings - this protects user input from being overwritten by DB load
   486→      debouncedSave.trackPendingUpdate(updated, currentEntityIdRef.current);
   487→
   488→      // Schedule auto-save (no-ops during loading - just keeps pending tracking)
   489→      debouncedSave.scheduleSave(currentEntityIdRef.current);
   490→
   491→      return updated;
   492→    });
   493→  }, [debouncedSave]);
   494→
   495→  // Revert to last saved settings
   496→  const revert = useCallback(() => {
   497→    if (loadedSettingsRef.current) {
   498→      setSettings(loadedSettingsRef.current);
   499→      debouncedSave.clearPending();
   500→    }
   501→  }, [debouncedSave]);
   502→
   503→  // Manual save - flushes debounce immediately
   504→  // Uses saveImmediateRef to avoid depending on saveImmediate directly
   505→  const save = useCallback(async () => {
   506→    debouncedSave.cancelPendingSave();
   507→    await saveImmediateRef.current();
   508→  }, [debouncedSave]);
   509→
   510→  // Reset to defaults (or provided settings)
   511→  const reset = useCallback((newDefaults?: T) => {
   512→    const resetTo = newDefaults || defaults;
   513→
   514→    setSettings(resetTo);
   515→    loadedSettingsRef.current = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(resetTo));
   516→    debouncedSave.clearPending();
   517→  }, [defaults, debouncedSave]);
   518→
   519→  // Initialize from external source (e.g., "last used" settings) - custom mode only
   520→  const initializeFrom = useCallback((data: Partial<T>) => {
   521→    if (!isCustomMode) return;
   522→    // Only apply if we don't have persisted data and aren't loading
   523→    if (hasPersistedData || isLoadingRef.current) {
   524→      return;
   525→    }
   526→
   527→    setSettings(prev => ({ ...prev, ...data }));
   528→  }, [isCustomMode, hasPersistedData]);
   529→
   530→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   531→  // Shared load-apply helpers (used by both custom and React Query load effects)
   532→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   533→
   534→  /** Transition to ready and schedule a debounced save for pending edits. */
   535→  const transitionReadyWithPendingSave = () => {
   536→    setStatus('ready');
   537→    debouncedSave.cancelPendingSave();
   538→    const toSave = debouncedSave.pendingSettingsRef.current!;
   539→    debouncedSave.saveTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {
   540→      try {
   541→        await saveImmediateRef.current(toSave);
   542→      } catch (err) {
   543→        normalizeAndPresentError(err, { context: 'useAutoSaveSettings.pendingSave', showToast: false });
   544→      }
   545→    }, debounceMs);
   546→  };
   547→
   548→  /** Apply loaded settings to state and transition to ready. */
   549→  const applyLoadedData = (data: T, hadPersistedData: boolean) => {
   550→    const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data));
   551→    setSettings(cloned);
   552→    loadedSettingsRef.current = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cloned));
   553→    if (isCustomMode) {
   554→      setHasPersistedData(hadPersistedData);
   555→    }
   556→    setStatus('ready');
   557→    setError(null);
   558→  };
   559→
   560→  // Handle entity changes during render (not in an effect).
   561→  // This prevents the "stale frame" where the previous entity's settings flash briefly.
   562→  // React will discard the current render and re-render with the new state before painting.
   563→  // See: https://react.dev/learn/you-might-not-need-an-effect#adjusting-some-state-when-a-prop-changes
   564→  //
   565→  // NOTE: We intentionally do NOT call debouncedSave.clearPending() here.
   566→  // The flush-on-entity-change cleanup in useDebouncedSettingsSave handles flushing
   567→  // pending saves for the old entity and clearing refs — calling clearPending here
   568→  // would null out the refs before the flush effect gets to read them.
   569→  const previousEntityId = currentEntityIdRef.current;
   570→  if (entityId !== previousEntityId) {
   571→    currentEntityIdRef.current = entityId;
   572→
   573→    if (!entityId) {
   574→      // Entity cleared — reset to idle
   575→      setSettings(defaults);
   576→      setStatus('idle');
   577→      setHasPersistedData(false);
   578→      loadedSettingsRef.current = null;
   579→    } else if (previousEntityId) {
   580→      // Navigating from one entity to another
   581→      setHasPersistedData(false);
   582→
   583→      // Fast path: if React Query already has cached data for the new entity,
   584→      // skip the loading transition entirely. This prevents the visible
   585→      // defaults→loading→ready flash when navigating between previously-visited shots.
   586→      // dbSettingsRef/rqIsLoadingRef are updated from useToolSettings earlier in this render,
   587→      // so they already reflect the new entity's cache state.
   588→      if (!isCustomMode && !rqIsLoadingRef.current && dbSettingsRef.current) {
   589→        const loaded = { ...defaults, ...(dbSettingsRef.current as Record<string, unknown>) } as T;
   590→        const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(loaded));
   591→        setSettings(cloned);
   592→        loadedSettingsRef.current = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cloned));
   593→        setStatus('ready');
   594→        setError(null);
   595→      } else {
   596→        // Slow path: data not cached, show loading state
   597→        setSettings(defaults);
   598→        setStatus('loading');
   599→        loadedSettingsRef.current = null;
   600→      }
   601→    }
   602→    // else: entityId went from null to a value (first mount) — load effects handle initial load
   603→  }
   604→
   605→  // Load settings - custom mode (imperative async load)
   606→  useCustomModeLoad({
   607→    isCustomMode,
   608→    entityId,
   609→    enabled,
   610→    statusRef,
   611→    defaults,
   612→    debounceMs,
   613→    debouncedSave,
   614→    customLoadRef,
   615→    currentEntityIdRef,
   616→    isLoadingRef,
   617→    transitionReadyWithPendingSave,
   618→    applyLoadedData,
   619→    setStatus,
   620→    setError,
   621→  });
   622→
   623→  // Load settings - React Query mode (reactive from useToolSettings)
   624→  useReactQueryModeLoad({
   625→    isCustomMode,
   626→    entityId,
   627→    enabled,
   628→    statusRef,
   629→    defaults,
   630→    toolId,
   631→    dbSettings,
   632→    rqIsLoading,
   633→    debounceMs,
   634→    debouncedSave,
   635→    loadedSettingsRef,
   636→    transitionReadyWithPendingSave,
   637→    setSettings,
   638→    setStatus,
   639→    setError,
   640→  });
   641→
   642→  // Memoize return value to prevent object recreation on every render
   643→  // NOTE: entityId uses currentEntityIdRef.current which is a ref value, so it updates
   644→  // without triggering memo recalculation. Consumers should check status === 'ready'
   645→  // alongside entityId to ensure settings are actually loaded for that entity.
   646→  return useMemo(() => ({
   647→    settings,
   648→    status,
   649→    entityId: currentEntityIdRef.current,
   650→    isDirty,
   651→    error,
   652→    hasShotSettings: isCustomMode ? hasPersistedData : hasShotSettings,
   653→    hasPersistedData: isCustomMode ? hasPersistedData : hasShotSettings,
   654→    updateField,
   655→    updateFields,
   656→    save,
   657→    saveImmediate,
   658→    revert,
   659→    reset,
   660→    initializeFrom,
   661→  }), [settings, status, isDirty, error, isCustomMode, hasPersistedData, hasShotSettings, updateField, updateFields, save, saveImmediate, revert, reset, initializeFrom]);
   662→}
   663→
```

1→import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→
     3→// Two-video system: Video A plays once (landing), then hands off to looping Video B.
     4→// Also handles poster preload, autoplay fix, and painterly reveal mask.
     5→export function useHeroVideo(isMobile: boolean) {
     6→  const videoARef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
     7→  const videoBRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
     8→  const [isLoopVideo, setIsLoopVideo] = useState(false);
     9→  const [videoBReady, setVideoBReady] = useState(false);
    10→
    11→  // Poster image loading
    12→  const [posterLoaded, setPosterLoaded] = useState(false);
    13→
    14→  // Painterly video reveal
    15→  const videoRevealRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    16→  const revealTriggeredRef = useRef(false);
    17→
    18→  // Preload poster image separately - only show when fully loaded
    19→  useEffect(() => {
    20→    const posterImg = new Image();
    21→    posterImg.onload = () => setPosterLoaded(true);
    22→    posterImg.src = '/hero-background-poster.jpg';
    23→  }, []);
    24→
    25→  // Video autoplay fix - ensures video plays on all devices
    26→  useEffect(() => {
    27→    const video = videoARef.current;
    28→    if (!video) return;
    29→
    30→    video.muted = true;
    31→
    32→    const attemptPlay = async () => {
    33→      try {
    34→        await video.play();
    35→      } catch {
    36→        const playOnInteraction = async () => {
    37→          try {
    38→            video.muted = true;
    39→            await video.play();
    40→          } catch { /* intentionally ignored */ }
    41→          document.removeEventListener('touchstart', playOnInteraction);
    42→          document.removeEventListener('click', playOnInteraction);
    43→        };
    44→        document.addEventListener('touchstart', playOnInteraction, { once: true, passive: true });
    45→        document.addEventListener('click', playOnInteraction, { once: true });
    46→      }
    47→    };
    48→
    49→    if (video.readyState >= 3) {
    50→      attemptPlay();
    51→    } else {
    52→      video.addEventListener('loadeddata', attemptPlay, { once: true });
    53→    }
    54→  }, []);
    55→
    56→  // Preload Video B once Video A starts loading (desktop only)
    57→  useEffect(() => {
    58→    if (isMobile) return;
    59→
    60→    const videoA = videoARef.current;
    61→    const videoB = videoBRef.current;
    62→    if (!videoA || !videoB) return;
    63→
    64→    const startPreloadingB = () => {
    65→      videoB.load();
    66→    };
    67→
    68→    const handleBCanPlay = () => {
    69→      setVideoBReady(true);
    70→    };
    71→
    72→    videoB.addEventListener('canplaythrough', handleBCanPlay, { once: true });
    73→
    74→    if (videoA.readyState >= 2) {
    75→      startPreloadingB();
    76→    } else {
    77→      videoA.addEventListener('loadeddata', startPreloadingB, { once: true });
    78→    }
    79→
    80→    return () => {
    81→      videoB.removeEventListener('canplaythrough', handleBCanPlay);
    82→    };
    83→  }, [isMobile]);
    84→
    85→  // Painterly video reveal: trigger when poster is ready
    86→  useEffect(() => {
    87→    if (!posterLoaded) return;
    88→    if (revealTriggeredRef.current) return;
    89→    revealTriggeredRef.current = true;
    90→
    91→    const el = videoRevealRef.current;
    92→    if (!el) return;
    93→    el.classList.add('revealing');
    94→  }, [posterLoaded]);
    95→
    96→  // Clean up mask after reveal animation completes
    97→  useEffect(() => {
    98→    const el = videoRevealRef.current;
    99→    if (!el) return;
   100→
   101→    const handleEnd = (e: AnimationEvent) => {
   102→      if (e.animationName !== 'side-reveal') return;
   103→      el.style.maskImage = 'none';
   104→      el.style.webkitMaskImage = 'none';
   105→      el.classList.remove('video-reveal', 'revealing');
   106→    };
   107→
   108→    el.addEventListener('animationend', handleEnd);
   109→    return () => el.removeEventListener('animationend', handleEnd);
   110→  }, []);
   111→
   112→  // Handler for Video A ending - switch to loop video
   113→  const handleVideoAEnded = () => {
   114→    if (!isLoopVideo && videoBReady) {
   115→      const videoB = videoBRef.current;
   116→      if (videoB) {
   117→        videoB.currentTime = 0;
   118→        videoB.play().catch(() => {});
   119→      }
   120→      setIsLoopVideo(true);
   121→    } else if (!isLoopVideo) {
   122→      const checkReady = setInterval(() => {
   123→        const videoB = videoBRef.current;
   124→        if (videoB && videoB.readyState >= 3) {
   125→          clearInterval(checkReady);
   126→          videoB.currentTime = 0;
   127→          videoB.play().catch(() => {});
   128→          setIsLoopVideo(true);
   129→        }
   130→      }, 50);
   131→      setTimeout(() => clearInterval(checkReady), 3000);
   132→    }
   133→  };
   134→
   135→  return {
   136→    videoARef,
   137→    videoBRef,
   138→    isLoopVideo,
   139→    posterLoaded,
   140→    videoRevealRef,
   141→    handleVideoAEnded,
   142→  };
   143→}
   144→
```

### TIMELINE-CLEANUP CLUSTER

#### 1. **Video setup uses setInterval retry loops polling for video readiness**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/pages/Home/hooks/useHeroVideo.ts` (lines 122-131)
- **Current code:**
```typescript
const checkReady = setInterval(() => {
  const videoB = videoBRef.current;
  if (videoB && videoB.readyState >= 3) {
    clearInterval(checkReady);
    videoB.currentTime = 0;
    videoB.play().catch(() => {});
    setIsLoopVideo(true);
  }
}, 50);
setTimeout(() => clearInterval(checkReady), 3000);
```
- **Risk assessment:** **MEDIUM RISK - SAFE QUICK FIX**
  - Could refactor to use a Promise-based approach with `waitForCondition` utility or similar
  - Main risk: must ensure cleanup still fires on timeout
  - Single file, clear logic
  - Callers: **1 call site** (line 122-131)

#### 2. **isOpen=false reset effect manually resets 11 individual pieces**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/pages/Home/components/panes/hooks/usePhilosophyPaneMedia.ts` (lines 177-189, 191-209)
- **Current code:** Two separate `useEffect` blocks that reset on `isOpen` state change:
  - Lines 177-189: Resets `loraPlaying` + 3 loraVideo refs
  - Lines 191-209: Resets 3 travelVideoRefs + calls `resetAll()` which resets multiple autoAdvance properties
  - The "11 pieces" refers to state mutations across these multiple refs and useState calls
- **Risk assessment:** **LOW RISK - MODERATE EFFORT**
  - Could consolidate into single effect or use a custom hook
  - Main risk: resetAll() has external dependencies (useTravelAutoAdvance) that need testing
  - Multiple state mutations should be consolidated into custom hook + context cleanup
  - Callers: Internal use only within this hook

#### 3. **segmentSlots resolution in useSegmentController**

```typescript
const previousEntityId = currentEntityIdRef.current;
if (entityId !== previousEntityId) {
  currentEntityIdRef.current = entityId;  // Ref write
  if (!entityId) {
    setSettings(defaults);  // useState call during render!
    setStatus('idle');
    setHasPersistedData(false);
