    18→  all_shot_associations?: Array<{ shot_id: string; position: number | null }>;
    19→}
    20→
    21→interface UseRepositionVariantSaveProps {
    22→  media: GenerationRow;
    23→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    24→  shotId?: string;
    25→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    26→  imageDimensions: { width: number; height: number } | null;
    27→  transform: ImageTransform;
    28→  hasTransformChanges: boolean;
    29→  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
    30→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    31→  onVariantCreated?: (variantId: string) => void;
    32→  refetchVariants?: () => void;
    33→  resetTransform: () => void;
    34→  /** Function to create the transformed canvas */
    35→  createTransformedCanvas: () => Promise<HTMLCanvasElement>;
    36→  /** Get current cache key for clearing */
    37→  getCacheKey: () => string;
    38→  /** Clear cache for a specific key */
    39→  clearCacheForKey: (key: string) => void;
    40→  /** Mark that next cache should be skipped */
    41→  markSkipNextCache: () => void;
    42→}
    43→
    44→interface UseRepositionVariantSaveReturn {
    45→  isSavingAsVariant: boolean;
    46→  saveAsVariantSuccess: boolean;
    47→  handleSaveAsVariant: () => Promise<void>;
    48→}
    49→
    50→/**
    51→ * Hook for saving transformed images as variants or new generations.
    52→ * Handles thumbnail generation, upload, DB insertion, and cache invalidation.
    53→ */
    54→export function useRepositionVariantSave({
    55→  media,
    56→  selectedProjectId,
    57→  shotId,
    58→  toolTypeOverride,
    59→  imageDimensions,
    60→  transform,
    61→  hasTransformChanges,
    62→  createAsGeneration,
    63→  activeVariantId,
    64→  onVariantCreated,
    65→  refetchVariants,
    66→  resetTransform,
    67→  createTransformedCanvas,
    68→  getCacheKey,
    69→  clearCacheForKey,
    70→  markSkipNextCache,
    71→}: UseRepositionVariantSaveProps): UseRepositionVariantSaveReturn {
    72→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    73→  const [isSavingAsVariant, setIsSavingAsVariant] = useState(false);
    74→  const [saveAsVariantSuccess, setSaveAsVariantSuccess] = useState(false);
    75→
    76→  // Save transformed image as a variant (without AI generation)
    77→  const handleSaveAsVariant = useCallback(async () => {
    78→    if (!selectedProjectId || !imageDimensions) {
    79→      toast.error('Missing project or image dimensions');
    80→      return;
    81→    }
    82→
    83→    if (!hasTransformChanges) {
    84→      toast.error('Please make some changes first');
    85→      return;
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    setIsSavingAsVariant(true);
    89→
    90→    try {
    91→
    92→      const transformedCanvas = await createTransformedCanvas();
    93→
    94→      // Create a new canvas with black background, then draw transformed image on top
    95→      const outputCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    96→      outputCanvas.width = transformedCanvas.width;
    97→      outputCanvas.height = transformedCanvas.height;
    98→      const outputCtx = outputCanvas.getContext('2d');
    99→
   100→      if (outputCtx) {
   101→        // Fill with black first
   102→        outputCtx.fillStyle = '#000000';
   103→        outputCtx.fillRect(0, 0, outputCanvas.width, outputCanvas.height);
   104→        // Draw the transformed image (with transparency) on top
   105→        outputCtx.drawImage(transformedCanvas, 0, 0);
   106→      }
   107→
   108→      // Convert canvas to blob for main image (use outputCanvas which has black background)
   109→      const transformedBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   110→        outputCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
   111→          if (blob) resolve(blob);
   112→          else reject(new Error('Failed to create transformed image blob'));
   113→        }, 'image/png');
   114→      });
   115→
   116→      // Generate thumbnail (max 300px)
   117→      const thumbnailMaxSize = 300;
   118→      const aspectRatio = outputCanvas.width / outputCanvas.height;
   119→      let thumbWidth: number, thumbHeight: number;
   120→
   121→      if (aspectRatio > 1) {
   122→        thumbWidth = Math.min(outputCanvas.width, thumbnailMaxSize);
   123→        thumbHeight = Math.round(thumbWidth / aspectRatio);
   124→      } else {
   125→        thumbHeight = Math.min(outputCanvas.height, thumbnailMaxSize);
   126→        thumbWidth = Math.round(thumbHeight * aspectRatio);
   127→      }
   128→
   129→      const thumbnailCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
   130→      thumbnailCanvas.width = thumbWidth;
   131→      thumbnailCanvas.height = thumbHeight;
   132→      const thumbCtx = thumbnailCanvas.getContext('2d');
   133→
   134→      if (thumbCtx) {
   135→        thumbCtx.drawImage(outputCanvas, 0, 0, thumbWidth, thumbHeight);
   136→      }
   137→
   138→      // Convert thumbnail to blob
   139→      const thumbnailBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   140→        thumbnailCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
   141→          if (blob) resolve(blob);
   142→          else reject(new Error('Failed to create thumbnail blob'));
   143→        }, 'image/jpeg', 0.8);
   144→      });
   145→
   146→      // Upload both images
   147→      const transformedFile = new File(
   148→        [transformedBlob],
   149→        `repositioned_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
   150→        { type: 'image/png' }
   151→      );
   152→      const thumbnailFile = new File(
   153→        [thumbnailBlob],
   154→        `repositioned_thumb_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.jpg`,
   155→        { type: 'image/jpeg' }
   156→      );
   157→
   158→      const [transformedUrl, thumbnailUrl] = await Promise.all([
   159→        uploadImageToStorage(transformedFile),
   160→        uploadImageToStorage(thumbnailFile)
   161→      ]);
   162→
   163→      // Get the actual generation ID
   164→      const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   165→
   166→      if (createAsGeneration) {
   167→        // Create a new generation with based_on pointing to the source
   168→        const generationParams = toJson({
   169→          // Note: transform is baked into the image, we only save it for historical reference
   170→          transform_applied: transform,
   171→          saved_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   172→          tool_type: toolTypeOverride || TOOL_IDS.EDIT_IMAGES,
   173→          repositioned_from: actualGenerationId,
   174→          ...(activeVariantId ? { source_variant_id: activeVariantId } : {}),
   175→        });
   176→
   177→        const { data: insertedGeneration, error: genError } = await supabase().from('generations')
   178→          .insert({
   179→            project_id: selectedProjectId,
   180→            location: transformedUrl,
   181→            thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl,
   182→            type: 'image',
   183→            based_on: actualGenerationId, // Track lineage
   184→            params: generationParams
   185→          })
   186→          .select('id')
   187→          .single();
   188→
   189→        if (genError) {
   190→          console.error('[Reposition] Failed to create generation:', genError);
   191→          throw genError;
   192→        }
   193→
   194→        // Create the original variant
   195→        await supabase().from('generation_variants').insert({
   196→          generation_id: insertedGeneration.id,
   197→          location: transformedUrl,
   198→          thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl,
   199→          is_primary: true,
   200→          variant_type: VARIANT_TYPE.ORIGINAL,
   201→          name: 'Original',
   202→          params: generationParams,
   203→        });
   204→
   205→        // Clear the transform cache for the current variant (we don't want to restore the editing transform)
   206→        const currentCacheKey = getCacheKey();
   207→        if (currentCacheKey) {
   208→          clearCacheForKey(currentCacheKey);
   209→        }
   210→        // Skip caching when the useEffect runs
   211→        markSkipNextCache();
   212→      } else {
   213→        // Create a new variant and make it primary (displayed by default)
   214→        const { data: insertedVariant, error: insertError } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
   215→          .insert({
   216→            generation_id: actualGenerationId ?? media.id,
   217→            location: transformedUrl,
   218→            thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl,
   219→            is_primary: true,
   220→            variant_type: 'repositioned',
   221→            name: 'Repositioned',
   222→            params: toJson({
   223→              // Note: transform is baked into the image, we only save it for historical reference
   224→              transform_applied: transform,
   225→              saved_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   226→              tool_type: toolTypeOverride || TOOL_IDS.EDIT_IMAGES,
   227→              ...(activeVariantId ? { source_variant_id: activeVariantId } : {}),
   228→            })
   229→          })
   230→          .select('id')
   231→          .single();
   232→
   233→        if (insertError) {
   234→          console.error('[Reposition] Failed to create variant:', insertError);
   235→          throw insertError;
   236→        }
   237→
   238→        // Clear the transform cache and skip re-caching on variant switch
   239→        const currentCacheKey = getCacheKey();
   240→        if (currentCacheKey) {
   241→          clearCacheForKey(currentCacheKey);
   242→        }
   243→        if (insertedVariant?.id) {
   244→          clearCacheForKey(insertedVariant.id);
   245→        }
   246→        // Skip caching when the useEffect runs due to variant change
   247→        markSkipNextCache();
   248→
   249→        // Switch to the newly created variant (only for variant mode)
   250→        if (insertedVariant?.id && onVariantCreated) {
   251→          onVariantCreated(insertedVariant.id);
   252→        }
   253→      }
   254→
   255→      setIsSavingAsVariant(false);
   256→      setSaveAsVariantSuccess(true);
   257→
   258→      setTimeout(() => {
   259→        setSaveAsVariantSuccess(false);
   260→        resetTransform();
   261→      }, 1000);
   262→
   263→      // Cache invalidation after UI feedback
   264→      const mediaExt = media as GenerationRow & MediaWithShotFields;
   265→      const effectiveShotId = shotId || mediaExt.shot_id ||
   266→        (mediaExt.all_shot_associations?.[0]?.shot_id);
   267→
   268→      await invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, {
   269→        generationId: actualGenerationId ?? media.id,
   270→        shotId: effectiveShotId,
   271→        reason: 'reposition-variant-created',
   272→        delayMs: 100,
   273→      });
   274→
   275→      if (refetchVariants) {
   276→        refetchVariants();
   277→      }
   278→    } catch (error) {
   279→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useRepositionVariantSave', toastTitle: 'Failed to save as variant' });
   280→    } finally {
   281→      setIsSavingAsVariant(false);
   282→    }
   283→  }, [
   284→    selectedProjectId,
   285→    imageDimensions,
   286→    hasTransformChanges,
   287→    media,
   288→    transform,
   289→    resetTransform,
   290→    createTransformedCanvas,
   291→    onVariantCreated,
   292→    refetchVariants,
   293→    createAsGeneration,
   294→    toolTypeOverride,
   295→    shotId,
   296→    queryClient,
   297→    getCacheKey,
   298→    clearCacheForKey,
   299→    markSkipNextCache,
   300→    activeVariantId,
   301→  ]);
   302→
   303→  return {
   304→    isSavingAsVariant,
   305→    saveAsVariantSuccess,
   306→    handleSaveAsVariant,
   307→  };
   308→}
   309→
```

1→import { useState } from 'react';
     2→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     3→import { transformVideo } from './transforms';
     4→import type { TrainingDataVideo } from './types';
     5→
     6→interface UseTrainingDataUploadProps {
     7→  selectedBatchId: string | null;
     8→  videos: TrainingDataVideo[];
     9→  setVideos: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<TrainingDataVideo[]>>;
    10→  createSegment: (
    11→    trainingDataId: string,
    12→    startTime: number,
    13→    endTime: number,
    14→    description?: string,
    15→  ) => Promise<string>;
    16→  fetchVideos: () => Promise<void>;
    17→  fetchSegments: () => Promise<void>;
    18→}
    19→
    20→export function useTrainingDataUpload({
    21→  selectedBatchId,
    22→  videos,
    23→  setVideos,
    24→  createSegment,
    25→  fetchVideos,
    26→  fetchSegments,
    27→}: UseTrainingDataUploadProps) {
    28→  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
    29→
    30→  /** Get the duration (in seconds) of a video file via a temporary <video> element. */
    31→  const getVideoDuration = (file: File): Promise<number> => {
    32→    return new Promise((resolve) => {
    33→      const video = document.createElement('video');
    34→      video.preload = 'metadata';
    35→      video.onloadedmetadata = () => {
    36→        resolve(video.duration);
    37→        URL.revokeObjectURL(video.src);
    38→      };
    39→      video.onerror = () => {
    40→        console.warn('Could not get video duration for:', file.name);
    41→        resolve(0);
    42→        URL.revokeObjectURL(video.src);
    43→      };
    44→      video.src = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    45→    });
    46→  };
    47→
    48→  /** Upload a single video file to storage + DB. Returns the new video's ID. */
    49→  const uploadVideoFile = async (file: File, userId: string): Promise<string> => {
    50→    const duration = await getVideoDuration(file);
    51→
    52→    const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop();
    53→    const fileName = `${userId}/${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`;
    54→
    55→    const { error: uploadError } = await supabase().storage
    56→      .from('training-data')
    57→      .upload(fileName, file);
    58→
    59→    if (uploadError) {
    60→      console.error('[Upload] Storage upload error:', uploadError);
    61→      throw uploadError;
    62→    }
    63→
    64→    const { data, error } = await supabase().from('training_data')
    65→      .insert({
    66→        user_id: userId,
    67→        batch_id: selectedBatchId!,
    68→        original_filename: file.name,
    69→        storage_location: fileName,
    70→        duration: Math.round(duration),
    71→        metadata: {
    72→          size: file.size,
    73→          type: file.type,
    74→        },
    75→      })
    76→      .select()
    77→      .single();
    78→
    79→    if (error) {
    80→      console.error('[Upload] Database insert error:', error);
    81→      throw error;
    82→    }
    83→
    84→    setVideos(prev => [transformVideo(data), ...prev]);
    85→    return data.id;
    86→  };
    87→
    88→  /** Create a segment for a video, resolving null endTimeMs from the video's duration. */
    89→  const createSegmentForVideo = async (
    90→    videoId: string,
    91→    startTimeMs: number,
    92→    endTimeMs: number | null,
    93→    description: string,
    94→  ) => {
    95→    if (endTimeMs === null) {
    96→      const localVideo = videos.find(v => v.id === videoId);
    97→      if (localVideo?.duration) {
    98→        endTimeMs = Math.round(localVideo.duration * 1000);
    99→      } else {
   100→        const { data, error } = await supabase().from('training_data')
   101→          .select('duration')
   102→          .eq('id', videoId)
   103→          .single();
   104→
   105→        if (!error && data?.duration) {
   106→          endTimeMs = Math.round(data.duration * 1000);
   107→        } else {
   108→          endTimeMs = 10000;
   109→        }
   110→      }
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    await createSegment(videoId, startTimeMs, endTimeMs, description);
   114→  };
   115→
   116→  /** Upload a single video file. Wraps uploadVideoFile with auth + batch checks. */
   117→  const uploadVideo = async (file: File): Promise<string> => {
   118→    setIsUploading(true);
   119→    try {
   120→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase().auth.getUser();
   121→      if (!user) throw new Error('User not authenticated');
   122→
   123→      if (!selectedBatchId) {
   124→        throw new Error('Please select a batch first');
   125→      }
   126→
   127→      return await uploadVideoFile(file, user.id);
   128→    } catch (error) {
   129→      console.error('[Upload] Error uploading video:', error);
   130→      throw error;
   131→    } finally {
   132→      setIsUploading(false);
   133→    }
   134→  };
   135→
   136→  /** Upload multiple videos, optionally creating segments based on each file's split mode. */
   137→  const uploadVideosWithSplitModes = async (videoFiles: Array<{
   138→    file: File;
   139→    splitMode: 'take-all' | 'manual' | 'auto-scene';
   140→    detectedScenes?: number[];
   141→  }>): Promise<void> => {
   142→    setIsUploading(true);
   143→    try {
   144→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase().auth.getUser();
   145→      if (!user) throw new Error('User not authenticated');
   146→
   147→      if (!selectedBatchId) {
   148→        throw new Error('Please select a batch first');
   149→      }
   150→
   151→      for (const videoFile of videoFiles) {
   152→        const videoId = await uploadVideoFile(videoFile.file, user.id);
   153→
   154→        switch (videoFile.splitMode) {
   155→          case 'take-all':
   156→            await createSegmentForVideo(videoId, 0, null, 'Full video segment');
   157→            break;
   158→
   159→          case 'auto-scene':
   160→            if (videoFile.detectedScenes && videoFile.detectedScenes.length > 1) {
   161→              for (let i = 0; i < videoFile.detectedScenes.length - 1; i++) {
   162→                const startTime = videoFile.detectedScenes[i];
   163→                const endTime = videoFile.detectedScenes[i + 1];
   164→                await createSegmentForVideo(
   165→                  videoId,
   166→                  Math.round(startTime * 1000),
   167→                  Math.round(endTime * 1000),
   168→                  `Auto-detected scene ${i + 1}`,
   169→                );
   170→              }
   171→            } else {
   172→              await createSegmentForVideo(videoId, 0, null, 'Auto-scene fallback (full video)');
   173→            }
   174→            break;
   175→
   176→          case 'manual':
   177→            // Don't create any segments - user will do it manually
   178→            break;
   179→        }
   180→      }
   181→
   182→      // Refresh videos to ensure they're properly loaded
   183→      await Promise.all([fetchVideos(), fetchSegments()]);
   184→
   185→      // Give a small delay to ensure DB has fully processed
   186→      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
   187→
   188→      // Refresh one more time to ensure URLs are generated
   189→      await fetchVideos();
   190→    } catch (error) {
   191→      console.error('Error uploading videos:', error);
   192→      throw error;
   193→    } finally {
   194→      setIsUploading(false);
   195→    }
   196→  };
   197→
   198→  return {
   199→    isUploading,
   200→    uploadVideo,
   201→    uploadVideosWithSplitModes,
   202→  };
   203→}
   204→

1→import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     3→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     4→import { settingsQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/settings';
     5→import { SETTINGS_IDS } from '@/shared/lib/settingsIds';
     6→import {
     7→  fetchToolSettingsSupabaseOrThrow,
     8→  type SettingsFetchResult,
     9→} from '@/shared/lib/toolSettingsService';
    10→
    11→// Central list of tool IDs we want to preload. Update when you add more tools.
    12→const PREFETCH_TOOL_IDS = [
    13→  SETTINGS_IDS.IMAGE_GENERATION,
    14→  SETTINGS_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES,
    15→  SETTINGS_IDS.PROJECT_IMAGE_SETTINGS, // Shared settings including reference images
    16→];
    17→const EMPTY_SHOT_IDS: string[] = [];
    18→
    19→/**
    20→ * Fetch tool settings using the shared service contract (for prefetching).
    21→ */
    22→function fetchSettingsForPrefetch(
    23→  toolId: string,
    24→  ctx: { projectId?: string; shotId?: string },
    25→): Promise<SettingsFetchResult> {
    26→  return fetchToolSettingsSupabaseOrThrow(toolId, ctx);
    27→}
    28→
    29→/**
    30→ * Prefetch tool settings for a project (and optionally its shots) so that
    31→ * individual pages can hydrate synchronously from React-Query cache.
    32→ * Now uses direct Supabase calls for better mobile reliability.
    33→ *
    34→ * @param projectId selected project id
    35→ * @param shotIds  array of shot ids belonging to the project (optional)
    36→ */
    37→export function usePrefetchToolSettings(projectId?: string | null, shotIds: string[] = EMPTY_SHOT_IDS) {
    38→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    39→  const shotIdsKey = shotIds.join(',');
    40→  const shotIdsForPrefetch = useMemo(
    41→    () => (shotIdsKey ? shotIdsKey.split(',') : EMPTY_SHOT_IDS),
    42→    [shotIdsKey],
    43→  );
    44→
    45→  useEffect(() => {
    46→    if (!projectId) {
    47→      return;
    48→    }
    49→
    50→    // Prefetch project-level settings for each tool.
    51→    PREFETCH_TOOL_IDS.forEach((toolId) => {
    52→      queryClient.prefetchQuery({
    53→        [REDACTED](toolId, projectId, undefined),
    54→        queryFn: () => fetchSettingsForPrefetch(toolId, { projectId }),
    55→        staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // keep fresh for 5 min (same as useToolSettings)
    56→      }).then(() => {
    57→      }).catch((error) => {
    58→        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'usePrefetchToolSettings', showToast: false });
    59→      });
    60→    });
    61→
    62→    // Prefetch shot-level settings when shot IDs are provided.
    63→    if (shotIdsForPrefetch.length) {
    64→      shotIdsForPrefetch.forEach((shotId) => {
    65→        PREFETCH_TOOL_IDS.forEach((toolId) => {
    66→          queryClient.prefetchQuery({
    67→            [REDACTED](toolId, projectId, shotId),
    68→            queryFn: () => fetchSettingsForPrefetch(toolId, { projectId, shotId }),
    69→            staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
    70→          });
    71→        });
    72→      });
    73→    }
    74→  }, [projectId, shotIdsForPrefetch, queryClient]);
    75→}
    76→

1→import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     2→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     3→import {
     4→  deleteExternalApiKey,
     5→  fetchExternalApiKey,
     6→  saveExternalApiKey,
     7→} from '@/shared/services/externalApiKeys/repository';
     8→import {
     9→  verifyHuggingFaceToken,
    10→  type HuggingFaceTokenVerificationResult,
    11→} from '@/shared/services/externalApiKeys/huggingFaceTokenVerifier';
    12→import type {
    13→  ExternalApiKeyMetadata,
    14→  ExternalService,
    15→  HuggingFaceMetadata,
    16→} from '@/shared/services/externalApiKeys/types';
    17→
    18→/**
    19→ * Hook to manage external API keys for third-party services
    20→ *
    21→ * @internal Used by useHuggingFaceToken - not directly exported.
    22→ * If you need this for another service, add a specific hook like useHuggingFaceToken.
    23→ */
    24→function useExternalApiKey(service: ExternalService) {
    25→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
    26→  const queryKey = ['externalApiKey', service];
    27→
    28→  // Query to fetch the API key
    29→  const {
    30→    data: apiKey,
    31→    isLoading,
    32→    error,
    33→    refetch,
    34→  } = useQuery({
    35→    queryKey,
    36→    queryFn: () => fetchExternalApiKey(service),
    37→    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
    38→  });
    39→
    40→  // Mutation to save/update the API key
    41→  const saveMutation = useMutation({
    42→    mutationFn: ({ keyValue, metadata }: { keyValue: string; metadata?: ExternalApiKeyMetadata }) =>
    43→      saveExternalApiKey(service, keyValue, metadata),
    44→    onSuccess: (savedKey) => {
    45→      queryClient.setQueryData(queryKey, savedKey);
    46→    },
    47→    onError: (error: Error) => {
    48→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useExternalApiKeys', toastTitle: 'Failed to save API key' });
    49→    },
    50→  });
    51→
    52→  // Mutation to delete the API key
    53→  const deleteMutation = useMutation({
    54→    mutationFn: () => deleteExternalApiKey(service),
    55→    onSuccess: () => {
    56→      queryClient.setQueryData(queryKey, null);
    57→    },
    58→    onError: (error: Error) => {
    59→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useExternalApiKeys', toastTitle: 'Failed to delete API key' });
    60→    },
    61→  });
    62→
    63→  return {
    64→    apiKey,
    65→    hasKey: !!apiKey,
    66→    isLoading,
    67→    error,
    68→    refetch,
    69→    save: (keyValue: string, metadata?: ExternalApiKeyMetadata) =>
    70→      saveMutation.mutateAsync({ keyValue, metadata }),
    71→    delete: () => deleteMutation.mutateAsync(),
    72→    isSaving: saveMutation.isPending,
    73→    isDeleting: deleteMutation.isPending,
    74→  };
    75→}
    76→
    77→function toVerifiedMetadata(
    78→  verification: HuggingFaceTokenVerificationResult,
    79→): ExternalApiKeyMetadata {
    80→  return {
    81→    username: verification.username,
    82→    verified: true,
    83→    verifiedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    84→  };
    85→}
    86→
    87→/**
    88→ * Hook specifically for HuggingFace token management
    89→ */
    90→export function useHuggingFaceToken() {
    91→  const {
    92→    apiKey,
    93→    hasKey,
    94→    isLoading,
    95→    error,
    96→    refetch,
    97→    save,
    98→    delete: deleteKey,
    99→    isSaving,
   100→    isDeleting,
   101→  } = useExternalApiKey('huggingface');
   102→
   103→  const metadata = apiKey?.metadata as HuggingFaceMetadata | undefined;
   104→
   105→  const verifyToken = (token: string): Promise<HuggingFaceTokenVerificationResult> => {
   106→    return verifyHuggingFaceToken(token);
   107→  };
   108→
   109→  const saveToken = async (token: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
   110→    const verification = await verifyToken(token);
   111→    if (!verification.valid) {
   112→      return { success: false, error: verification.error };
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    try {
   116→      await save(token, toVerifiedMetadata(verification));
   117→      return { success: true };
   118→    } catch (err) {
   119→      return { success: false, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to save token' };
   120→    }
   121→  };
   122→
   123→  return {
   124→    hasToken: hasKey,
   125→    isLoading,
   126→    error,
   127→    username: metadata?.username,
   128→    isVerified: metadata?.verified,
   129→    verifyToken,
   130→    saveToken,
   131→    deleteToken: deleteKey,
   132→    isSaving,
   133→    isDeleting,
   134→    refetch,
   135→  };
   136→}
   137→

1→/**
     2→ * Loose type for objects that might have generation_id and/or id.
     3→ * Use this when dealing with untyped or loosely-typed media objects.
     4→ */
     5→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     6→
     7→interface MaybeHasGenerationId {
     8→  generation_id?: string | null;
     9→  id?: string | null;
    10→  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
    11→}
    12→
    13→const NON_PRELOADABLE_URL_MARKERS = ['_joined_frame.jpg'] as const;
    14→
    15→/**
    16→ * Get the canonical generation ID from a media item.
    17→ * Handles both direct generations (id) and variants (generation_id).
    18→ * Also checks metadata.generation_id for compatibility with various data shapes.
    19→ *
    20→ * Priority: generation_id > metadata.generation_id > id
    21→ *
    22→ * @example
    23→ * // Instead of: (media as unknown).generation_id || media.id
    24→ * const genId = getGenerationId(media);
    25→ */
    26→export function getGenerationId(
    27→  media: MaybeHasGenerationId | null | undefined
    28→): string | null {
    29→  if (!media) return null;
    30→  const metadataGenerationId = typeof media.metadata?.generation_id === 'string'
    31→    ? media.metadata.generation_id
    32→    : null;
    33→  return media.generation_id || metadataGenerationId || media.id || null;
    34→}
    35→
    36→/**
    37→ * Centralized preload eligibility contract for URL-based media entries.
    38→ */
    39→export function isPreloadableMediaUrl(url: string | null | undefined): boolean {
    40→  if (!url) {
    41→    return false;
    42→  }
    43→
    44→  return !NON_PRELOADABLE_URL_MARKERS.some((marker) => url.includes(marker));
    45→}
    46→
    47→/**
    48→ * Media item with various URL properties (normalized across sources)
    49→ */
    50→interface MediaWithUrls {
    51→  location?: string | null;
    52→  url?: string | null;
    53→  thumbnail_url?: string | null;
    54→  thumbUrl?: string | null;
    55→  imageUrl?: string | null;
    56→}
    57→
    58→export function getMediaUrl(media: MediaWithUrls | null | undefined): string | undefined {
    59→  if (!media) return undefined;
    60→  return media.location || media.url || media.imageUrl || undefined;
    61→}
    62→
    63→export function getThumbnailUrl(media: MediaWithUrls | null | undefined): string | undefined {
    64→  if (!media) return undefined;
    65→  return media.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl || undefined;
    66→}
    67→
    68→/** Transform a variant to a GenerationRow shape for lightbox display. */
    69→export function variantToGenerationRow(
    70→  media: {
    71→    id: string;
    72→    url: string;
    73→    thumbUrl?: string;
    74→    createdAt?: string;
    75→    starred?: boolean;
    76→    metadata?: { prompt?: string; tool_type?: string; variant_type?: string; generation_id?: string | null };
    77→    generation_id?: string | null;
    78→  },
    79→  mediaType: 'video' | 'image',
    80→): GenerationRow {
    81→  const generationId = getGenerationId(media) ?? media.id;
    82→
    83→  return {
    84→    id: media.id,
    85→    generation_id: generationId,
    86→    location: media.url,
    87→    thumbUrl: media.thumbUrl,
    88→    type: mediaType,
    89→    createdAt: media.createdAt,
    90→    params: {
    91→      prompt: media.metadata?.prompt,
    92→      tool_type: media.metadata?.tool_type,
    93→      variant_type: media.metadata?.variant_type,
    94→      variant_id: media.id,
    95→    },
    96→    starred: media.starred ?? false,
    97→  };
    98→}

1→/**
     2→ * Shared Generation Data Transformers
     3→ * 
     4→ * SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH for transforming generation data from database to UI format.
     5→ * 
     6→ * This eliminates the need to manually update transformation logic in multiple places
     7→ * when adding new fields. Instead, update once here and all consumers automatically
     8→ * get the new field.
     9→ * 
    10→ * Architecture Benefits:
    11→ * - DRY: No duplicated transformation logic across hooks/components
    12→ * - Type Safety: Centralized TypeScript types ensure consistency
    13→ * - Maintainability: Add new fields in one place instead of 5+
    14→ * - Testability: Can unit test transformations in isolation
    15→ * 
    16→ * NOTE: upscaled_url has been removed - upscaled versions are now stored as
    17→ * generation_variants with variant_type='upscaled' and become the primary variant,
    18→ * so `location` already contains the best available URL.
    19→ */
    20→
    21→import type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery/types';
    22→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    23→import type { GenerationMetadata } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    24→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
    25→import { stripQueryParameters } from '@/shared/lib/media/mediaUrl';
    26→import { TOOL_IDS, isToolId } from '@/shared/lib/toolIds';
    27→import { ServerError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/errors';
    28→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
    29→import { expandShotData } from '@/shared/lib/shotData';
    30→import { parseGenerationTaskId } from '@/shared/lib/generationTaskIdParser';
    31→import { filterUuidStrings } from '@/shared/lib/uuid';
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Result type for calculateDerivedCounts
    35→ */
    36→export interface DerivedCountsResult {
    37→  /** Count of variants/derivatives per generation */
    38→  derivedCounts: Record<string, number>;
    39→  /** Whether each generation has any unviewed variants (viewed_at IS NULL) */
    40→  hasUnviewedVariants: Record<string, boolean>;
    41→  /** Count of unviewed variants per generation */
    42→  unviewedVariantCounts: Record<string, number>;
    43→  /** True when counts were intentionally returned empty due to a read-path failure. */
    44→  degraded?: boolean;
    45→  /** Machine-readable reason for degraded fallback behavior. */
    46→  errorCode?: 'query_failed';
    47→}
    48→
    49→function createEmptyDerivedCountsResult(): DerivedCountsResult {
    50→  return {
    51→    derivedCounts: {},
    52→    hasUnviewedVariants: {},
    53→    unviewedVariantCounts: {},
    54→  };
    55→}
    56→
    57→/**
    58→ * Calculate derivedCount for generations (how many variants/derivatives exist)
    59→ * Also tracks whether any variants are unviewed (for NEW badge display)
    60→ *
    61→ * Queries both:
    62→ * - generations table (based_on relationships)
    63→ * - generation_variants table (edit variants)
    64→ *
    65→ * @param generationIds - Array of generation IDs to count variants for
    66→ * @returns Object with derivedCounts and hasUnviewedVariants maps
    67→ */
    68→async function calculateDerivedCounts(
    69→  generationIds: string[]
    70→): Promise<DerivedCountsResult> {
    71→  const persistedGenerationIds = filterUuidStrings(generationIds);
    72→  if (persistedGenerationIds.length === 0) {
    73→    return createEmptyDerivedCountsResult();
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  const { derivedCounts, hasUnviewedVariants, unviewedVariantCounts } = createEmptyDerivedCountsResult();
    77→
    78→  // Only count from generation_variants table (actual variants)
    79→  // Note: We intentionally don't count based_on generations here - those are
    80→  // separate images in the gallery, not variants of this image
    81→  const { data: variantCountsData, error: variantCountsError } = await supabase().from('generation_variants')
    82→    .select('generation_id, viewed_at')
    83→    .in('generation_id', persistedGenerationIds);
    84→
    85→  if (variantCountsError) {
    86→    throw new ServerError('Failed to load variant badge counts', {
    87→      context: { generationIdsCount: generationIds.length },
    88→      cause: variantCountsError,
    89→    });
    90→  }
    91→
    92→  if (variantCountsData) {
    93→    for (const item of variantCountsData as Array<{ generation_id: string; viewed_at: string | null }>) {
    94→      const genId = item.generation_id;
    95→      derivedCounts[genId] = (derivedCounts[genId] || 0) + 1;
    96→      if (item.viewed_at === null) {
    97→        hasUnviewedVariants[genId] = true;
    98→        unviewedVariantCounts[genId] = (unviewedVariantCounts[genId] || 0) + 1;
    99→      }
   100→    }
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  return { derivedCounts, hasUnviewedVariants, unviewedVariantCounts };
   104→}
   105→
   106→/**
   107→ * Fail-open variant of calculateDerivedCounts for non-critical UI surfaces.
   108→ * Keeps badge rendering resilient when counts query intermittently fails.
   109→ */
   110→export async function calculateDerivedCountsSafe(
   111→  generationIds: string[]
   112→): Promise<DerivedCountsResult> {
   113→  try {
   114→    return await calculateDerivedCounts(generationIds);
   115→  } catch (error) {
   116→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'generationTransformers.calculateDerivedCountsSafe', showToast: false });
   117→    return {
   118→      ...createEmptyDerivedCountsResult(),
   119→      degraded: true,
   120→      errorCode: 'query_failed',
   121→    };
   122→  }
   123→}
   124→
   125→/**
   126→ * Raw variant record from generation_variants table (before transformation)
   127→ */
   128→export interface RawVariant {
   129→  id: string;
   130→  generation_id: string;
   131→  location: string;
   132→  thumbnail_url?: string | null;
   133→  params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   134→  variant_type?: string | null;
   135→  name?: string | null;
   136→  created_at: string;
   137→  /** Only present for derived-items queries */
   138→  is_primary?: boolean;
   139→  viewed_at?: string | null;
   140→}
   141→
   142→/**
   143→ * Raw generation record from database (before transformation)
   144→ */
   145→export interface RawGeneration {
   146→  id: string;
   147→  location: string;
   148→  thumbnail_url?: string | null;
   149→  type?: string | null;
   150→  created_at: string;
   151→  updated_at?: string | null;
   152→  params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   153→  starred?: boolean | null;
   154→  tasks?: string[] | string | null;
   155→  based_on?: string | null;
   156→  name?: string | null;
   157→  derivedCount?: number; // Number of generations/variants based on this one
   158→  hasUnviewedVariants?: boolean; // Whether any variants have viewed_at === null (for NEW badge)
   159→  unviewedVariantCount?: number; // Count of unviewed variants
   160→  // Parent/child relationship fields
   161→  is_child?: boolean | null;
   162→  parent_generation_id?: string | null;
   163→  child_order?: number | null;
   164→  // JSONB column mapping shot_id -> array of timeline_frames
   165→  // Each generation can appear multiple times in the same shot (different positions)
   166→  // Example: { "shot_id_123": [120, 420, null] } means 3 entries: at frame 120, 420, and one unpositioned
   167→  shot_data?: Record<string, (number | null)[]>;
   168→}
   169→
   170→/**
   171→ * Raw shot_generation record from database (before transformation)
   172→ */
   173→export interface RawShotGeneration {
   174→  id: string;
   175→  shot_id: string;
   176→  generation_id: string;
   177→  timeline_frame: number | null;
   178→  metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   179→  created_at?: string;
   180→  generation?: RawGeneration | RawGeneration[] | null;
   181→  generations?: RawGeneration | RawGeneration[] | null;
   182→}
   183→
   184→/**
   185→ * Options for transformation customization
   186→ */
   187→interface TransformOptions {
   188→  /** Shot image entry ID (from shot_generations table) */
   189→  shotImageEntryId?: string;
   190→  /** Timeline frame position */
   191→  timeline_frame?: number | null;
   192→  /** Additional metadata to merge */
   193→  metadata?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   194→  /** Shot ID for filtering */
   195→  shotId?: string;
   196→  /** Whether to include verbose logging */
   197→  verbose?: boolean;
   198→}
   199→
   200→/**
   201→ * Extract prompt from various nested param structures
   202→ */
   203→function extractPrompt(params: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined): string {
   204→  if (!params) return 'No prompt';
   205→
   206→  const originalParams = params.originalParams as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   207→  const orchestratorDetails = originalParams?.orchestrator_details as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   208→  const metadataBlock = params.metadata as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   209→
   210→  return (
   211→    (orchestratorDetails?.prompt as string) ||
   212→    (params.prompt as string) ||
   213→    (metadataBlock?.prompt as string) ||
   214→    'No prompt'
   215→  );
   216→}
   217→
   218→/**
   219→ * Extract thumbnail URL with fallback logic
   220→ * Handles special case for travel-between-images videos where thumbnail might be in params
   221→ */
   222→function extractThumbnailUrl(item: RawGeneration, mainUrl: string): string {
   223→  // Start with database thumbnail_url field
   224→  let thumbnailUrl = item.thumbnail_url;
   225→  const toolType = item.params?.tool_type;
   226→  
   227→  // If no thumbnail in database, check params for travel-between-images videos
   228→  if (!thumbnailUrl && isToolId(toolType) && toolType === TOOL_IDS.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES) {
   229→    const originalParams = item.params?.originalParams as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   230→    const orchestratorDetails = originalParams?.orchestrator_details as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   231→    const fullPayload = item.params?.full_orchestrator_payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   232→    const originalFullPayload = originalParams?.full_orchestrator_payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   233→
   234→    thumbnailUrl =
   235→      (item.params?.thumbnailUrl as string) ||
   236→      (orchestratorDetails?.thumbnail_url as string) ||
   237→      (fullPayload?.thumbnail_url as string) ||
   238→      (originalFullPayload?.thumbnail_url as string);
   239→  }
   240→  
   241→  // Final fallback to main URL
   242→  return thumbnailUrl || mainUrl;
   243→}
   244→
   245→/**
   246→ * Normalize timeline_frame to position (divide by 50)
   247→ */
   248→function normalizePosition(timelineFrame: number | null | undefined): number | null {
   249→  if (timelineFrame === null || timelineFrame === undefined) return null;
   250→  return Math.floor(timelineFrame / 50);
   251→}
   252→
   253→/**
   254→ * Transform a raw generation record from database to UI format
   255→ * 
   256→ * This is the MAIN transformation function used by most hooks/components
   257→ * 
   258→ * @param item - Raw generation from database query
   259→ * @param options - Optional customization (shot context, metadata, etc.)
   260→ * @returns Transformed generation ready for UI display
   261→ */
   262→export function transformGeneration(
   263→  item: RawGeneration,
   264→  options: TransformOptions = {}
   265→): GeneratedImageWithMetadata {
   266→  const mainUrl = item.location;
   267→  const thumbnailUrl = extractThumbnailUrl(item, mainUrl);
   268→  const taskIdParse = parseGenerationTaskId(item.tasks);
   269→  const taskId = taskIdParse.taskId;
   270→  const prompt = extractPrompt(item.params);
   271→  
   272→  // Extract content_type from params for proper download file extensions
   273→  // Stored as 'image' or 'video', convert to MIME type
   274→  const storedContentType = item.params?.content_type;
   275→  const isVideo = item.type?.includes('video') || storedContentType === 'video' || false;
   276→  let contentType: string | undefined;
   277→  if (storedContentType === 'video') {
   278→    // Default to mp4 for videos, can be overridden by URL extension
   279→    contentType = 'video/mp4';
   280→  } else if (storedContentType === 'image') {
   281→    // Default to png for images, can be overridden by URL extension  
   282→    contentType = 'image/png';
   283→  }
   284→  
   285→  // Compute stable URL identities for caching/comparison
   286→  // Supabase URLs have rotating tokens but the file path is stable
   287→  const urlIdentity = stripQueryParameters(mainUrl);
   288→  const thumbUrlIdentity = stripQueryParameters(thumbnailUrl);
   289→
   290→  // Base transformation - fields common to all generations
   291→  const baseItem: GeneratedImageWithMetadata = {
   292→    id: item.id,
   293→    url: mainUrl,
   294→    thumbUrl: thumbnailUrl,
   295→    urlIdentity,
   296→    thumbUrlIdentity,
   297→    prompt,
   298→    metadata: {
   299→      ...(item.params || {}),
   300→      taskId, // Include task ID in metadata for MediaGalleryItem
   301→      taskIdStatus: taskIdParse.status, // Surface parse health to avoid silent shape drift
   302→      based_on: item.based_on, // Include based_on for lineage tracking
   303→      ...(options.metadata || {}), // Merge any additional metadata
   304→    },
   305→    createdAt: item.created_at,
   306→    updatedAt: item.updated_at,
   307→    isVideo,
   308→    contentType, // For proper download file extensions
   309→    starred: item.starred || false,
   310→    based_on: item.based_on, // Top level for easy access
   311→    position: null, // Will be set if shot context provided
   312→    timeline_frame: null, // Will be set if shot context provided
   313→    name: item.name || (item.params?.name as string | undefined) || undefined,
   314→    derivedCount: item.derivedCount || 0, // Number of generations/variants based on this one
   315→    hasUnviewedVariants: item.hasUnviewedVariants || false, // For NEW badge display
   316→    unviewedVariantCount: item.unviewedVariantCount || 0, // Count for tooltip
   317→    // Parent/child relationship fields
   318→    is_child: item.is_child ?? undefined,
   319→    parent_generation_id: item.parent_generation_id ?? undefined,
   320→    child_order: item.child_order ?? undefined,
   321→  };
   322→
   323→  // Normalize JSONB shot_data into a flat association list.
   324→  const shotGenerations = expandShotData(item.shot_data as Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined);
   325→  
   326→  // If shot context is provided via options, use it
   327→  if (options.shotImageEntryId || options.timeline_frame !== undefined) {
   328→    return {
   329→      ...baseItem,
   330→      shotImageEntryId: options.shotImageEntryId,
   331→      timeline_frame: options.timeline_frame ?? null,
   332→      position: normalizePosition(options.timeline_frame),
   333→    };
   334→  }
   335→
   336→  // Otherwise, process shot associations from query data
   337→  if (shotGenerations.length > 0) {
   338→    // Single shot optimization
   339→    if (shotGenerations.length === 1) {
   340→      const singleShot = shotGenerations[0];
   341→      return {
   342→        ...baseItem,
   343→        shot_id: singleShot.shot_id,
   344→        position: normalizePosition(singleShot.timeline_frame),
   345→        timeline_frame: singleShot.timeline_frame,
   346→      };
   347→    }
   348→    
   349→    // Multiple shots: include all associations
   350→    const allAssociations = shotGenerations.map(shotGen => ({
   351→      shot_id: shotGen.shot_id,
   352→      timeline_frame: shotGen.timeline_frame,
   353→      position: normalizePosition(shotGen.timeline_frame),
   354→    }));
   355→    
   356→    // When filtering by specific shot, use that shot as primary
   357→    let primaryShot = shotGenerations[0];
   358→    if (options.shotId) {
   359→      const matchingShot = shotGenerations.find(shotGen => shotGen.shot_id === options.shotId);
   360→      if (matchingShot) {
   361→        primaryShot = matchingShot;
   362→      }
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    return {
   366→      ...baseItem,
   367→      shot_id: primaryShot.shot_id,
   368→      position: normalizePosition(primaryShot.timeline_frame),
   369→      timeline_frame: primaryShot.timeline_frame,
   370→      all_shot_associations: allAssociations,
   371→    };
   372→  }
   373→  
   374→  return baseItem;
   375→}
   376→
   377→/**
   378→ * Transform for Timeline component's specific needs
   379→ * Maps to GenerationRow format expected by Timeline
   380→ */
   381→export function transformForTimeline(
   382→  shotGen: RawShotGeneration
   383→): GenerationRow & { timeline_frame?: number } {
   384→  const gen = shotGen.generation || shotGen.generations;
   385→  const genData = Array.isArray(gen) ? gen[0] : gen;
   386→  
   387→  if (!genData) {
   388→    return {
   389→      // PRIMARY IDs: id = shot_generations.id (unique per entry), generation_id = actual generation
   390→      id: shotGen.id,
   391→      generation_id: shotGen.generation_id,
   392→      // Deprecated (backwards compat)
   393→      shotImageEntryId: shotGen.id,
   394→      timeline_frame: shotGen.timeline_frame ?? undefined,
   395→    };
   396→  }
   397→  
   398→  return {
   399→    // PRIMARY IDs: id = shot_generations.id (unique per entry), generation_id = actual generation
   400→    id: shotGen.id,
   401→    generation_id: shotGen.generation_id,
   402→    // Deprecated (backwards compat)
   403→    shotImageEntryId: shotGen.id,
   404→    imageUrl: genData.location,
   405→    thumbUrl: genData.location,
   406→    location: genData.location,
   407→    type: genData.type ?? undefined,
   408→    createdAt: genData.created_at,
   409→    timeline_frame: shotGen.timeline_frame ?? undefined,
   410→    metadata: (shotGen.metadata as GenerationMetadata | undefined) ?? undefined,
   411→    starred: genData.starred ?? false,
   412→    based_on: genData.based_on ?? undefined,
   413→    derivedCount: genData.derivedCount ?? 0,
   414→  };
   415→}
   416→
   417→/**
   418→ * Detect if a URL points to a video based on file extension.
   419→ * Used for variants which lack a `type` column.
   420→ */
   421→function isVideoUrl(url: string | null | undefined): boolean {
   422→  if (!url) return false;
   423→  const videoExtensions = ['.mp4', '.webm', '.mov', '.avi', '.mkv'];
   424→  return videoExtensions.some(ext => url.toLowerCase().includes(ext));
   425→}
   426→
   427→/**
   428→ * Transform a raw variant record from generation_variants table to UI format.
   429→ *
   430→ * Mirrors `transformGeneration` but for variants, which have a different
   431→ * schema (no `type` column, different metadata shape).
   432→ *
   433→ * @param variant - Raw variant from database query
   434→ * @param options - Optional overrides (e.g. toolType fallback)
   435→ * @returns Transformed variant ready for UI display
   436→ */
   437→export function transformVariant(
   438→  variant: RawVariant,
   439→  options?: { toolType?: string }
   440→): GeneratedImageWithMetadata {
   441→  const isVideo = isVideoUrl(variant.location);
   442→  const variantParams = (variant.params ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   443→  const storedContentType = variantParams.content_type as string | undefined;
   444→
   445→  let contentType: string | undefined;
   446→  if (storedContentType === 'video' || isVideo) {
   447→    contentType = 'video/mp4';
   448→  } else {
   449→    contentType = 'image/png';
   450→  }
   451→
   452→  return {
   453→    id: variant.id,
   454→    url: variant.location,
   455→    thumbUrl: variant.thumbnail_url || variant.location,
   456→    isVideo,
   457→    contentType,
   458→    createdAt: variant.created_at,
   459→    starred: false, // Variants don't have starred flag
   460→    metadata: {
   461→      prompt: variantParams.prompt as string | undefined,
   462→      variant_type: variant.variant_type,
   463→      name: variant.name,
   464→      generation_id: variant.generation_id,
   465→      tool_type: (variantParams.tool_type as string) || options?.toolType,
   466→      created_from: variantParams.created_from as string | undefined,
   467→      source_task_id: variantParams.source_task_id as string | undefined,
   468→      content_type: storedContentType,
   469→    },
   470→    shot_id: undefined,
   471→    position: undefined,
   472→    all_shot_associations: undefined,
   473→  };
   474→}
   475→
```

    41→export const extractSettings = (taskData: TaskData): ExtractedSettings => {

    47→  const guidanceTarget = structureGuidance?.target as string | undefined;

    50→    guidanceTarget === 'uni3c'

   104→    negativePrompts: orchestrator.negative_prompts_expanded as string[] | undefined,

   130→    // Advanced mode settings

   145→    loras: (() => {

   149→        | undefined;

   161→    structureVideos,

   186→      if (Array.isArray(params.input_image_paths_resolved)) {

## Desloppify Queue Investigation Report

I've investigated all 11 items. Here's the breakdown:

### ERROR-HANDLING CLUSTER

**1. useSegmentMutations uses console.error + return null**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/segments/useSegmentMutations.ts`
- **Pattern:** Lines 65, 98, 112, 162, 192, 212, 228, 238, 257, 271, 281 - All follow: `if (error) { console.error(...); return false; }` in catch blocks
- **Issue:** `console.error` calls should be removed; the `normalizeAndPresentError` call in try-catch blocks already handles logging
- **Fix time:** < 2 min, LOW RISK - just delete console.error calls, keep the return false

**2. useRepositionVariantSave uses console.error before throw**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/reposition/useRepositionVariantSave.ts`
- **Pattern:** Lines 190, 234 - `console.error(...)` then `throw genError/insertError`; then line 279 uses `normalizeAndPresentError`
- **Issue:** Redundant console.error since the catch block (line 279) already calls `normalizeAndPresentError`
- **Fix time:** < 2 min, LOW RISK - delete console.error calls (lines 190, 234), keep throws

**3. useTrainingDataUpload uses console.error + throw**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/training-data-helper/hooks/useTrainingDataUpload.ts`
- **Pattern:** Lines 60, 80, 129, 191 - Console.error then throw; line 129 inside try-catch that rethrows
- **Issue:** Same pattern - console.error is redundant when errors are propagated up and handled there
- **Fix time:** < 2 min, LOW RISK - delete console.error calls, keep throws

**4. usePrefetchToolSettings handles project-level prefetch**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/usePrefetchToolSettings.ts`
- **Pattern:** Lines 51-60 - `.then(() => {}).catch(...)` empty then handler with catch that does `normalizeAndPresentError`
- **Issue:** Empty `.then(() => {})` is dead code (line 56)
- **Fix time:** < 1 min, LOW RISK - delete the empty then handler, keep the catch

**5. useHuggingFaceToken.saveToken returns {success, error}**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/services/externalApiKeys/hooks/useHuggingFaceToken.ts`
- **Pattern:** Lines 109-121 - `saveToken` returns `{success: boolean, error?: string}` instead of throwing
- **Issue:** Inconsistent error handling - catches thrown errors and returns them as object members instead of letting them bubble up or using proper error pattern
- **Fix time:** 2-3 min, LOW-MEDIUM RISK - should throw on verification fail (line 112) or on save fail (line 119), or caller expects this shape. Need to check callers.

**6. useRepositionVariantSave uses toast.error for programming errors**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/reposition/useRepositionVariantSave.ts`
- **Pattern:** Lines 79, 84 - `toast.error('Missing project...')` and `toast.error('Please make some changes...')`
- **Issue:** CLAUDE.md rule: "Toasts: **errors only** (no success toasts)" - but these are validation errors not user action errors. Should use early return guards instead of user-facing toasts for precondition failures.
- **Fix time:** 2-3 min, LOW RISK - convert to early guard checks (setState, return) instead of toasts, or silently return

### TYPE-SAFETY CLUSTER

**7. variantToGenerationRow returns params with tool type cast as string**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/media/mediaTypeHelpers.ts`, lines 90-95
- **Pattern:** Returns `params: { prompt?, tool_type?, variant_type?, variant_id: string }` which is assigned to `GenerationRow.params: PersistedGenerationParams`
- **Issue:** Shape mismatch - `PersistedGenerationParams` doesn't have `tool_type` or `variant_type` fields; these are custom extensions not in the type
- **Fix time:** 2-3 min, LOW-MEDIUM RISK - either extend `PersistedGenerationParams` to include these fields or cast as `Record<string, unknown>` to avoid false type safety

**8. transformForTimeline returns shotImageEntryId with hardcoded null fallback**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/generationTransformers.ts`, lines 387-395
- **Pattern:** When `!genData`, returns object with `shotImageEntryId: shotGen.id` (hardcoded), no `null` fallback issue visible
- **Issue:** Actually NOT FOUND - line 393 shows `shotImageEntryId: shotGen.id` (always assigned), line 331 shows `shotImageEntryId: options.shotImageEntryId` (can be undefined). No hardcoded null fallback detected. **FALSE POSITIVE**
- **Fix time:** N/A - Not an issue

**9. extractPrompt uses 'as string' on potentially null**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/generationTransformers.ts`, lines 206-213
- **Pattern:** Lines 206-208 cast to `Record<string, unknown>`, then line 211-213 cast `?.prompt as string` three times
- **Issue:** `as string` cast hides null/undefined risks - if none of the fallbacks match, returns falsy value which then uses `'No prompt'` fallback. The `as string` casts are defensive but hide type errors.
- **Fix time:** 2-3 min, LOW RISK - replace `as string` casts with proper type narrowing (e.g., `typeof x === 'string' ? x : undefined`) or rely on the fallback chain

**10. clearAllEnhancedPrompts awaits each DB update sequentially**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks/useTimelineCore.ts`, lines 581-613
- **Pattern:** Lines 592-603 - `for...of` loop calling `supabase().from('shot_generations').update(...).eq(...).await` inside loop
- **Issue:** Awaiting sequentially in a loop is inefficient - should batch updates or use `Promise.all`
- **Fix time:** 3-4 min, LOW-MEDIUM RISK - convert to `Promise.all()` for parallel DB updates

**11. extractSettings in taskDataService.ts accesses deeply nested object**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/applySettings/taskDataService.ts`, lines 41-192
- **Pattern:** Lines 46-48, 58-62, 97-104, etc. - Multiple levels of `as Record<string, unknown>` followed by property access
- **Issue:** Lines 27-28 cast whole object, then lines 46-48 re-cast nested properties; lots of potential null/undefined exposure
- **Fix time:** 3-5 min, LOW-MEDIUM RISK - create typed helper functions to safely extract nested values or use optional chaining more systematically

---

### SUMMARY

**Quick wins (< 2 min, very low risk):**
- Items 1, 2, 3: Remove console.error calls (redundant)
- Item 4: Remove empty `.then()` handler

**Worth fixing (2-3 min, low risk):**
- Item 5: Clarify error handling (throw vs return object)
- Item 6: Remove toast.error for precondition failures
- Item 7: Fix type mismatch in params object shape
- Item 9: Replace unsafe `as string` casts with proper type narrowing

**Optimization (3-5 min, low-medium risk):**
- Item 10: Batch DB updates with Promise.all
- Item 11: Simplify deeply nested object access patterns

**Not an issue:**
- Item 8: False positive - no actual hardcoded null fallback found
